+"""
+tests/unit/test_discovery_detection_invariants.py
+-------------------------------------------------
+Test di conformità per gli invarianti di Topic Discovery, Topic Detection,
+Provenance e Consistency:
+- Criterio 6: Discovery multi-chunk contabilizza tutti i chunk; failure tracciati; nessun silent fail.
+- Criterio 6: Se 0 chunk hanno successo -> OperationalAnalysisFailedError.
+- Criterio 6: Se successo parziale -> marcato PARTIAL / INCOMPLETE.
+- Criterio 7, 10: chunk_count è rigorosamente int (non tupla) sia in Detection sia in Discovery.
+- Criterio 8, 11, 12: Nessun [:max_topics] silenzioso; Deterministic Support Ranking esplicito.
+- Criterio 9: Normalizzazione label con NFKC, casefold, whitespace e punteggiatura.
+- Criterio 10, 13: Discovery riceve ed inoltra max_tokens operativo.
+- Criterio 12, 14: Detection 0 chunk validi -> solleva OperationalAnalysisFailedError, mai falso ABSENT/UNCERTAIN.
+- Criterio 12, 15: Errori backend fatali (LmStudioUnavailableError, AiModelMismatchError, ecc.) propagano subito.
+- Criterio 13, 17: Fallimento operativo non produce ConsistencyResult.
+- Criterio 14, 18: verify_discovered_topic verifica provenance_document_id == document.document_id.
+- Criterio 15, 19: ConsistencyService.evaluate rifiuta cross-document.
+- Criterio 23, 26, 27: Privacy gating basato solo su metadata data_classification == SYNTHETIC_DEMO; source_name=None non crasha.
+- Criterio 24, 28: Documento senza sezioni analizzabili -> AnalysisNoEvidenceError (non ABSENT).
+- Criterio 30: TopicAnalysisRunMetadata usato correttamente.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from typing import Any
+
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import (
+    BaseLlmClient,
+    LlmCompletionResponse,
+    LmStudioUnavailableError,
+)
+from ai.chunking import aggregate_topic_discovery, normalize_topic_label
+from ai.models import (
+    ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    DiscoveredTopic,
+    MessageEvidenceBundle,
+    TopicDecision,
+    TopicDetectionResult,
+    TopicDiscoveryResult,
+    TopicQuery,
+)
+from importer.models import RawRecord
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+)
+from unified.models import UnifiedMessage
+from validation.models import ValidationResult
+from analysis.consistency import ConsistencyService
+from analysis.models import (
+    AnalysisNoEvidenceError,
+    ConsistencyStatus,
+    DiscoveredTopicRef,
+    DiscoveryChunkOutcome,
+    OperationalAnalysisConfig,
+    OperationalAnalysisFailedError,
+    TopicAnalysisRunMetadata,
+)
+from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService
+
+
+class MockServiceLlmClient(BaseLlmClient):
+    def __init__(self, responses: list[dict[str, Any] | Exception] | None = None) -> None:
+        self.responses = list(responses or [])
+        self.call_count = 0
+        self.last_max_tokens: int | None = None
+        self.model_id = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+
+    def is_available(self) -> bool:
+        return True
+
+    def chat_completion(
+        self,
+        messages: list[dict[str, str]],
+        model_id: str | None = None,
+        max_tokens: int | None = None,
+        **kwargs: Any,
+    ) -> LlmCompletionResponse:
+        self.call_count += 1
+        self.last_max_tokens = max_tokens
+        if not self.responses:
+            data = {"decision": "ABSENT", "evidence_ids": [], "rationale": "Nessuna evidenza."}
+        else:
+            item = self.responses.pop(0)
+            if isinstance(item, Exception):
+                raise item
+            data = item
+        return LlmCompletionResponse(
+            content=json.dumps(data),
+            model=model_id or self.model_id,
+            prompt_tokens=10,
+            completion_tokens=10,
+            total_tokens=20,
+            latency_seconds=0.05,
+        )
+
+
+def _make_doc(
+    doc_id: str = "doc_test_1",
+    num_bundles: int = 3,
+    sections_per_bundle: int = 1,
+    classification: str | None = None,
+    source_name: str | None = "msgstore_db",
+) -> ConversationEvidenceDocument:
+    bundles = []
+    meta = {}
+    if classification:
+        meta["data_classification"] = classification
+    for b_idx in range(num_bundles):
+        text = f"Messaggio {b_idx + 1} con contenuto forense." if sections_per_bundle > 0 else ""
+        raw = RawRecord(
+            source_name=source_name or "unknown",
+            source_path="/test",
+            source_record_id=str(b_idx + 1),
+            record_type="message",
+            raw_fields={"data": text},
+            media_reference=None,
+            metadata={},
+        )
+        val = ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=())
+        ts = NormalizedTimestamp(status=TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT)
+        norm = NormalizedRecord(
+            raw_record=raw,
+            validation_result=val,
+            source_name=source_name or "unknown",
+            source_record_id=str(b_idx + 1),
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+        )
+        m = UnifiedMessage(
+            message_id=f"msg::{b_idx + 1}",
+            source_name=source_name or "unknown",
+            source_record_id=str(b_idx + 1),
+            source_path="/test",
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+            provenance_record=norm,
+        )
+        bundle = MessageEvidenceBundle(message=m)
+        bundles.append(bundle)
+    return ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id=doc_id,
+        bundles=tuple(bundles),
+        source_name=source_name,
+        metadata=meta,
+    )
+
+
+def test_chunk_count_is_strictly_int():
+    """Verifica che chunk_count nei metadata sia rigorosamente int (non tupla) sia in Detection che in Discovery."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=2)
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id], "rationale": "Ok"},
+        {"topics": [{"label": "T1", "short_description": "D1", "evidence_ids": [doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id]}]},
+    ])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    # Detection
+    q = TopicQuery(topic_id="t1", label="Test Label", description="Test Desc")
+    det = service.run_topic_detection(doc, q)
+    assert isinstance(det.metadata["chunk_count"], int)
+    assert not isinstance(det.metadata["chunk_count"], tuple)
+
+    # Discovery
+    disc = service.run_topic_discovery(doc)
+    assert isinstance(disc.metadata["chunk_count"], int)
+    assert not isinstance(disc.metadata["chunk_count"], tuple)
+
+
+def test_discovery_forwards_max_tokens():
+    """Verifica che run_topic_discovery inoltri il max_tokens operativo all'analyzer."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=2)
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        {"topics": [{"label": "T1", "short_description": "D1", "evidence_ids": [doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id]}]},
+    ])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.run_topic_discovery(doc, max_tokens=777)
+    assert client.last_max_tokens == 777
+

+def test_discovery_all_chunks_fail_raises_operational_analysis_failed_error():
+    """Se TUTTI i chunk di Discovery falliscono, solleva OperationalAnalysisFailedError (non topics vuoti)."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=3)
+    # Simula errori di structured output per tutti i chunk
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        ValueError("Invalid json 1"),
+        ValueError("Invalid json 2"),
+    ])
+    cfg = OperationalAnalysisConfig(chunk_character_budget=1000, max_chunk_bundles=1)
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client, config=cfg)
+
+    with pytest.raises(OperationalAnalysisFailedError) as exc_info:
+        service.run_topic_discovery(doc)
+    assert "falliti durante l'inferenza" in str(exc_info.value)
+
+
+def test_discovery_partial_chunk_failure_marked_partial_metadata():
+    """Se alcuni chunk riescono e altri falliscono, il risultato è marcato PARTIAL nei metadata con tutti i chunk contabilizzati."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=2)
+    eid = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        {"topics": [{"label": "Tema Valido", "short_description": "Descrizione", "evidence_ids": [eid]}]},
+        ValueError("Chunk 2 payload invalid"),
+    ])
+    cfg = OperationalAnalysisConfig(chunk_character_budget=1000, max_chunk_bundles=1)
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client, config=cfg)
+
+    res = service.run_topic_discovery(doc)
+    assert res.metadata["status"] == "PARTIAL"
+    assert res.metadata["completed_chunk_count"] == 1
+    assert res.metadata["failed_chunk_count"] == 1
+    assert res.metadata["expected_chunk_count"] == 2
+    assert len(res.topics) >= 1
+
+
+def test_fatal_backend_error_propagates_immediately():
+    """Errori di backend fatali (es. LmStudioUnavailableError) propagano immediatamente senza continuare chunk dopo chunk."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=5)
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        LmStudioUnavailableError("Server is down"),
+    ])
+    cfg = OperationalAnalysisConfig(chunk_character_budget=1000, max_chunk_bundles=1)
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client, config=cfg)
+
+    with pytest.raises(LmStudioUnavailableError):
+        service.run_topic_detection(doc, TopicQuery(topic_id="q", label="L", description="D"))
+    assert client.call_count == 1  # Nessun retry inutile chunk dopo chunk
+
+
+def test_detection_all_chunks_fail_raises_operational_analysis_failed_error():
+    """Se TUTTI i chunk di Detection falliscono per errori recuperabili, solleva OperationalAnalysisFailedError (mai falso ABSENT o UNCERTAIN)."""
+    doc = _make_doc(num_bundles=2)

+        ValueError("Chunk 2 decode error"),

+    cfg = OperationalAnalysisConfig(chunk_character_budget=1000, max_chunk_bundles=1)
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client, config=cfg)
+
+    with pytest.raises(OperationalAnalysisFailedError):
+        service.run_topic_detection(doc, TopicQuery(topic_id="q", label="L", description="D"))

+def test_deterministic_support_ranking_for_max_topics():
+    """Verifica che aggregate_topic_discovery applichi Deterministic Support Ranking esplicito senza silent truncation."""
+    eids = ["msg::1::ORIGINAL_TEXT", "msg::2::ORIGINAL_TEXT", "msg::3::ORIGINAL_TEXT"]
+
+    # 3 chunk
+    chunk1 = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Supportato", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[0],)),
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Minore A", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[0],)),
+        ),
+        provenance_document_id="doc1",
+    )
+    chunk2 = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Supportato", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[1],)),
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Minore B", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[1],)),
+        ),
+        provenance_document_id="doc1",
+    )
+    chunk3 = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Supportato", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[2],)),
+            DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Minore C", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eids[2],)),
+        ),
+        provenance_document_id="doc1",
+    )
+
+    # Con max_topics=2, "Tema Supportato" (support count 3) deve prevalere sui temi minori (support count 1)
+    agg = aggregate_topic_discovery([chunk1, chunk2, chunk3], parent_document_id="doc1", max_topics=2)
+    assert len(agg.topics) == 2
+    assert agg.topics[0].label == "Tema Supportato"
+    # Tutti gli evidence_ids del tema supportato devono essere preservati
+    assert set(agg.topics[0].evidence_ids) == set(eids)
+
+    # I metadata devono registrare la riduzione deterministica
+    assert agg.metadata["raw_topic_count"] == 6
+    assert agg.metadata["consolidated_topic_count"] == 4
+    assert agg.metadata["selected_topic_count"] == 2
+    assert agg.metadata["reduction_strategy"] == "deterministic_support_ranking"
+
+
+def test_normalize_topic_label_nfkc_casefold_punctuation():
+    """Verifica che la normalizzazione topic rimuova variazioni superficiali di punteggiatura e unicode."""
+    assert normalize_topic_label("Contratti  e  Accordi...") == normalize_topic_label("contratti e accordi")
+    assert normalize_topic_label("Forniture & Merci!") == normalize_topic_label("forniture  merci")

+def test_document_with_no_analyzable_evidence_raises_error():

+    with pytest.raises(AnalysisNoEvidenceError):
+        service.run_topic_discovery(doc)

+def test_discovery_provenance_validation_in_service():
+    """verify_discovered_topic rifiuta topic con document_id non corrispondente."""
+    doc_a = _make_doc(doc_id="doc_a")
+    doc_b = _make_doc(doc_id="doc_b")
+
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    disc_ref_b = DiscoveredTopicRef(
+        document_id="doc_b",
+        discovery_run_id="run_1",
+        topic_index=0,
+        topic=DiscoveredTopic(label="Topic B", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=("msg::1::ORIGINAL_TEXT",)),
+    )
+
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Discrepanza di provenienza"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(doc_a, disc_ref_b)

+def test_consistency_service_rejects_cross_document():
+    """ConsistencyService.evaluate rifiuta risultati appartenenti a documenti diversi."""
+    doc_a = _make_doc(doc_id="doc_a")
+    eid = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    det_b = TopicDetectionResult(
+        topic=TopicQuery(topic_id="q", label="L", description="D"),
+        decision=TopicDecision.PRESENT,
+        evidence_ids=(eid,),
+        rationale="Ok",
+        provenance_document_id="doc_b",  # Diverso da doc_a!
+    )
+    disc_a = DiscoveredTopic(label="L", short_description="D", evidence_ids=(eid,))
+
+    evaluator = ConsistencyService()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="provenance"):
+        evaluator.evaluate(
+            document_id="doc_a",
+            detection_result=det_b,
+            discovered_topic=disc_a,
+        )
+

+def test_synthetic_data_classification_gating():
+    """raw_response viene salvata SOLO se data_classification == SYNTHETIC_DEMO; source_name=None non crasha."""
+    # 1. Documento reale (nessuna conservazione automatica)
+    doc_real = _make_doc(doc_id="doc_real", classification=None, source_name=None)
+    eid = doc_real.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    client = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid], "rationale": "Ok"},
+    ])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    res_real = service.run_topic_detection(doc_real, TopicQuery(topic_id="q", label="L", description="D"))
+    assert "raw_response" not in res_real.metadata
+
+    # 2. Documento sintetico esplicito
+    doc_synth = _make_doc(doc_id="doc_synth", classification="SYNTHETIC_DEMO", source_name=None)
+    client2 = MockServiceLlmClient([
+        {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid], "rationale": "Ok"},
+    ])
+    service2 = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client2)
+    res_synth = service2.run_topic_detection(doc_synth, TopicQuery(topic_id="q", label="L", description="D"))
+    assert "raw_response" in res_synth.metadata

+"""
+tests/unit/test_discovery_provenance_hardening.py
+-------------------------------------------------
+Test di unità per la provenance hardening di Topic Discovery (BLOCKER 3).
+
+Verifica:
+1. Run prodotta da Discovery operativa: accettata e certificata autoritativamente.
+2. Run sintetica (SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE): respinta dalla registrazione live e dalla verifica.
+3. Run costruita esternamente con origin=LIVE_LM_STUDIO: respinta da register_discovery_run.
+4. Run mock: registrata tramite interfaccia esplicita register_mock_discovery_run() e identificata come MOCK_INSPECTION.
+5. Run non registrata: respinta da verify_discovered_topic().
+6. Run di documento estraneo: respinta da verify_discovered_topic().
+7. Topic alterato (label, short_description o evidence_ids modificati): respinto.
+8. Evidenze estranee non presenti nel documento: respinte.
+9. Nuova inferenza Detection effettivamente indipendente (nuova chiamata LLM con client/modello).
+10. Nessun passaggio degli evidence ID Discovery nel prompt inviato alla Detection.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from typing import Any
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import BaseLlmClient, LlmCompletionResponse
+from ai.models import (
+    ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    DiscoveredTopic,
+    MessageEvidenceBundle,
+    TopicDiscoveryResult,
+)
+from importer.models import RawRecord
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+)
+from unified.models import UnifiedMessage
+from validation.models import ValidationResult
+
+from analysis.models import (
+    AnalysisOrigin,
+    ConsistencyStatus,
+    TopicProvenanceError,
+)
+from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService
+
+
+class MockTestLlmClient(BaseLlmClient):
+    """Client mock controllato per test di provenance e indipendenza."""
+
+    def __init__(self, responses: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> None:
+        self.responses = list(responses or [])
+        self.call_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+        self.model_id = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+
+    def is_available(self) -> bool:
+        return True
+
+    def chat_completion(
+        self,
+        messages: list[dict[str, str]],
+        model_id: str | None = None,
+        max_tokens: int | None = None,
+        **kwargs: Any,
+    ) -> LlmCompletionResponse:
+        self.call_history.append({
+            "messages": messages,
+            "model_id": model_id,
+            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
+            "kwargs": kwargs,
+        })
+        if self.responses:
+            data = self.responses.pop(0)
+        else:
+            data = {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [], "rationale": "Mock rationale."}
+        return LlmCompletionResponse(
+            content=json.dumps(data),
+            model=model_id or self.model_id,
+            prompt_tokens=20,
+            completion_tokens=20,
+            total_tokens=40,
+            latency_seconds=0.01,
+        )
+
+
+def _build_doc(doc_id: str, count: int = 2) -> ConversationEvidenceDocument:
+    bundles = []
+    for i in range(1, count + 1):
+        text = f"Messaggio forense {i} relativo al trasporto."
+        raw = RawRecord(
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_path="/test/chat.txt",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            record_type="message",
+            raw_fields={"text": text},
+            media_reference=None,
+            metadata={},
+        )
+        val = ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=())
+        ts = NormalizedTimestamp(status=TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT)
+        norm = NormalizedRecord(
+            raw_record=raw,
+            validation_result=val,
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+        )
+        um = UnifiedMessage(
+            message_id=f"msg::{doc_id}::{i}",
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            source_path="/test/chat.txt",
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+            provenance_record=norm,
+        )
+        bundles.append(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=um))
+
+    return ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id=doc_id,
+        bundles=tuple(bundles),
+        source_name="whatsapp",
+        metadata={"data_classification": "SYNTHETIC_DEMO"},
+    )
+
+
+class TestDiscoveryProvenanceHardening:
+    """Verifiche Blocker 3: Discovery Provenance e protezione da registrazione arbitraria."""
+
+    def test_run_produced_by_operational_discovery_is_accepted(self):
+        """1. Run prodotta dall'operazione autoritativa run_topic_discovery() è accettata e verificabile."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_live_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        mock_client = MockTestLlmClient([
+            # Chiamata 1: discovery
+            {"topics": [{"label": "Viaggio", "short_description": "Discussione viaggio", "evidence_ids": [eid1]}]},
+            # Chiamata 2: detection (prova del nove)
+            {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid1], "rationale": "Confermato"},
+        ])
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+
+        disc_res = svc.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+        run_id = disc_res.metadata["run_id"]
+
+        # La run è autoritativamente registrata internamente
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run("doc_live_01", run_id) is disc_res
+
+        # Verifica con Topic Detection (Prova del Nove)
+        det_res, cons_res = svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=disc_res.topics[0],
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=mock_client,
+        )
+        assert cons_res.status == ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT
+        assert len(mock_client.call_history) == 2
+
+    def test_synthetic_reference_rejected_from_live_registration_and_verification(self):
+        """2. Run sintetica (SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE) non può essere registrata come live né verificata."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_synth_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        synth_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema demo", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        synth_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[synth_topic],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_synth_01",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_synth_01", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE.value},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+
+        # Registrazione esplicitamente respinta
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE"):
+            svc.register_discovery_run("doc_synth_01", synth_res)
+
+        # Tentativo di verifica respinto
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non registrata|SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=synth_topic,
+                discovery_result=synth_res,
+            )
+
+    def test_forged_external_run_with_live_origin_rejected(self):
+        """3. Run costruita esternamente con origin LIVE_LM_STUDIO respinta da register_discovery_run."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_forged_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        forged_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Falso", short_description="Falso", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        forged_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[forged_topic],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_forged_01",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_forged_001", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+
+        # Una semplice stringa LIVE_LM_STUDIO non basta: deve provenire dal percorso operativo reale
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non certificata"):
+            svc.register_discovery_run("doc_forged_01", forged_res)
+
+    def test_mock_discovery_run_explicit_test_interface(self):
+        """4. Run mock iniettata via register_mock_discovery_run() è identificata come MOCK_INSPECTION."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_mock_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        mock_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Test Mock", short_description="Desc Mock", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        mock_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[mock_topic],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_mock_01",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_mock_001"},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        registered_id = svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_mock_01", mock_res)
+
+        stored = svc.get_discovery_run("doc_mock_01", registered_id)
+        assert stored is not None
+        assert stored.metadata["origin"] == AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value
+
+    def test_unregistered_run_rejected_by_verification(self):
+        """5. Run non registrata respinta da verify_discovered_topic()."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_unreg_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non registrata"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=topic,
+                discovery_run_id="run_non_esistente_999",
+            )
+
+    def test_foreign_document_run_rejected(self):
+        """6. Run registrata per un documento estraneo respinta se verificata su altro documento."""
+        doc_a = _build_doc("doc_a")
+        doc_b = _build_doc("doc_b")
+        eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        topic_a = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema A", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid_a])
+        res_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[topic_a],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_a_001"},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", res_a)
+
+        # Tentativo di verificare la run di doc_a su doc_b
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc_b,
+                discovered_topic=topic_a,
+                discovery_result=res_a,
+                discovery_run_id="run_a_001",
+            )
+
+    def test_tampered_topic_rejected(self):
+        """7. Topic con label o evidenze alterate rispetto alla run registrata respinto."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_tamp_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        eid2 = doc.all_evidence_sections[1].evidence_id
+        topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Originale", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[topic],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_tamp_01",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_tamp_001"},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_tamp_01", res)
+
+        # Topic alterato: label diversa
+        tampered_label = DiscoveredTopic(label="Modificato", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non appartiene alla run|non trovato"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=tampered_label,
+                discovery_result=res,
+            )
+
+        # Topic alterato: evidence_ids diverse
+        tampered_eids = DiscoveredTopic(label="Originale", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid2])
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Discrepanza nei dati del topic|non corrispondono"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=tampered_eids,
+                discovery_result=res,
+            )
+
+    def test_foreign_evidence_ids_in_topic_rejected(self):
+        """8. Evidenze non appartenenti al documento attivo respinte."""

+        foreign_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+            label="Tema Alieno",
+            short_description="Desc",
+            evidence_ids=["ev_9999_alien"],  # Non presente nel documento!

+            provenance_document_id="doc_valid_01",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_alien_001"},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_valid_01", res)
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="evidence_id estraneo|non appartiene"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=foreign_topic,
+                discovery_result=res,
+            )
+
+    def test_detection_receives_only_label_and_short_description_no_evidence_ids(self):
+        """9 & 10. Indipendenza inferenziale: nessun evidence_id Discovery viene passato al prompt della Detection."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_indep_01")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        mock_client = MockTestLlmClient([
+            # Chiamata 1: Discovery
+            {"topics": [{"label": "Corruzione", "short_description": "Accordi illeciti", "evidence_ids": [eid1]}]},
+            # Chiamata 2: Detection
+            {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid1], "rationale": "Rilevato"},
+        ])
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+        disc_res = svc.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+
+        svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=disc_res.topics[0],
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=mock_client,
+        )
+
+        assert len(mock_client.call_history) == 2
+        # La seconda chiamata è la Detection
+        detection_call = mock_client.call_history[1]
+        all_messages_text = " ".join(m["content"] for m in detection_call["messages"])
+
+        # Il label e la descrizione sono presenti nel prompt
+        assert "Corruzione" in all_messages_text
+        assert "Accordi illeciti" in all_messages_text
+
+    def test_missing_origin_rejected_fail_closed(self):
+        """Fail-Closed: run priva di origin nei metadata respinta da register_discovery_run."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_no_origin_01")

+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="origin mancante"):
+            svc.register_discovery_run("doc_no_origin_01", res)

+    def test_multi_conversation_history_isolation_a_b_a(self):
+        """Isolamento cronologia multi-conversazione: transizione A -> B -> A senza interferenze."""
+        doc_a = _build_doc("doc_conv_A")
+        doc_b = _build_doc("doc_conv_B")
+        eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        eid_b = doc_b.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+
+        # Step 1: Esecuzione A
+        res_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema A", short_description="Desc A", evidence_ids=[eid_a])],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_conv_A",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_A_1"},
+        )
+        run_id_a = svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_conv_A", res_a)
+
+        # Step 2: Esecuzione B
+        res_b = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema B", short_description="Desc B", evidence_ids=[eid_b])],
+            provenance_document_id="doc_conv_B",
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_B_1"},
+        )
+        run_id_b = svc.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_conv_B", res_b)
+
+        # Step 3: Ritorno su A
+        # Verifica che il registro di A sia intatto e separato da B
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run("doc_conv_A", run_id_a) is not None
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run("doc_conv_B", run_id_b) is not None
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run("doc_conv_A", run_id_b) is None
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run("doc_conv_B", run_id_a) is None
+
+    def test_caso_a_substitution_same_run_id_rejected_original_preserved(self):
+        """Caso A: Tentativo di sostituzione con stesso run_id ma topic alterato: respinto, originale intatto."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_caso_a")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+        # 1. Test con run live autoritativa registrata
+        live_run_id = "run_live_a_orig_01"
+        live_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Originale Live", short_description="Desc Orig", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": live_run_id, "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc._authoritative_live_run_ids.add(live_run_id)
+        svc._record_authoritative_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_res)
+
+        # Crea un risultato esterno con stesso document_id e run_id ma topic alterato
+        forged_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Falsificato Live", short_description="Desc Falsificata", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        forged_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[forged_topic],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": live_run_id, "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value},
+        )
+
+        # Tentativo di registrazione respinto sia via register_discovery_run che interno
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc.register_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_res)
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc._record_authoritative_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_res)
+
+        # La run originale deve rimanere immutata nello store
+        stored = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_run_id)
+        assert stored is not None
+        assert stored.topics[0].label == "Tema Originale Live"
+        assert stored.topics[0].short_description == "Desc Orig"
+
+        # 2. Test con run originata da run_topic_discovery (mock)
+        mock_client = MockTestLlmClient([
+            {"topics": [{"label": "Tema Mock Orig", "short_description": "Desc Mock Orig", "evidence_ids": [eid1]}]},
+        ])
+        svc_mock = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+        mock_disc_res = svc_mock.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+        mock_run_id = mock_disc_res.metadata["run_id"]
+
+        forged_mock = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Mock Falso", short_description="Falso", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": mock_run_id},
+        )
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc_mock.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_mock)
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc_mock._record_authoritative_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_mock)
+
+        stored_mock = svc_mock.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_run_id)
+        assert stored_mock is not None
+        assert stored_mock.topics[0].label == "Tema Mock Orig"
+
+    def test_caso_b_external_live_origin_known_run_id_cannot_replace_original(self):
+        """Caso B: Risultato esterno dichiara origine LIVE_LM_STUDIO con run_id noto: non può sostituire l'originale."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_caso_b")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        live_run_id = "run_live_known_001"
+        live_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Live Autoritativo", short_description="Desc Live", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        live_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[live_topic],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={
+                "run_id": live_run_id,
+                "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value,
+                "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            },
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc._authoritative_live_run_ids.add(live_run_id)
+        svc._record_authoritative_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_res)
+
+        # Costruiamo esternamente un risultato contraffatto che dichiara LIVE_LM_STUDIO con lo stesso run_id
+        forged_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Live Contraffatto", short_description="Desc Contraffatta", evidence_ids=[eid1])
+        forged_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[forged_topic],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={
+                "run_id": live_run_id,
+                "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value,
+                "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            },
+        )
+
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc.register_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_res)
+
+        # Verifica che il risultato originale rimanga inalterato
+        stored = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_run_id)
+        assert stored is not None
+        assert stored.topics[0].label == "Tema Live Autoritativo"
+        assert stored.topics[0].short_description == "Desc Live"
+
+        # Tentativo di verifica del topic contraffatto deve essere respinto
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non appartiene alla run"):
+            svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+                document=doc,
+                discovered_topic=forged_topic,
+                discovery_run_id=live_run_id,
+            )
+
+    def test_caso_c_mock_interface_cannot_overwrite_live_or_different_mock(self):
+        """Caso C: Tentativo tramite interfaccia mock di sovrascrivere run live o run mock con contenuti differenti."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_caso_c")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+        # 1. Crea una run live autoritativa
+        live_run_id = "run_live_c_001"
+        live_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Live C", short_description="Desc C", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": live_run_id, "origin": AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value},
+        )
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        svc._authoritative_live_run_ids.add(live_run_id)
+        svc._record_authoritative_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_res)
+
+        # Prova a sovrascrivere la run live esistente tramite interfaccia mock -> DEVE FALLIRE
+        mock_attempt = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Mock Overwrite", short_description="Desc Mock", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": live_run_id},
+        )
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="appartiene a una run live|run live autoritativa"):
+            svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_attempt)
+
+        # Verifica che la run live sia intatta
+        stored_live = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, live_run_id)
+        assert stored_live is not None
+        assert stored_live.topics[0].label == "Tema Live C"
+
+        # 2. Crea una run mock controllata
+        mock_run_id = "run_mock_c_002"
+        mock_orig = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Mock Originale", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": mock_run_id},
+        )
+        svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_orig)
+
+        # Prova a sovrascrivere la run mock esistente con contenuti differenti -> DEVE FALLIRE
+        mock_diff = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Mock Differente", short_description="Altra desc", evidence_ids=[eid1])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": mock_run_id},
+        )
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_diff)
+
+        # Prova con contenuti identici -> IDEMPOTENTE SUCCESS
+        res_same = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_orig)
+        assert res_same == mock_run_id
+        stored_mock = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, mock_run_id)
+        assert stored_mock is not None
+        assert stored_mock.topics[0].label == "Tema Mock Originale"
+
+    def test_caso_d_mock_discovery_separated_from_live_registry_and_usable_for_test(self):
+        """Caso D: Discovery mock non aggiunge run_id alle run live, ma resta utilizzabile nei test espliciti."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_caso_d")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        mock_client = MockTestLlmClient([
+            {"topics": [{"label": "Tema Mock D", "short_description": "Desc D", "evidence_ids": [eid1]}]},
+            {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid1], "rationale": "Mock verif"},
+        ])
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+
+        disc_res = svc.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+        run_id = disc_res.metadata["run_id"]
+
+        # Verifica che il run_id NON sia stato aggiunto a _authoritative_live_run_ids
+        assert run_id not in svc._authoritative_live_run_ids
+        assert disc_res.metadata["origin"] == AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value
+
+        # Verifica che il risultato mock resti utilizzabile per test espliciti (Prova del Nove)
+        det_res, cons_res = svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=disc_res.topics[0],
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=mock_client,
+        )
+        assert cons_res.status == ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT
+
+    def test_caso_e_history_immutability_and_multi_conversation_preservation(self):
+        """Caso E: Esecuzione di due Discovery per lo stesso documento: cronologia, run e risultati preservati (A -> B -> A)."""
+        doc_a = _build_doc("doc_caso_e_A")
+        doc_b = _build_doc("doc_caso_e_B")
+        eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        eid_b = doc_b.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+
+        # Due Discovery per documento A
+        res_a1 = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema A1", short_description="Desc A1", evidence_ids=[eid_a])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc_a.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_a_1"},
+        )
+        res_a2 = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema A2", short_description="Desc A2", evidence_ids=[eid_a])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc_a.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_a_2"},
+        )
+        id_a1 = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, res_a1)
+        id_a2 = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, res_a2)
+
+        # Discovery per documento B
+        res_b = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=[DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema B1", short_description="Desc B1", evidence_ids=[eid_b])],
+            provenance_document_id=doc_b.document_id,
+            metadata={"run_id": "run_b_1"},
+        )
+        id_b = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc_b.document_id, res_b)
+
+        # Transizione a B verificata
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run(doc_b.document_id, id_b) is not None
+
+        # Ritorno ad A (A -> B -> A): entrambe le run di A devono essere preservate e distinte
+        stored_a1 = svc.get_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, id_a1)
+        stored_a2 = svc.get_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, id_a2)
+        assert stored_a1 is not None
+        assert stored_a2 is not None
+        assert stored_a1.topics[0].label == "Tema A1"
+        assert stored_a2.topics[0].label == "Tema A2"
+        assert id_a1 != id_a2
+
+        # Cross-document isolation: le run di A non devono esistere per B e viceversa
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run(doc_b.document_id, id_a1) is None
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run(doc_b.document_id, id_a2) is None
+        assert svc.get_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, id_b) is None
+
+    def test_caso_f_independent_detection_two_distinct_calls_no_evidence_hints(self):
+        """Caso F: Detection indipendente: due chiamate distinte, prompt Detection privo di evidence ID discovery."""
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_caso_f")
+        eid1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+        mock_client = MockTestLlmClient([
+            # Chiamata 1: discovery
+            {"topics": [{"label": "Tema Investigativo F", "short_description": "Dettaglio investigativo", "evidence_ids": [eid1]}]},
+            # Chiamata 2: detection
+            {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [eid1], "rationale": "Rilevato in detection"},
+        ])
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+
+        disc_res = svc.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+        assert len(mock_client.call_history) == 1
+
+        selected_topic = disc_res.topics[0]
+        det_res, cons_res = svc.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=selected_topic,
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=mock_client,
+        )
+
+        # Verifica due chiamate distinte al backend LLM
+        assert len(mock_client.call_history) == 2
+
+        # Analisi della seconda chiamata (Detection)
+        detection_call = mock_client.call_history[1]
+        detection_messages = detection_call["messages"]
+        all_messages_text = " ".join(m["content"] for m in detection_messages)
+        system_message_content = next(
+            m["content"] for m in detection_messages if m["role"] == "system"
+        )
+
+        # Il prompt della Detection riceve label e description del topic selezionato
+        assert "Tema Investigativo F" in all_messages_text
+        assert "Dettaglio investigativo" in all_messages_text
+
+        # Verifica che le istruzioni/prompt della Detection non contengano gli evidence ID come suggerimento
+        assert "topic_evidence_ids" not in all_messages_text
+        assert "evidence_hint" not in all_messages_text
+        assert eid1 not in system_message_content
+        assert cons_res.status == ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT
+
+
+class TestDiscoveryMetadataIdentity:
+    """
+    Test suite per l'identità integrale dei metadata in TopicDiscoveryResult.
+    Verifica che l'idempotenza sia concessa SOLO quando il risultato è totalmente identico,
+    e che qualsiasi discrepanza (anche in un singolo campo di metadata o parametro)
+    sollevi TopicProvenanceError preservando incondizionatamente il risultato originale.
+    """
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _create_full_discovery_result(
+        doc_id: str = "doc_meta_test",
+        run_id: str = "run_meta_001",
+        label: str = "Tema Completo",
+        description: str = "Descrizione Completa",
+        evidence_ids: tuple[str, ...] = ("msg::doc_meta_test::1::ORIGINAL_TEXT",),
+        origin: str = AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value,
+        model: str = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        status: str = "COMPLETE",
+        timestamp: str = "2026-10-11T08:00:00Z",
+        context_length: int = 8192,
+        latency: float = 1.25,
+        chunk_count: int = 1,
+        completed_chunk_count: int = 1,
+        failed_chunk_count: int = 0,
+        strategy: str = "DIRECT_MULTILINGUAL",
+        prompt_version: str = "topic_discovery_v1",
+        temperature: float = 0.0,
+        seed: int = 42,
+        max_tokens: int = 512,
+        extra_meta: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> TopicDiscoveryResult:
+        meta: dict[str, Any] = {
+            "run_id": run_id,
+            "origin": origin,
+            "model": model,
+            "status": status,
+            "timestamp": timestamp,
+            "context_length": context_length,
+            "latency_seconds": latency,
+            "chunk_count": chunk_count,
+            "completed_chunk_count": completed_chunk_count,
+            "failed_chunk_count": failed_chunk_count,
+            "strategy": strategy,
+            "prompt_version": prompt_version,
+            "temperature": temperature,
+            "seed": seed,
+            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
+        }
+        if extra_meta:
+            meta.update(extra_meta)
+
+        topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+            label=label,
+            short_description=description,
+            evidence_ids=evidence_ids,
+        )
+        return TopicDiscoveryResult(
+            topics=(topic,),
+            provenance_document_id=doc_id,
+            metadata=meta,
+        )
+
+    def test_duplicate_registration_totally_identical_allowed(self):
+        """1. Registrazione duplicata totalmente identica: accettata come operazione idempotente."""
+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc = _build_doc("doc_meta_test")
+        res1 = self._create_full_discovery_result()
+        res2 = self._create_full_discovery_result()
+
+        reg_id_1 = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, res1)
+        # Seconda registrazione con risultato totalmente identico: no-op idempotente
+        reg_id_2 = svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, res2)
+        assert reg_id_1 == reg_id_2 == "run_meta_001"
+
+        stored = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, "run_meta_001")
+        assert stored is not None
+        assert stored.metadata["status"] == "COMPLETE"
+        assert stored.metadata["context_length"] == 8192
+        assert stored.metadata["timestamp"] == "2026-10-11T08:00:00Z"
+

+    def test_additional_metadata_key_only_in_one_rejected(self):

+        svc = LocalTopicAnalysisService()

+        orig = self._create_full_discovery_result()
+        svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, orig)
+
+        # Risultato contraffatto con chiave extra
+        forged_extra = self._create_full_discovery_result(extra_meta={"unauthorized_injection": "malicious_value"})
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_extra)
+
+        stored = svc.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, "run_meta_001")
+        assert "unauthorized_injection" not in stored.metadata
+
+        # Caso opposto: il risultato memorizzato ha una chiave extra che manca nel nuovo
+        svc2 = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        orig_with_extra = self._create_full_discovery_result(run_id="run_extra_002", extra_meta={"custom_field": "val"})
+        svc2.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, orig_with_extra)
+
+        forged_lacking = self._create_full_discovery_result(run_id="run_extra_002")  # privo di custom_field
+        with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Immutabilità violata"):
+            svc2.register_mock_discovery_run(doc.document_id, forged_lacking)
+
+        stored2 = svc2.get_discovery_run(doc.document_id, "run_extra_002")
+        assert stored2.metadata.get("custom_field") == "val"
+

+        forged_nested = self._create_full_discovery_result(

+

+        assert stored.metadata["strategy"] == "DIRECT_MULTILINGUAL"
+        assert stored.metadata["prompt_version"] == "topic_discovery_v1"

+"""
+tests/unit/test_discovery_provenance_service.py
+------------------------------------------------
+Test completi di conformità per FASE BLOCKER 2:
+DISCOVERY PROVENANCE E CONSISTENCY
+
+Verifiche obbligatorie:
+1. Discovery A + Document A -> allowed
+2. Discovery A + Document B -> rejected (TopicProvenanceError)
+3. Foreign evidence ID -> rejected (TopicProvenanceError)
+4. Missing discovery run -> rejected (TopicProvenanceError)
+5. Invalid topic reference -> rejected (TopicProvenanceError)
+6. A -> B -> A preserves history
+7. Different conversations remain isolated
+8. Discovery evidence IDs not passed to Detection (solo label + short_description)
+9. Detection generates independent LLM call (nessun riutilizzo fraudolento)
+10. Consistency uses actual results (quattro stati autoritativi: CONSISTENT, PARTIALLY_CONSISTENT, DISCREPANCY_ABSENT, DISCREPANCY_UNCERTAIN)
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from typing import Any
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import BaseLlmClient, LlmCompletionResponse
+from ai.models import (
+    ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    DiscoveredTopic,
+    MessageEvidenceBundle,
+    TopicDecision,
+    TopicDetectionResult,
+    TopicDiscoveryResult,
+    TopicQuery,
+)
+from importer.models import RawRecord
+from multimodal.evidence import EvidenceSourceType, TextEvidenceSection
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+)
+from unified.models import UnifiedMessage
+from validation.models import ValidationResult
+
+from analysis.consistency import ConsistencyService
+from analysis.models import (
+    AnalysisOrigin,
+    ConsistencyResult,
+    ConsistencyStatus,
+    DiscoveredTopicRef,
+    DocumentAnalysisState,
+    OperationalAnalysisFailedError,
+    TopicProvenanceError,
+)
+from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService
+from ui.state import (
+    get_document_analysis_state,
+    load_demo_dataset,
+    set_detection_results,
+    set_discovery_results,
+)
+
+
+class MockProvenanceLlmClient(BaseLlmClient):
+    """Client fittizio per verificare le chiamate LLM indipendenti."""
+    def __init__(self, responses: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> None:
+        self.responses = list(responses or [])
+        self.call_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+        self.model_id = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+
+    def is_available(self) -> bool:
+        return True
+
+    def chat_completion(
+        self,
+        messages: list[dict[str, str]],
+        model_id: str | None = None,
+        max_tokens: int | None = None,
+        **kwargs: Any,
+    ) -> LlmCompletionResponse:
+        self.call_history.append({
+            "messages": messages,
+            "model_id": model_id,
+            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
+            "kwargs": kwargs,
+        })
+        if self.responses:
+            data = self.responses.pop(0)
+        else:
+            data = {"decision": "PRESENT", "evidence_ids": [], "rationale": "Mock default."}
+        return LlmCompletionResponse(
+            content=json.dumps(data),
+            model=model_id or self.model_id,
+            prompt_tokens=25,
+            completion_tokens=25,
+            total_tokens=50,
+            latency_seconds=0.02,
+        )
+
+
+def _build_test_document(doc_id: str, num_messages: int = 3) -> ConversationEvidenceDocument:
+    bundles = []
+    for i in range(1, num_messages + 1):
+        text = f"Messaggio forense {i} relativo al traffico merci."
+        raw = RawRecord(
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_path="/test/chat.txt",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            record_type="message",
+            raw_fields={"text": text},
+            media_reference=None,
+            metadata={},
+        )
+        val = ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=())
+        ts = NormalizedTimestamp(status=TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT)
+        norm = NormalizedRecord(
+            raw_record=raw,
+            validation_result=val,
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+        )
+        um = UnifiedMessage(
+            message_id=f"msg::{doc_id}::{i}",
+            source_name="whatsapp",
+            source_record_id=str(i),
+            source_path="/test/chat.txt",
+            record_type="message",
+            timestamp=ts,
+            message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+            text_content=text,
+            media_reference=None,
+            provenance_record=norm,
+        )
+        bundle = MessageEvidenceBundle(message=um)
+        bundles.append(bundle)
+
+    return ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id=doc_id,
+        bundles=tuple(bundles),
+        source_name="whatsapp",
+        metadata={"data_classification": "SYNTHETIC_DEMO"},
+    )
+
+
+# --- Test 1: Discovery A + Document A -> allowed ---
+
+def test_discovery_a_document_a_allowed():
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Logistica Portuale",
+        short_description="Ispezione container al porto",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+    )
+    disc_result_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_disc_a_100"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [eid_a],
+        "rationale": "Confermato scarico merci.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", disc_result_a)
+
+    det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc_a,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_disc_a_100",
+    )
+
+    assert det_res.decision == TopicDecision.PRESENT
+    assert consistency.status == ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT
+    assert consistency.overlap_evidence_ids == (eid_a,)
+    assert consistency.document_id == "doc_a"
+    assert consistency.discovery_run_id == "run_disc_a_100"
+
+
+# --- Test 2: Discovery A + Document B -> rejected ---
+
+def test_discovery_a_document_b_rejected():
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    doc_b = _build_test_document("doc_b")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_ref_a = DiscoveredTopicRef(
+        document_id="doc_a",
+        discovery_run_id="run_a",
+        topic_index=0,
+        topic=DiscoveredTopic(
+            label="Tema A",
+            short_description="Descrizione A",
+            evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+        ),
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    # Document mismatch via DiscoveredTopicRef -> reject
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Discrepanza di provenienza"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_b,
+            discovered_topic=disc_ref_a,
+        )
+
+    # Document mismatch via TopicDiscoveryResult -> reject
+    disc_result_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_ref_a.topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_a"},
+    )
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Discrepanza di provenienza"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_b,
+            discovered_topic=disc_ref_a.topic,
+            discovery_result=disc_result_a,
+            discovery_run_id="run_a",
+        )
+
+

+def test_foreign_evidence_id_in_detection_result_rejected():
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    valid_eid = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    foreign_eid = "eid::alien_doc::42::ORIGINAL_TEXT"
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Traffico Merci",
+        short_description="Spedizione merci",
+        evidence_ids=(valid_eid,),
+    )
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_disc_valid"},
+    )
+
+    # LLM inventa o restituisce un evidence ID che non appartiene a doc_a
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [foreign_eid],
+        "rationale": "Allucinazione evidence estranea.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", disc_result)
+
+    with pytest.raises((TopicProvenanceError, OperationalAnalysisFailedError)):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_disc_valid",
+        )

+# --- Test 6 & 7: Multi-conversation isolation & history preservation (A -> B -> A) ---
+
+def test_multi_conversation_isolation_and_history_preservation():
+    session_state: dict[str, Any] = {}
+
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("chat_alpha")
+    doc_b = _build_test_document("chat_beta")
+
+    # Inizializza stato per chat_alpha
+    state_a = get_document_analysis_state("chat_alpha", state=session_state)
+    det_a = TopicDetectionResult(
+        topic=TopicQuery(topic_id="q_a", label="Tema Alpha", description="Desc Alpha"),
+        decision=TopicDecision.PRESENT,
+        evidence_ids=(doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id,),
+        rationale="Rilevato in Alpha",
+        provenance_document_id="chat_alpha",
+        metadata={"run_id": "det_alpha_run_1"},
+    )
+    state_a.add_detection_result(det_a)
+
+    # Passa a chat_beta (A -> B)
+    state_b = get_document_analysis_state("chat_beta", state=session_state)
+    det_b = TopicDetectionResult(
+        topic=TopicQuery(topic_id="q_b", label="Tema Beta", description="Desc Beta"),
+        decision=TopicDecision.PRESENT,
+        evidence_ids=(doc_b.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id,),
+        rationale="Rilevato in Beta",
+        provenance_document_id="chat_beta",
+        metadata={"run_id": "det_beta_run_1"},
+    )
+    state_b.add_detection_result(det_b)
+
+    # Verifica isolamento di B
+    assert len(state_b.detection_runs) == 1
+    assert state_b.detection_runs[0].provenance_document_id == "chat_beta"
+    assert state_b.detection_runs[0].topic.label == "Tema Beta"
+
+    # Ritorna a chat_alpha (B -> A)
+    state_a_returned = get_document_analysis_state("chat_alpha", state=session_state)
+    assert len(state_a_returned.detection_runs) == 1
+    assert state_a_returned.detection_runs[0].provenance_document_id == "chat_alpha"
+    assert state_a_returned.detection_runs[0].topic.label == "Tema Alpha"
+    assert state_a_returned.latest_detection_run_id == "det_alpha_run_1"
+
+    # Nessuna contaminazione tra sessioni
+    assert state_a is not state_b
+    assert all(r.provenance_document_id == "chat_alpha" for r in state_a_returned.detection_runs)
+    assert all(r.provenance_document_id == "chat_beta" for r in state_b.detection_runs)
+
+
+# --- Test 8: Discovery evidence IDs not passed to Detection ---
+
+def test_discovery_evidence_ids_not_passed_to_detection():
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_inspect")
+    eid = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Tema Segreto",
+        short_description="Descrizione confidenziale",
+        evidence_ids=(eid,),
+    )
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_inspect",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_inspect_999"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [eid],
+        "rationale": "Indipendente.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_inspect", disc_result)
+
+    service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_inspect_999",
+    )
+
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 1
+    call = client.call_history[0]
+    prompt_str = " ".join(m.get("content", "") for m in call["messages"])
+
+    # La detection riceve label e short_description
+    assert "Tema Segreto" in prompt_str
+    assert "Descrizione confidenziale" in prompt_str
+
+    # La detection NON riceve gli evidence_ids emersi da Discovery come hint
+    assert "evidence_ids emersi da Discovery" not in prompt_str
+    assert "run_inspect_999" not in prompt_str
+
+
+# --- Test 9: Detection generates independent LLM call ---
+
+def test_detection_generates_independent_llm_call():
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_indep")
+    eid = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Tema Verifica",
+        short_description="Descrizione Verifica",
+        evidence_ids=(eid,),
+    )
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_indep",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_indep_001"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "ABSENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [],
+        "rationale": "Nessuna evidenza rilevata.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_indep", disc_result)
+
+    det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_indep_001",
+    )
+
+    # Verifica che una nuova chiamata LLM reale sia stata effettuata
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 1
+    assert det_res.decision == TopicDecision.ABSENT
+    assert consistency.status == ConsistencyStatus.DISCREPANCY_ABSENT
+
+
+# --- Test 10: Consistency uses actual results ---
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "detection_decision, target_eid_idx, expected_status",
+    [
+        (TopicDecision.PRESENT, 0, ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT),
+        (TopicDecision.PRESENT, 1, ConsistencyStatus.PARTIALLY_CONSISTENT),
+        (TopicDecision.ABSENT, None, ConsistencyStatus.DISCREPANCY_ABSENT),
+        (TopicDecision.UNCERTAIN, None, ConsistencyStatus.DISCREPANCY_UNCERTAIN),
+    ],
+)
+def test_consistency_uses_actual_results_matrix(
+    detection_decision: TopicDecision,
+    target_eid_idx: int | None,
+    expected_status: ConsistencyStatus,
+):
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_matrix")
+    disc_eids = (doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id,)
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Matrice Coerenza",
+        short_description="Verifica 4 stati",
+        evidence_ids=disc_eids,
+    )
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_matrix",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_matrix_check"},
+    )
+
+    if target_eid_idx is not None:
+        det_eids = [doc.all_evidence_sections[target_eid_idx].evidence_id]
+    else:
+        det_eids = []
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": detection_decision.value,
+        "evidence_ids": det_eids,
+        "rationale": "Verifica matrice.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_matrix", disc_result)
+
+    det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_matrix_check",
+    )
+
+    assert det_res.decision == detection_decision
+    assert consistency.status == expected_status
+
+
+# --- Test 11: Nonexistent discovery run -> rejected ---
+
+def test_nonexistent_discovery_run_rejected():
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Tema Fantasma",

+        evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+    )
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    # run non registrata solleva TopicProvenanceError prima di chiamare il client LLM
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Discovery run inesistente o non registrata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+            discovery_run_id="unregistered_run_999",
+        )
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 0
+

+# --- Test 12: Foreign document discovery run -> rejected ---

+def test_foreign_document_discovery_run_rejected():

+
+    disc_topic_b = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Tema Doc B",
+        short_description="Descrizione Doc B",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_b,),
+    )
+    disc_result_b = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic_b,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_b",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_b_valid"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    # Registra run solo per doc_b
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_b", disc_result_b)
+
+    # Tentativo di verificare su doc_a usando la run registrata su doc_b
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="Discovery run inesistente o non registrata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=disc_topic_b,
+            discovery_run_id="run_b_valid",
+        )

+# --- Test 13: Foreign topic not in registered run -> rejected ---

+def test_foreign_topic_not_in_registered_run_rejected():

+def test_forged_external_run_cannot_be_implicitly_registered_via_verify():
+    """Una run costruita esternamente e fornita a verify non deve essere registrata implicitamente."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Forged Topic",
+        short_description="Forged Description",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+    )
+    forged_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_forged_external"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    # NOT registering via register_discovery_run!
+
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non registrata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+            discovery_result=forged_result,
+            discovery_run_id="run_forged_external",
+        )
+    # Zero chiamate LLM effettuate
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 0
+    # Verifica che la run forged NON sia stata memorizzata nel registro del service
+    assert service.get_discovery_run("doc_a", "run_forged_external") is None
+
+
+def test_same_label_different_description_rejected():
+    """Un topic con la stessa label ma descrizione differente deve sollevare TopicProvenanceError."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    registered_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Logistica Portuale",
+        short_description="Descrizione ORIGINALE registrata",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+    )
+    tampered_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Logistica Portuale",
+        short_description="Descrizione ALTERATA o FORGIATA",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_a,),
+    )
+
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(registered_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_tampered_desc"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", disc_result)
+
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="descrizione specificata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=tampered_topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_tampered_desc",
+        )
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 0
+
+

+    """Un topic con la stessa label ma evidence ID differenti deve sollevare TopicProvenanceError."""

+    eid_2 = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[1].evidence_id
+

+        short_description="Descrizione valida",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_2,),
+    )
+
+    disc_result = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(registered_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_tampered_eids"},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", disc_result)
+
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="evidence_id specificati"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=tampered_topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_tampered_eids",
+        )
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 0
+

+def test_audit_b3_1_demo_loaded_without_live_discovery():
+    """1. DEMO caricata senza Discovery live: non esistono run live al caricamento demo."""
+    session: dict = {}
+    load_demo_dataset(session)
+    from ui.state import get_discovery_run_store, KEY_DISCOVERY_RUN_STORE_BY_DOCUMENT
+    store = get_discovery_run_store(session)
+    assert len(store) == 0
+    assert session.get(KEY_DISCOVERY_RUN_STORE_BY_DOCUMENT) == {}
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_2_live_runs_start_empty():
+    """2. Le run live iniziano vuote (0 discovery live)."""
+    session: dict = {}
+    load_demo_dataset(session)
+    from ui.state import get_discovery_run_store
+    assert get_discovery_run_store(session) == {}
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_3_synthetic_references_visible_separately():
+    """3. Le reference sintetiche sono visibili separatamente e hanno origin SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE."""
+    session: dict = {}
+    load_demo_dataset(session)
+    from ui.state import get_demo_reference_discovery_results
+    refs = get_demo_reference_discovery_results(session)
+    assert len(refs) > 0
+    first_ref = refs[0]
+    assert first_ref.metadata.get("origin") == AnalysisOrigin.SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE.value
+    assert first_ref.metadata.get("run_id") == "run::discovery::demo_synthetic_run_01"
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_4_synthetic_reference_rejected_from_live_registry():
+    """4. Una reference sintetica NON può essere registrata come run live in AnalysisService."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc::demo_forensic_chat")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    synthetic_ref = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(DiscoveredTopic(label="Tema Demo", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eid_a,)),),
+        provenance_document_id=doc_a.document_id,
+        metadata={
+            "run_id": "run::discovery::demo_synthetic_run_01",
+            "origin": AnalysisOrigin.SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE.value,
+        },
+    )
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="SYNTHETIC_REFERENCE"):
+        service.register_discovery_run(doc_a.document_id, synthetic_ref)
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_5_live_verification_requires_actual_live_run():
+    """5. La verifica live richiede una run effettivamente prodotta dal relativo percorso (rifiuta reference sintetica)."""
+    session: dict = {}
+    load_demo_dataset(session)
+    from ui.state import get_conversation_document, get_demo_reference_discovery_results
+    doc = get_conversation_document(session)
+    assert doc is not None
+    ref_disc = get_demo_reference_discovery_results(session)[0]
+    topic = ref_disc.topics[0]
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    # Tentativo di verificare il topic sintetico direttamente senza discovery live
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="non registrata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=topic,
+            discovery_run_id=ref_disc.metadata["run_id"],
+        )
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 0
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_6_nonexistent_run_rejected():
+    """6. Run inesistente respinta categoricamente con TopicProvenanceError."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Test", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eid_a,))
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="inesistente o non registrata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_phantom_999",
+        )
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_7_foreign_document_run_rejected():
+    """7. Run appartenente ad altro documento respinta con TopicProvenanceError."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    doc_b = _build_test_document("doc_b")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Test", short_description="Desc", evidence_ids=(eid_a,))
+    run_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_a_123", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value},
+    )
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", run_a)
+
+    # Invocazione su doc_b con la run registrata per doc_a
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_b,
+            discovered_topic=topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_a_123",
+        )
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_8_tampered_topic_rejected():
+    """8. Topic alterato respinto (label identica ma descrizione o evidence_id manomessi)."""
+    doc_a = _build_test_document("doc_a")
+    eid_a = doc_a.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+

+    altered_topic = DiscoveredTopic(label="Droga", short_description="ALTERATA", evidence_ids=(eid_a,))
+
+    run_a = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(orig_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_a",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_a_alt", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value},
+    )
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_a", run_a)
+
+    with pytest.raises(TopicProvenanceError, match="descrizione specificata"):
+        service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_a,
+            discovered_topic=altered_topic,
+            discovery_run_id="run_a_alt",
+        )
+

+def test_audit_b3_9_mock_discovery_correctly_identified_as_mock():
+    """9. Discovery prodotta da client mock identificata con origin MOCK_INSPECTION."""
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_mock")
