+
+    mock_client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "topics": [{"label": "Tema Mock", "short_description": "Descrizione mock", "evidence_ids": [eid]}],
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=mock_client)
+    res = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=mock_client)
+
+    assert res.metadata.get("origin") == AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value
+    assert res.metadata.get("status") == "COMPLETE"
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_10_lmstudio_discovery_verified_via_new_detection():
+    """10. Una Discovery prodotta con LmStudioClient può essere verificata tramite nuova Detection."""
+    from ai.lmstudio import LmStudioClient, LocalModelMetadata
+
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_lm")
+    eid = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    # Sottoclasse controllata per simulare LmStudioClient conforme senza connessione reale
+    class SimulatedLmStudioClient(LmStudioClient):
+        def __init__(self):
+            super().__init__(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234", model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+            self.call_count = 0
+
+        def is_available(self):
+            return True
+
+        def get_native_models(self):
+            return [LocalModelMetadata(key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct", display_name="Qwen2.5 7B", loaded_instances=[{"id": "qwen2.5-inst-1"}])]
+
+        def ensure_model_loaded(self, model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct", **kwargs):
+            self.model_id = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+            self._last_loaded_model = "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+            return "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+
+        def chat_completion(self, messages, **kwargs):
+            self.call_count += 1
+            if self.call_count == 1:
+                # Risposta Discovery
+                content = json.dumps({
+                    "topics": [{"label": "Ispezione Merci", "short_description": "Container al varco", "evidence_ids": [eid]}],
+                })
+            else:
+                # Risposta Detection di verifica
+                content = json.dumps({
+                    "decision": "PRESENT",
+                    "evidence_ids": [eid],
+                    "rationale": "Confermato container al varco.",
+                })
+            return LlmCompletionResponse(
+                content=content,
+                model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                prompt_tokens=30,
+                completion_tokens=20,
+                total_tokens=50,
+                latency_seconds=0.03,
+            )
+
+    client = SimulatedLmStudioClient()
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+
+    # 1. Esegui discovery
+    disc_res = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc, client=client, model_name="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert disc_res.metadata.get("origin") == AnalysisOrigin.LIVE_LM_STUDIO.value
+    run_id = disc_res.metadata["run_id"]
+
+    # 2. Esegui verifica
+    topic = disc_res.topics[0]
+    det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=topic,
+        discovery_run_id=run_id,
+        client=client,
+    )
+
+    assert det_res.decision == TopicDecision.PRESENT
+    assert consistency.status == ConsistencyStatus.CONSISTENT
+    assert consistency.discovery_run_id == run_id
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_11_no_discovery_evidence_ids_passed_to_detection():
+    """11. Nessun evidence ID del Discovery viene passato alla seconda inferenza."""
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_isolation")
+    eid_secret = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Logistica Clandestina",
+        short_description="Ispezione notturna",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_secret,),
+    )
+    disc_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_isolation",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_iso_001", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value},
+    )
+
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [eid_secret],
+        "rationale": "Rilevato autonomamente.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_isolation", disc_res)
+
+    service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_iso_001",
+    )
+
+    assert len(client.call_history) == 1
+    call = client.call_history[0]
+    messages_str = json.dumps(call["messages"])
+
+    # La label e la descrizione DEVONO essere presenti
+    assert "Logistica Clandestina" in messages_str
+    assert "Ispezione notturna" in messages_str
+    # L'evidence_id del discovery NON deve apparire come suggerimento nel prompt di query
+    assert f'"{eid_secret}"' not in messages_str or eid_secret not in messages_str.split("CRITERI DI DECISIONE")[0]
+
+
+def test_audit_b3_12_consistency_uses_only_properly_identified_results():
+    """12. La Consistency usa soltanto i risultati correttamente identificati."""
+    doc = _build_test_document("doc_cons")
+    eid_1 = doc.all_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+    eid_2 = doc.all_evidence_sections[1].evidence_id
+
+    disc_topic = DiscoveredTopic(
+        label="Traffico",
+        short_description="Desc",
+        evidence_ids=(eid_1,),
+    )
+    disc_res = TopicDiscoveryResult(
+        topics=(disc_topic,),
+        provenance_document_id="doc_cons",
+        metadata={"run_id": "run_cons_001", "origin": AnalysisOrigin.MOCK_INSPECTION.value},
+    )
+
+    # Caso A: Disgiunto -> PARTIALLY_CONSISTENT
+    client = MockProvenanceLlmClient(responses=[{
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [eid_2],
+        "rationale": "Evidenza diversa trovata.",
+    }])
+    service = LocalTopicAnalysisService(client=client)
+    service.register_mock_discovery_run("doc_cons", disc_res)
+
+    det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=doc,
+        discovered_topic=disc_topic,
+        discovery_run_id="run_cons_001",
+    )
+    assert consistency.status == ConsistencyStatus.PARTIALLY_CONSISTENT
+    assert len(consistency.overlap_evidence_ids) == 0
+

+    def test_whatsapp_importer_streams_transcript_without_read_bytes(self, tmp_path):
+        """Verifica che WhatsAppExportImporter non effettui read_bytes() dell'intero transcript in RAM."""
+        chat_path = tmp_path / "_chat.txt"
+        chat_path.write_text(
+            "10/05/2024, 10:00 - Alice: Messaggio riga 1\n"
+            "10/05/2024, 10:01 - Bob: Messaggio riga 2\n",
+            encoding="utf-8",
+        )
+
+        importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+        with patch.object(Path, "read_bytes", side_effect=AssertionError("read_bytes() non deve essere chiamato per il transcript!")):
+            records = list(importer.import_records(chat_path))
+            assert len(records) == 2
+            assert records[0].source_record_id == "msg_1"
+            assert records[1].source_record_id == "msg_2"

 class TestLargeWhatsAppZipArchiveSecurity:

+"""
+tests/unit/test_large_pipeline_bounded.py
+-----------------------------------------
+Test per la pipeline di ingestion su stream di grandi dimensioni (Criteri 27, 28, 29, 37):
+- Verifica che la pipeline processi record in modo deterministico e senza duplicazioni non necessarie.
+- Verifica del comportamento su centinaia di messaggi senza degradazione o leak.
+- Verifica corretta classificazione di ImportedConversationInfo.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+import pytest
+
+from importer.whatsapp_export import WhatsAppExportImporter
+from ui.ingestion import ingest_file_payload
+from ui.models import IngestionRequest, SourceFormat
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_synthetic_large_stream_ingestion(tmp_path: Path):
+    """
+    Simula una chat con un elevato numero di righe e messaggi sequenziali,
+    verificando che l'ingestion avvenga con successo, calcoli lo SHA-256 incrementale,
+    e produca ConversationEvidenceDocument immutabili corretti.
+    """
+    chat_file = tmp_path / "chat_large_stream.txt"
+    num_messages = 500
+
+    # Scrive 500 messaggi strutturati WhatsApp
+    with open(chat_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        for i in range(num_messages):
+            sender = "Alice" if i % 2 == 0 else "Bob"
+            f.write(f"12/03/2024, 10:15 - {sender}: Messaggio forense numero {i} con dati di audit.\n")
+
+    req = IngestionRequest(
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        filename=chat_file.name,
+        file_bytes=chat_file.read_bytes(),
+    )
+
+    res = ingest_file_payload(req)
+
+    assert res.summary.raw_record_count == num_messages
+    assert res.summary.normalized_record_count == num_messages
+    assert res.summary.unified_message_count == num_messages
+    assert len(res.documents) >= 1
+
+    # Verifica che il documento principale sia stato costruito correttamente
+    doc_id = res.summary.selected_document_id
+    assert doc_id is not None
+    doc = res.documents[doc_id]
+    assert len(doc.bundles) == num_messages
+    assert len(doc.all_evidence_sections) == num_messages
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_upload_size_limit_distinction():
+    """Verifica che MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE sia impostato a 2GB (Criterio 29)."""
+    from ui.ingestion import MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE
+    assert MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE == 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024  # 2 GB
diff --git a/tests/unit/test_lmstudio_binding_lifecycle.py b/tests/unit/test_lmstudio_binding_lifecycle.py

index 0000000..eb20a30

+++ b/tests/unit/test_lmstudio_binding_lifecycle.py
@@ -0,0 +1,761 @@
+"""
+tests/unit/test_lmstudio_binding_lifecycle.py
+---------------------------------------------
+Test di unità per la gestione del ciclo di vita dei binding dei modelli LM Studio (BLOCKER 2).
+
+Verifica:
+1. Binding registrato e unload riuscito -> binding invalidato.
+2. Binding registrato e unload fallito -> binding preservato.
+3. Due client indipendenti con registri isolati.
+4. Due istanze differenti senza cross-talk.
+5. Stesso instance ID riutilizzato successivamente.
+6. Risposta completion senza choices -> AiBackendProtocolError, binding NON verificato.
+7. Risposta completion senza message -> AiBackendProtocolError, binding NON verificato.
+8. Risposta completion con JSON malformato -> AiBackendProtocolError, binding NON verificato.
+9. Response model errato -> AiModelMismatchError, binding NON verificato.
+10. Status di caricamento sconosciuto -> AiBackendProtocolError, mai LOADED.
+11. Status loading -> ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADING, _last_loaded_model non aggiornato.
+12. Status loaded valido -> ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED, binding verificato.
+13. JIT_UNVERIFIED -> _last_loaded_model non aggiornato prematuramente.
+14. Promozione positiva a verificato solo dopo completamento interamente valido.
+15. Nessuna contaminazione tra famiglie (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, Llama, DeepSeek).
+16. Nessun aggiornamento prematuro dei metadati su fallimento.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+import urllib.error
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import (
+    AiBackendProtocolError,
+    AiModelMismatchError,
+    AiModelLoadError,
+)
+from ai.lmstudio import (
+    LmStudioClient,
+    ModelIdentityBinding,
+    ModelLifecycleStatus,
+    ModelLoadResult,
+    get_model_family,
+    is_conflicting_model_or_instance,
+    is_equivalent_operational_model,
+)
+
+
+def _make_http_response(body_dict_or_str: dict | str, status: int = 200) -> MagicMock:
+    if isinstance(body_dict_or_str, dict):
+        raw_bytes = json.dumps(body_dict_or_str).encode("utf-8")
+    else:
+        raw_bytes = body_dict_or_str.encode("utf-8")
+    resp = MagicMock()
+    resp.read.return_value = raw_bytes
+    resp.status = status
+    resp.__enter__.return_value = resp
+    resp.__exit__.return_value = None
+    return resp
+
+
+class TestLmStudioModelBindingLifecycle:
+    """Verifiche formali e architetturali del lifecycle dei binding (Blocker 2)."""
+
+    def test_binding_registered_and_unload_success(self):
+        """1. Binding registrato e unload riuscito -> binding rimosso."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        binding = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-uuid-1",
+            api_identifier="inst-uuid-1",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(binding)
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-uuid-1") is binding
+        assert client.get_binding("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is binding
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response({}, status=200)):
+            res = client.unload_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res is True
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-uuid-1") is None
+        assert client.get_binding("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is None
+        assert client._active_binding is None
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None
+
+    def test_binding_registered_and_unload_failure(self):
+        """2. Binding registrato e unload fallito -> binding NON invalidato arbitrariamente."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        binding = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-uuid-2",
+            api_identifier="inst-uuid-2",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(binding)
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=urllib.error.HTTPError("http://127.0.0.1:1234", 500, "Internal Server Error", {}, None)):
+            res = client.unload_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res is False
+        # Il binding deve rimanere intatto perché il modello non è stato effettivamente scaricato
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-uuid-2") is binding
+        assert client.get_binding("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is binding
+
+    def test_two_independent_clients_isolated_bindings(self):
+        """3. Due client indipendenti possiedono registri isolati."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234")
+        client_b = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234")
+
+        binding_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="client-a-inst",
+            api_identifier="client-a-inst",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(binding_a)
+
+        # client_a ha il proprio binding
+        assert client_a.get_binding("client-a-inst") is binding_a
+        # Invalida su client_a
+        client_a.invalidate_binding("client-a-inst")
+        assert client_a.get_binding("client-a-inst") is None
+
+    def test_two_different_instances_distinct_bindings(self):
+        """4. Due istanze differenti hanno binding distinti senza contaminazione."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        binding_qwen = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen-42",
+            api_identifier="inst-qwen-42",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        binding_llama = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-llama-99",
+            api_identifier="inst-llama-99",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(binding_qwen)
+        client.register_binding(binding_llama)
+
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-qwen-42") is binding_qwen
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-llama-99") is binding_llama
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-qwen-42") is not client.get_binding("inst-llama-99")
+
+        # Invalidazione di Qwen non tocca Llama
+        client.invalidate_binding("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-qwen-42") is None
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-llama-99") is binding_llama
+
+    def test_same_instance_id_reused_subsequently(self):
+        """5. Stesso instance ID riutilizzato successivamente senza conflitti."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        b1 = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="reused-id",
+            api_identifier="reused-id",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b1)
+        assert client.get_binding("reused-id") is b1
+
+        client.invalidate_binding("reused-id")
+        assert client.get_binding("reused-id") is None
+
+        b2 = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="reused-id",
+            api_identifier="reused-id",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b2)
+        assert client.get_binding("reused-id") is b2
+
+    def test_completion_response_missing_or_empty_choices(self):
+        """6. Completion con choices=[] solleva AiBackendProtocolError e lascia binding NON verificato."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="jit-inst-01",
+            api_identifier="jit-inst-01",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+
+        invalid_resp = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [],  # Vuoto!
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(invalid_resp)):
+            with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError, match="choices.*mancante, vuoto o non lista"):
+                client.chat_completion(
+                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                    model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                )
+
+        # Regola critica: il binding NON deve essere diventato verificato!
+        assert provisional.is_verified is False
+        assert provisional.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None
+
+    def test_completion_response_missing_message(self):
+        """7. Completion senza campo message solleva AiBackendProtocolError e non verifica il binding."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+
+        invalid_resp = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop"}],  # message mancante!
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(invalid_resp)):
+            with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError, match="message.*deve essere un dizionario"):
+                client.chat_completion(
+                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                    model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                )
+
+        assert provisional.is_verified is False
+        assert provisional.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED
+
+    def test_completion_malformed_json(self):
+        """8. Completion con JSON malformato solleva AiBackendProtocolError e non verifica il binding."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response("{not valid json")):
+            with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError, match="JSON malformato"):
+                client.chat_completion(
+                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                    model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                )
+
+        assert provisional.is_verified is False
+
+    def test_completion_wrong_response_model(self):
+        """9. Response model errato solleva AiModelMismatchError e non verifica il binding."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+
+        mismatched_resp = {
+            "model": "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",  # Mismatch con Qwen!
+            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "ciao"}, "finish_reason": "stop"}],
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(mismatched_resp)):
+            with pytest.raises(AiModelMismatchError, match="Response model mismatch"):
+                client.chat_completion(
+                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                    model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                )
+
+        assert provisional.is_verified is False
+
+    def test_load_model_unknown_status_rejected(self):
+        """10. Status sconosciuto in load_model solleva AiBackendProtocolError e NON diventa LOADED."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        nonsense_resp = {
+            "status": "nonsense",
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(nonsense_resp)):
+            with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError, match="status sconosciuto o non riconosciuto"):
+                client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None
+        assert client._active_binding is None
+
+    def test_load_model_loading_status(self):
+        """11. Status loading restituisce ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADING senza impostare _last_loaded_model."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        loading_resp = {
+            "status": "loading",
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(loading_resp)):
+            res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADING
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None
+
+    def test_load_model_loaded_valid(self):
+        """12. Status loaded valido produce ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED e certifica il binding."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        valid_resp = {
+            "status": "loaded",
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "instance_id": "real-inst-uuid-101",
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(valid_resp)):
+            res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED
+        assert res.instance_id == "real-inst-uuid-101"
+        assert client._last_loaded_model == "real-inst-uuid-101"
+        assert client._active_binding is not None
+        assert client._active_binding.is_verified is True
+
+    def test_load_model_jit_unverified_preserves_unverified(self):
+        """13. Risposta senza modello esplicito produce JIT_UNVERIFIED non verificato."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        opaque_resp = {
+            "id": "opaque-inst-999",
+            # Nessun campo model o status loaded
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(opaque_resp)):
+            res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED
+        assert client._active_binding is not None
+        assert client._active_binding.is_verified is False
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None  # NON aggiornato prematuramente
+
+    def test_provisional_binding_promoted_only_after_full_completion(self):
+        """14. Promozione a verificato avviene solo dopo completamento convalidato."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+
+        valid_completion = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [
+                {
+                    "message": {"content": "Risposta valida"},
+                    "finish_reason": "stop",
+                }
+            ],
+            "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 10, "completion_tokens": 5, "total_tokens": 15},
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(valid_completion)):
+            resp = client.chat_completion(
+                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "ciao"}],
+                model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            )
+
+        assert resp.content == "Risposta valida"
+        assert provisional.is_verified is True
+        assert provisional.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED
+        assert client._last_loaded_model == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+        assert client.load_mode == "jit_verified"
+
+    def test_no_cross_model_contamination(self):
+        """15. Distinzione rigorosa tra famiglie Qwen2.5, Qwen3, Llama e DeepSeek."""
+        assert get_model_family("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") == "QWEN2.5"
+        assert get_model_family("qwen3-8b-instruct") == "QWEN3"
+        assert get_model_family("meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct") == "LLAMA"
+        assert get_model_family("deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b") == "DEEPSEEK"
+
+        # Conflitti incrociati vietati
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct") is True
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "qwen3-8b-instruct") is True
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b") is True
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct", "qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is True
+
+        # Non equivalenti
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "qwen3-8b-instruct") is False
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct") is False
+
+    def test_no_premature_metadata_update_on_failure(self):
+        """16. In caso di errore HTTP durante il caricamento, nessun metadato viene aggiornato."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=urllib.error.HTTPError("http://127.0.0.1:1234", 400, "Bad Request", {}, io.BytesIO(b"Bad Request"))):
+            with pytest.raises(AiModelLoadError):
+                client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert client._last_loaded_model is None
+        assert client._last_loaded_model_key is None
+        assert client._active_binding is None
+
+
+class TestLmStudioClientIsolationAudit:
+    """
+    Test suite mirata per la verifica formale dell'isolamento per-client
+    e l'assenza totale di registri globali mutabili (BLOCKER 1).
+    """
+
+    def test_client_registration_isolated_from_other_clients(self):
+        """Riproduzione esatta Blocker 1: Client A registra binding, Client B non deve vederlo."""
+        from ai.lmstudio import get_model_binding
+        client_a = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234")
+        client_b = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234")
+        inst_id = "opaque-private-client-a"
+
+        assert client_b.get_binding(inst_id) is None
+        assert get_model_binding(inst_id) is None
+
+        binding_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id=inst_id,
+            api_identifier=inst_id,
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(binding_a)
+
+        # Client A possiede il binding
+        assert client_a.get_binding(inst_id) is binding_a
+
+        # Client B NON deve vedere il binding di Client A (Isolamento rigoroso)
+        assert client_b.get_binding(inst_id) is None
+        assert (client_b.get_binding(inst_id) is binding_a) is False
+
+        # Il vecchio registro globale NON memorizza lo stato
+        assert get_model_binding(inst_id) is None
+
+    def test_two_clients_same_model_different_instance_ids(self):
+        """Due client con lo stesso model key ma instance ID differenti rimangono isolati."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient()
+        client_b = LmStudioClient()
+
+        binding_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen-alpha",
+            api_identifier="inst-qwen-alpha",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        binding_b = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen-beta",
+            api_identifier="inst-qwen-beta",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(binding_a)
+        client_b.register_binding(binding_b)
+
+        assert client_a.get_binding("inst-qwen-alpha") is binding_a
+        assert client_a.get_binding("inst-qwen-beta") is None
+
+        assert client_b.get_binding("inst-qwen-beta") is binding_b
+        assert client_b.get_binding("inst-qwen-alpha") is None
+
+    def test_two_clients_different_model_keys(self):
+        """Due client con model key differenti non condividono metadati."""
+        client_qwen = LmStudioClient()
+        client_llama = LmStudioClient()
+
+        b_qwen = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen-1",
+            api_identifier="inst-qwen-1",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        b_llama = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-llama-1",
+            api_identifier="inst-llama-1",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_qwen.register_binding(b_qwen)
+        client_llama.register_binding(b_llama)
+
+        assert client_qwen.get_binding("inst-llama-1") is None
+        assert client_llama.get_binding("inst-qwen-1") is None
+
+    def test_unload_client_a_does_not_contaminate_or_clear_client_b(self):
+        """Unload del client A non tocca né contamina i binding del client B."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient()
+        client_b = LmStudioClient()
+
+        b_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-a",
+            api_identifier="inst-a",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        b_b = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-b",
+            api_identifier="inst-b",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(b_a)
+        client_b.register_binding(b_b)
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response({}, 200)):
+            res = client_a.unload_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+        assert res is True
+        assert client_a.get_binding("inst-a") is None
+        # Client B è perfettamente integro e non contaminato
+        assert client_b.get_binding("inst-b") is b_b
+
+    def test_binding_invalidation_local_only(self):
+        """L'invalidazione dei binding è confinata al singolo client."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient()
+        client_b = LmStudioClient()
+
+        b_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-shared-name",
+            api_identifier="inst-shared-name",
+        )
+        b_b = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-shared-name",
+            api_identifier="inst-shared-name",
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(b_a)
+        client_b.register_binding(b_b)
+
+        client_a.invalidate_binding("inst-shared-name")
+        assert client_a.get_binding("inst-shared-name") is None
+        assert client_b.get_binding("inst-shared-name") is b_b
+
+    def test_model_switch_without_residue(self):
+        """Il cambio modello aggiorna l'active binding e lo stato del client senza residui obsoleti."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        b_qwen = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen-old",
+            api_identifier="inst-qwen-old",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b_qwen)
+        assert client._active_binding is b_qwen
+
+        client.invalidate_binding("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-qwen-old") is None
+        assert client._active_binding is None
+
+        b_llama = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-llama-new",
+            api_identifier="inst-llama-new",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b_llama)
+        assert client._active_binding is b_llama
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-qwen-old") is None
+        assert client.get_binding("inst-llama-new") is b_llama
+
+    def test_reuse_of_old_instance_id_rejected_if_different_model(self):
+        """Riutilizzo di un vecchio instance ID per un modello non corrispondente solleva conflitto."""
+        from ai.lmstudio import is_conflicting_model_or_instance
+        # Se un vecchio instance ID conteneva frammenti o identificativi incompatibili
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "llama-instance-old") is True
+
+    def test_equivalent_model_id_only_with_local_verified_binding(self):
+        """L'equivalenza tra model key e instance ID opaco richiede binding locale esplicito."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient()
+        client_b = LmStudioClient()
+        inst_id = "opaque-instance-xyz"
+
+        binding_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id=inst_id,
+            api_identifier=inst_id,
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(binding_a)
+
+        # Con il client_a che possiede il binding -> EQUIVALENTE
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", inst_id, client=client_a) is True
+        assert client_a.is_equivalent_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", inst_id) is True
+
+        # Con client_b o senza client -> NON EQUIVALENTE (nessun leak o binding globale)
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", inst_id, client=client_b) is False
+        assert client_b.is_equivalent_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", inst_id) is False
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", inst_id) is False
+
+    def test_completion_via_opaque_id_succeeds_only_on_owning_client(self):
+        """Completion con identificativo opaco verificata con successo solo sul client proprietario."""
+        client_a = LmStudioClient()
+        client_b = LmStudioClient()
+        inst_id = "opaque-verified-01"
+
+        binding_a = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id=inst_id,
+            api_identifier=inst_id,
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_a.register_binding(binding_a)
+
+        chat_payload = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "Risposta isolata da Client A"}}],
+        }
+
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(chat_payload, 200)):
+            res_a = client_a.chat_completion(
+                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                model_id=inst_id,
+            )
+        assert res_a.content == "Risposta isolata da Client A"
+
+        # Client B, non avendo il binding per opaque-verified-01, solleva mismatch se la risposta è qwen2.5
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response(chat_payload, 200)):
+            with pytest.raises(AiModelMismatchError):
+                client_b.chat_completion(
+                    messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "test"}],
+                    model_id=inst_id,
+                )
+
+    def test_get_active_binding_lifecycle(self):
+        """Blocker 3.1-3.5: Lifecycle completo di get_active_binding()."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        # 1. Nessun binding prima del caricamento
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+        # 2. Binding provvisorio (JIT_UNVERIFIED): presente ma NON verificato
+        provisional = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="jit-instance-01",
+            api_identifier="jit-instance-01",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED,
+            is_verified=False,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(provisional)
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is provisional
+        assert client.get_active_binding().is_verified is False
+
+        # 3. Binding verificato dopo caricamento con successo
+        verified = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="verified-instance-01",
+            api_identifier="verified-instance-01",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+            loaded_instance_context_length=8192,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(verified)
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is verified
+        assert client.get_active_binding().is_verified is True
+        assert client.get_active_binding().loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+
+        # 4. Binding eliminato dopo unload riuscito
+        with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=_make_http_response({}, 200)):
+            res_unload = client.unload_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        assert res_unload is True
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+    def test_binding_invalidated_after_model_change(self):
+        """Blocker 3.5: Binding aggiornato e invalidato dopo cambio modello sullo stesso client."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        b_qwen = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-qwen",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b_qwen)
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is b_qwen
+
+        # Cambio modello a Llama
+        b_llama = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="inst-llama",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(b_llama)
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is b_llama
+        assert client.get_active_binding().requested_canonical_id == "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct"
+
+    def test_two_independent_clients_never_share_active_binding(self):
+        """Blocker 3.6: Due client indipendenti non condividono alcuno stato o binding attivo."""
+        client_1 = LmStudioClient()
+        client_2 = LmStudioClient()
+
+        b1 = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="client1-inst",
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client_1.register_binding(b1)
+
+        assert client_1.get_active_binding() is b1
+        assert client_2.get_active_binding() is None
+
+    def test_unexpected_instance_replacement_detected(self):
+        """Blocker 3.10: Sostituzione imprevista dell'istanza rilevata e rifiutata."""
+        client = LmStudioClient()
+        binding = ModelIdentityBinding(
+            requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            resolved_model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            loaded_instance_id="qwen-inst-01",
+            status=ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED,
+            is_verified=True,
+        )
+        client.register_binding(binding)
+
+        # Sostituzione con famiglia aliena
+        alien_resp_model = "deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b"
+        assert is_conflicting_model_or_instance("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", alien_resp_model) is True
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", alien_resp_model, client=client) is False
+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_lmstudio_model_loading.py
+-----------------------------------------
+Test esaustivi per il Model Loading Lifecycle e la validazione rigorosa dei contratti LM Studio:
+- HTTP 200 con payload loaded valido -> ModelLoadResult con status LOADED.
+- HTTP 200 con payload failed (es. "Not enough VRAM") -> solleva AiModelLoadError, mai LOADED.
+- HTTP 200 con JSON malformato -> solleva AiBackendProtocolError.
+- HTTP 200 con body vuoto -> solleva AiBackendProtocolError.
+- HTTP 400 -> solleva AiModelLoadError con status_code=400.
+- HTTP 404 / 405 -> restituisce status JIT_UNVERIFIED e NON memorizza _last_loaded_model.
+- HTTP 500 -> solleva AiModelLoadError con status_code=500.
+- Timeout -> solleva AiBackendTimeoutError.
+- Server offline -> solleva LmStudioUnavailableError.
+- Model Mismatch in modalità JIT -> solleva AiModelMismatchError se response.model non corrisponde.
+- JIT verificato con completion conforme -> valida il binding.
+- Rigorosa distinzione DeepSeek vs Qwen e Qwen2.5 vs Qwen3.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+import socket
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from typing import Any
+
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import (
+    AiBackendProtocolError,
+    AiBackendRequestError,
+    AiBackendTimeoutError,
+    AiModelLoadError,
+    AiModelMismatchError,
+    AiModelNotInstalledError,
+    LmStudioUnavailableError,
+)
+from ai.lmstudio import (
+    LmStudioClient,
+    ModelLifecycleStatus,
+    ModelLoadResult,
+    clear_model_bindings,
+    get_model_binding,
+    is_equivalent_operational_model,
+)
+
+
+class DummyHTTPResponse:
+    def __init__(self, data: bytes, status: int = 200):
+        self.data = data
+        self.status = status
+
+    def read(self) -> bytes:
+        return self.data
+
+    def __enter__(self):
+        return self
+
+    def __exit__(self, *args):
+        pass
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def clean_bindings():
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    yield
+    clear_model_bindings()
+
+
+def test_load_model_http_200_success(monkeypatch):
+    """Verifica caricamento riuscito con status loaded e instance_id valido."""
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        payload = {
+            "status": "loaded",
+            "instance_id": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct-inst-42",
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        }
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert isinstance(res, ModelLoadResult)
+    assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED
+    assert res.instance_id == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct-inst-42"
+    assert res.model_key == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+    assert client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is True
+
+
+def test_load_model_http_200_failure_payload_raises_error(monkeypatch):
+    """Verifica che HTTP 200 con status 'failed' o messaggio di errore sollevi AiModelLoadError e NON diventi loaded."""
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        payload = {
+            "status": "failed",
+            "error": "Not enough VRAM available to load model",
+        }
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelLoadError) as exc_info:
+        client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert "Not enough VRAM" in str(exc_info.value)
+    assert exc_info.value.status_code == 200
+    assert getattr(client, "_last_loaded_model", None) is None
+
+
+def test_load_model_http_200_malformed_json_raises_protocol_error(monkeypatch):
+    """Verifica che HTTP 200 con corpo non-JSON sollevi AiBackendProtocolError e NON finga loaded."""
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(b"<html><body>502 Bad Gateway or invalid text</body></html>")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError):
+        client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert getattr(client, "_last_loaded_model", None) is None
+
+

+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelLoadError) as exc_info:

+

+def test_jit_completion_verifies_model_binding(monkeypatch):
+    """Verifica che in modalità JIT_UNVERIFIED la prima completion verifichi il binding del modello."""
+    client = LmStudioClient(model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    client.load_mode = "jit_unverified"
+
+    # Caso 1: response.model conforme
+    def mock_chat_success(req, timeout):
+        payload = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "Risposta"}}],
+        }
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_chat_success)
+    res = client.chat_completion([{"role": "user", "content": "test"}])
+    assert res.content == "Risposta"
+    assert client.load_mode == "jit_verified"
+    assert client._last_loaded_model == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+

+def test_audit_1_http_200_opaque_instance_id_only_is_jit_unverified(monkeypatch):

+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps({"id": "opaque-instance-001"}).encode("utf-8"))

+    binding = client.get_binding("opaque-instance-001")

+def test_audit_4_http_200_different_model_raises_mismatch_error(monkeypatch):
+    """4. Risposta con modello diverso -> solleva categoricamente AiModelMismatchError."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        payload = {
+            "status": "loaded",
+            "model": "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+            "id": "opaque-instance-004",
+        }
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelMismatchError):
+        client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert getattr(client, "_last_loaded_model", None) is None
+
+
+def test_audit_5_completion_with_opaque_target_and_bound_canonical_response_succeeds(monkeypatch):
+    """5. Completion con target API identifier opaco e response.model canonico correttamente associato -> accettata e binding verificato."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    # Step 1: load_model con risposta opaca (JIT_UNVERIFIED)
+    def mock_load(req, timeout):
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps({"id": "opaque-instance-005"}).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_load)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    load_res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert load_res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED
+    assert getattr(client, "_last_loaded_model", None) is None
+
+    # Step 2: completion con target_model="opaque-instance-005" e response.model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+    def mock_chat(req, timeout):
+        payload = {
+            "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "choices": [{"message": {"content": "Risposta inferenza valida"}}],
+        }
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_chat)
+    chat_res = client.chat_completion(
+        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "ping"}],
+        model_id="opaque-instance-005",
+    )
+
+    assert chat_res.content == "Risposta inferenza valida"
+    assert chat_res.model == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+    # Ora il binding è stato verificato dalla prima completion conforme
+    binding = client.get_binding("opaque-instance-005")
+    assert binding is not None
+    assert binding.is_verified is True
+    assert client._last_loaded_model == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+

+def test_official_load_response_valid_schema_verified(monkeypatch):
+    """Blocker 2.1, 2.2: Payload ufficiale POST /api/v1/models/load con load_config.context_length=8192."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    # Schema esatto dalla documentazione LM Studio: https://lmstudio.ai/docs/developer/rest/load
+    official_payload = {
+        "type": "llm",
+        "instance_id": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        "status": "loaded",
+        "load_config": {
+            "context_length": 8192,
+        },
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(official_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", context_length=8192)
+
+    assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED
+    assert res.instance_id == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+    assert res.context_length == 8192
+    assert res.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert res.context_evidence_source == "LOADER_API_RESPONSE"
+    assert client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is True
+    binding = client.get_active_binding()
+    assert binding is not None
+    assert binding.is_verified is True
+    assert binding.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert binding.context_evidence_source == "LOADER_API_RESPONSE"
+
+

+def test_official_load_response_opaque_instance_verified_via_get(monkeypatch):
+    """Blocker 2.4: Istanza opaca senza model_key nel load, verificata tramite query GET dei modelli."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    load_payload = {
+        "type": "llm",
+        "instance_id": "instance-qwen-001",
+        "status": "loaded",
+        "load_config": {
+            "context_length": 8192,
+        },
+    }
+    get_payload = {
+        "models": [
+            {
+                "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+                "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+                "loaded_instances": [
+                    {
+                        "id": "instance-qwen-001",
+                        "config": {"context_length": 8192},
+                    }
+                ],
+            }
+        ]
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/api/v1/models/load" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(load_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        elif "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(get_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(b"{}")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", context_length=8192)
+
+    assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED
+    assert res.instance_id == "instance-qwen-001"
+    assert res.model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"
+    binding = client.get_active_binding()
+    assert binding is not None
+    assert binding.is_verified is True
+    assert binding.loaded_instance_id == "instance-qwen-001"
+    assert binding.resolved_model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"
+
+
+def test_official_load_response_opaque_instance_unverifiable_becomes_jit_unverified(monkeypatch):
+    """Blocker 2.5: Istanza opaca non verificabile tramite GET diventa JIT_UNVERIFIED senza fingere successo."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    load_payload = {
+        "type": "llm",
+        "instance_id": "unknown-opaque-instance-999",
+        "status": "loaded",
+        "load_config": {
+            "context_length": 8192,
+        },
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/api/v1/models/load" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(load_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        elif "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            # GET non elenca questa istanza
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps({"models": []}).encode("utf-8"))
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(b"{}")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert res.status == ModelLifecycleStatus.JIT_UNVERIFIED
+    binding = client.get_active_binding()
+    assert binding is not None
+    assert binding.is_verified is False
+    assert res.context_evidence_source == "UNVERIFIED"
+

+def test_official_load_response_opaque_instance_belongs_to_alien_model_raises_mismatch(monkeypatch):

+                "loaded_instances": [{"id": "instance-alien-42"}],
+            }
+        ]
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/api/v1/models/load" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(load_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        elif "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(get_payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        return DummyHTTPResponse(b"{}")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelMismatchError) as exc_info:
+        client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+

+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_lmstudio_native_integration.py
+----------------------------------------------
+Test di integrazione simulata end-to-end conforme ai contratti ufficiali LM Studio REST API:
+Sequenza:
+1. GET /api/v1/models (elenco modelli installati con Qwen2.5 Q4_K_M)
+2. Risoluzione deterministica del modello Qwen2.5 Q4_K_M
+3. POST /api/v1/models/load (payload ufficiale con instance_id e load_config.context_length)
+4. Lettura instance_id e load_config
+5. Successiva interrogazione GET /api/v1/models per verifica istanza caricata e binding
+6. Topic Discovery con vero adapter LmStudioClient e validazione response.model
+7. Topic Detection con vero adapter LmStudioClient e validazione response.model
+8. Valutazione Consistency e integrità del binding
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import LlmCompletionResponse
+from ai.lmstudio import (
+    AiModelNotInstalledError,
+    LmStudioClient,
+    ModelLifecycleStatus,
+    clear_model_bindings,
+    is_equivalent_operational_model,
+    resolve_operational_model_from_metadata,
+)
+from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService
+from ui.demo import build_synthetic_demo_dataset
+
+
+class DummyHTTPResponse:
+    def __init__(self, data: bytes, status: int = 200):
+        self.data = data
+        self.status = status
+
+    def read(self) -> bytes:
+        return self.data
+
+    def __enter__(self):
+        return self
+
+    def __exit__(self, *args):
+        pass
+
+
+def _build_test_document():
+    doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+    return doc_demo
+
+
+def test_full_simulated_native_integration_sequence():
+    """Esegue l'intero ciclo GET -> RESOLVE -> LOAD -> GET -> DISCOVERY -> DETECTION -> CONSISTENCY."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    client = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234", model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    # Stato del mock server LM Studio
+    server_state = {
+        "is_loaded": False,
+        "loaded_instance_id": None,
+        "loaded_context": 8192,
+    }
+
+    doc = _build_test_document()
+    eid = doc.bundles[0].text_evidence_sections[0].evidence_id
+
+    # Risposta Discovery strutturata conforme
+    discovery_llm_json = json.dumps({
+        "topics": [
+            {
+                "label": "Logistica e Ispezione Depositi",
+                "short_description": "Pianificazione container e ispezione depositi merci",
+                "evidence_ids": [eid],
+            }
+        ]
+    })
+
+    # Risposta Detection strutturata conforme
+    detection_llm_json = json.dumps({
+        "decision": "PRESENT",
+        "evidence_ids": [eid],
+        "rationale": "Il messaggio contiene espliciti riferimenti a logistica container e ispezione depositi.",
+    })
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout=30.0):
+        url = req.full_url
+        method = req.get_method()
+
+        # 1. GET /api/v1/models (elenco dettagliato nativo LM Studio con schema ufficiale)
+        if "/api/v1/models" in url and method == "GET":
+            loaded_instances = []
+            if server_state["is_loaded"]:
+                loaded_instances.append({
+                    "id": server_state["loaded_instance_id"],
+                    "config": {
+                        "context_length": server_state["loaded_context"],
+                    },
+                })
+            payload = {
+                "models": [
+                    {
+                        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+                        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+                        "quantization": {
+                            "name": "Q4_K_M",
+                            "bits_per_weight": 4,
+                        },
+                        "max_context_length": 32768,
+                        "loaded_instances": loaded_instances,
+                    }
+                ]
+            }
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+        # 2. GET /v1/models (liveness check)
+        if "/v1/models" in url and method == "GET":
+            payload = {"data": [{"id": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"}]}
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+        # 3. POST /api/v1/models/load (caricamento modello con schema ufficiale)
+        if "/api/v1/models/load" in url and method == "POST":
+            server_state["is_loaded"] = True
+            server_state["loaded_instance_id"] = "qwen-instance-8192"
+            payload = {
+                "type": "llm",
+                "instance_id": server_state["loaded_instance_id"],
+                "status": "loaded",
+                "load_config": {
+                    "context_length": server_state["loaded_context"],
+                },
+            }
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+
+        # 4. POST /v1/chat/completions (inferenza LLM per Discovery o Detection)
+        if "/v1/chat/completions" in url and method == "POST":
+            req_data = json.loads(req.data.decode("utf-8"))
+            messages = req_data.get("messages", [])
+            all_text = " ".join(m.get("content", "") for m in messages).lower()
+            if "open topic discovery" in all_text or "discover the main topics" in all_text or ("topics" in all_text and "evidence_ids" in all_text and "label" in all_text):
+                resp_content = discovery_llm_json
+            else:
+                resp_content = detection_llm_json
+
+            chat_res = {
+                "id": "chatcmpl-mock-12345",
+                "object": "chat.completion",
+                "model": server_state["loaded_instance_id"],
+                "choices": [
+                    {
+                        "index": 0,
+                        "message": {
+                            "role": "assistant",
+                            "content": resp_content,
+                        },
+                        "finish_reason": "stop",
+                    }
+                ],
+                "usage": {
+                    "prompt_tokens": 120,
+                    "completion_tokens": 45,
+                    "total_tokens": 165,
+                },
+            }
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(chat_res).encode("utf-8"))
+
+        raise ValueError(f"URL non gestito nel mock di integrazione: {url} ({method})")
+
+    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=mock_urlopen):
+        # PASSAGGIO 1: Raggiungibilità backend
+        assert client.is_available() is True
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 2: Query GET /api/v1/models e risoluzione Qwen2.5 Q4_K_M
+        native_models = client.get_native_models()
+        assert len(native_models) == 1
+        resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(native_models, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        assert resolved.model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"
+        assert resolved.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+        assert resolved.selected_quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+        assert resolved.quantization_evidence_source == "SERVER_API_QUANTIZATION_OBJECT"
+        assert resolved.is_loaded is False  # Non ancora caricato
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 3: Caricamento modello via ensure_model_loaded (POST /api/v1/models/load)
+        active_id = client.ensure_model_loaded(
+            model_id=resolved.model_key,
+            context_length=8192,
+        )
+        assert active_id == "qwen-instance-8192"
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 4: Verifica binding attivo del client
+        binding = client.get_active_binding()
+        assert binding is not None
+        assert binding.is_verified is True
+        assert binding.resolved_model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"
+        assert binding.loaded_instance_id == "qwen-instance-8192"
+        assert binding.api_identifier == "qwen-instance-8192"
+        assert binding.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+        assert binding.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+        assert binding.context_evidence_source == "LOADER_API_RESPONSE"
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 5: Verifica successiva interrogazione GET /api/v1/models
+        updated_native = client.get_native_models()
+        res_after = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(updated_native, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        assert res_after.is_loaded is True
+        assert res_after.instance_id == "qwen-instance-8192"
+        assert res_after.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+        assert res_after.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 6: Esecuzione Topic Discovery con vero adapter LmStudioClient
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc = _build_test_document()
+
+        disc_res = service.run_topic_discovery(
+            document=doc,
+            client=client,
+            model_name="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            max_topics=5,
+        )
+        assert len(disc_res.topics) == 1
+        assert disc_res.topics[0].label == "Logistica e Ispezione Depositi"
+        disc_model = disc_res.metadata.get("response_model")
+        assert disc_model == "qwen-instance-8192"
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", disc_model, client=client) is True
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 7: Esecuzione Topic Detection con vero adapter LmStudioClient (prova del nove)
+        first_topic = disc_res.topics[0]
+        det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc,
+            discovered_topic=first_topic,
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=client,
+            model_name="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        )
+        assert det_res is not None
+        assert det_res.decision.value == "PRESENT"
+        det_model = det_res.metadata.get("response_model")
+        assert det_model == "qwen-instance-8192"
+        assert is_equivalent_operational_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", det_model, client=client) is True
+
+        # PASSAGGIO 8: Verifica Consistency e allineamento formale
+        assert consistency is not None
+        assert consistency.status.value in ("CONFIRMED", "STRONG_CONFIRMATION", "CONSISTENT")
+        assert disc_model == det_model == binding.api_identifier
+
+
+def test_simulated_native_integration_negative_nonexistent_instance():
+    """Caso negativo: target_instance_id inesistente solleva AiModelNotInstalledError e non crea binding."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    client = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234", model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    payload = {
+        "models": [
+            {
+                "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+                "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+                "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+                "max_context_length": 32768,
+                "loaded_instances": [
+                    {"id": "qwen-instance-8192", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}
+                ],
+            }
+        ]
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout=30.0):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/v1/models" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps({"data": [{"id": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"}]}).encode("utf-8"))
+        if "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        raise ValueError(f"URL non atteso: {url}")
+
+    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=mock_urlopen):
+        from ai.backend import AiModelNotInstalledError
+        with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError) as exc_info:
+            client.ensure_model_loaded(
+                model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                target_instance_id="not-an-instance-id",
+            )
+        assert "not-an-instance-id" in str(exc_info.value)
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+
+def test_simulated_native_integration_negative_wrong_quantization():
+    """Caso negativo: quantizzazione esposta Q8_0 quando è richiesta Q4_K_M."""
+    clear_model_bindings()
+    client = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234", model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    payload = {
+        "models": [
+            {
+                "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q8_0",
+                "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+                "quantization": {"name": "Q8_0", "bits_per_weight": 8},
+                "max_context_length": 32768,
+                "loaded_instances": [
+                    {"id": "qwen-instance-q8", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}
+                ],
+            }
+        ]
+    }
+
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout=30.0):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            return DummyHTTPResponse(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        raise ValueError(f"URL non atteso: {url}")
+
+    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=mock_urlopen):
+        native_models = client.get_native_models()
+        # 1. Strict mode (fallback disabilitato): REJECTED con AiModelNotInstalledError
+        with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+            resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+                native_models,
+                "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+                allow_fallback_quantization=False,
+            )
+
+        # 2. UI / Explicit fallback autorizzato: ACCEPTED con fallback tracciato
+        res = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+            native_models,
+            "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+            allow_fallback_quantization=True,
+        )
+        assert res.selected_quantization == "Q8_0"
+        assert res.requested_quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+        assert res.selected_quantization != res.requested_quantization
+
