+"""
+tests/unit/test_media_asset_access.py
+--------------------------------------
+Test di conformità completi per FASE BLOCKER 4:
+MEDIA ZIP E MULTIMODALITÀ REALE
+
+Verifiche obbligatorie:
+1. TXT without media -> resolve_message_asset restituisce NO_REFERENCE
+2. ZIP with image -> estrazione sicura on-demand e risoluzione corretta
+3. ZIP with audio -> estrazione sicura on-demand e risoluzione corretta
+4. ZIP with missing media -> allegato citato ma assente nello ZIP produce MISSING
+5. ZIP with ambiguous media -> referenza ambigua produce AMBIGUOUS / reject
+6. ZIP with malformed member -> gestito con eccezione tipizzata
+7. ZIP traversal attacks (../evil.txt, ..\\evil.txt, folder/../evil.txt, C:\\evil.txt, \\\\server\\share, /etc/passwd) -> reject (MediaArchiveSecurityError)
+8. ZIP symlink -> membro con symlink attributo POSIX -> reject (MediaArchiveSecurityError)
+9. ZIP bomb -> membri eccedenti i limiti dimensionali o compression ratio -> reject (MediaArchiveSecurityError)
+10. ZIP CRC mismatch -> payload alterato con CRC non corrispondente -> reject (MediaArchiveSecurityError)
+11. source unavailable after ingestion -> solleva esplicitamente MediaSourceUnavailableError
+12. STT backend unavailable -> trascrizione fallita/non disponibile senza crash, evidenze originali preservate
+13. OCR backend unavailable -> OCR non disponibile senza crash, evidenze originali preservate
+14. Vision backend unavailable -> nessuna invocazione di Fake Vision su dati reali
+15. real processing with test fixtures -> arricchimento completo con evidenze tracciabili e documento immutabile
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+import tempfile
+import zipfile
+import zlib
+import pytest
+
+from ai.models import ConversationEvidenceDocument
+from multimodal.asset_access import (
+    MediaArchiveSecurityError,
+    MediaAssetAccessService,
+    MediaAssetRegistry,
+    MediaMemberNotFoundError,
+    MediaSourceUnavailableError,
+    validate_zip_member_security,
+)
+from multimodal.evidence import EvidenceSourceType, MessageEvidenceBundle, TextEvidenceSection
+from multimodal.models import (
+    AudioTranscriptionResult,
+    ImageOcrResult,
+    MediaKind,
+    MediaResolutionStatus,
+    OcrStatus,
+    ResolvedMediaAsset,
+    TranscriptionStatus,
+    VisionStatus,
+)
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+)
+from importer.models import RawRecord
+from validation.models import ValidationResult
+from unified.models import UnifiedMessage
+
+
+def _make_dummy_message(
+    msg_id: str = "msg_1",
+    media_ref: str | None = None,
+    text: str = "Test messaggio",
+    msg_type: CanonicalMessageType = CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+) -> UnifiedMessage:
+    raw = RawRecord(
+        source_name="whatsapp_export",
+        source_path="/test/chat.zip",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        record_type="message",
+        raw_fields={"text": text},
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+        metadata={},
+    )
+    norm = NormalizedRecord(
+        raw_record=raw,
+        validation_result=ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=()),
+        source_name="whatsapp_export",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        record_type="message",
+        timestamp=NormalizedTimestamp(status=TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT),
+        message_type=msg_type,
+        text_content=text,
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+    )
+    return UnifiedMessage(
+        message_id=f"unified:whatsapp_export:{msg_id}",
+        source_name="whatsapp_export",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        source_path="/test/chat.zip",
+        record_type="message",
+        timestamp=norm.timestamp,
+        message_type=msg_type,
+        text_content=text,
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+        provenance_record=norm,
+    )
+
+
+# --- 1. TXT without media ---
+
+def test_txt_without_media_returns_no_reference(tmp_path: Path):
+    txt_file = tmp_path / "chat.txt"
+    txt_file.write_text("12/03/2024, 10:00 - Alice: Ciao Bob\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="1", media_ref=None, text="Ciao Bob")
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=txt_file)
+    asset = service.resolve_message_asset(msg)
+
+    assert asset.status == MediaResolutionStatus.NO_REFERENCE
+    assert asset.is_resolved is False
+    assert asset.resolved_path is None
+
+
+# --- 2. ZIP with image ---
+
+def test_zip_with_image_materializes_on_demand(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_with_image.zip"
+    img_data = b"\xFF\xD8\xFF\xE0\x00\x10JFIF" + b"\x00" * 64
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("photo.jpg", img_data)
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="2", media_ref="photo.jpg", text="Ecco la foto", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    asset = service.resolve_message_asset(msg)
+
+    assert asset.status == MediaResolutionStatus.RESOLVED
+    assert asset.media_kind == MediaKind.IMAGE
+    assert asset.file_size_bytes == len(img_data)
+
+    # Verifica estrazione on-demand e cleanup automatico
+    with service.materialize_member("photo.jpg") as extracted_path:
+        assert extracted_path.exists()
+        assert extracted_path.read_bytes() == img_data
+
+    # La sandbox temporanea deve essere eliminata
+    assert not extracted_path.exists()
+
+
+# --- 3. ZIP with audio ---
+
+def test_zip_with_audio_materializes_on_demand(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_with_audio.zip"
+    audio_data = b"OggS\x00\x02" + b"\x00" * 128
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("voice_note.opus", audio_data)
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="3", media_ref="voice_note.opus", text="Nota vocale", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    asset = service.resolve_message_asset(msg)
+
+    assert asset.status == MediaResolutionStatus.RESOLVED
+    assert asset.media_kind == MediaKind.AUDIO
+    assert asset.file_size_bytes == len(audio_data)
+
+    with service.materialize_member("voice_note.opus") as extracted_path:
+        assert extracted_path.exists()
+        assert extracted_path.read_bytes() == audio_data
+    assert not extracted_path.exists()
+
+
+# --- 4. ZIP with missing media ---
+
+def test_zip_with_missing_media_returns_missing(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_empty.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("transcript.txt", "12/03/2024, 10:00 - Alice: audio.opus (allegato)")
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="4", media_ref="missing_audio.opus", text="Audio assente", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    asset = service.resolve_message_asset(msg)
+
+    assert asset.status == MediaResolutionStatus.MISSING
+    assert asset.is_resolved is False
+
+
+# --- 5. ZIP with ambiguous media ---
+
+def test_zip_with_ambiguous_media_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_ambiguous.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("folder1/photo.jpg", b"photo1")
+        zf.writestr("folder2/photo.jpg", b"photo2")
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="5", media_ref="photo.jpg", text="Foto ambigua", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    asset = service.resolve_message_asset(msg)
+
+    assert asset.status == MediaResolutionStatus.AMBIGUOUS
+
+
+# --- 6. ZIP with malformed member ---
+
+def test_zip_with_malformed_member_raises_error(tmp_path: Path):
+    corrupt_zip = tmp_path / "corrupt.zip"
+    corrupt_zip.write_bytes(b"NOT_A_ZIP_HEADER_DATA")
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=corrupt_zip)
+    with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError):
+        with service.materialize_member("any.jpg"):
+            pass
+
+
+# --- 7. ZIP traversal attacks ---
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "bad_name",
+    [
+        "../evil.txt",
+        "..\\evil.txt",
+        "folder/../evil.txt",
+        "folder\\..\\evil.txt",
+        "C:\\evil.txt",
+        "\\\\server\\share\\evil.txt",
+        "/etc/passwd",
+        "folder/../../etc/passwd",
+    ],
+)
+def test_zip_traversal_attacks_rejected(tmp_path: Path, bad_name: str):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "traversal.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("safe.txt", b"safe")
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
+        with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError, match="non consentito"):
+            validate_zip_member_security(zf, bad_name)
+
+
+# --- 8. ZIP symlink ---
+
+def test_zip_symlink_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "symlink.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        info = zipfile.ZipInfo("symlink.txt")
+        # Attributi POSIX per symlink: 0o120000
+        info.external_attr = 0o120777 << 16
+        zf.writestr(info, b"/etc/passwd")
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
+        with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError, match="symlink non consentito"):
+            validate_zip_member_security(zf, "symlink.txt")
+
+
+# --- 9. ZIP bomb ---
+
+def test_zip_bomb_large_member_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "bomb.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("huge.bin", b"X" * 100)
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
+        with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError, match="supera il limite consentito"):
+            validate_zip_member_security(zf, "huge.bin", max_single_size=50)
+
+
+# --- 10. ZIP CRC mismatch ---
+
+def test_zip_crc_mismatch_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "crc_tampered.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("tampered.jpg", b"ORIGINAL_CORRECT_DATA_STRING")
+
+    # Manomissione diretta del payload compresso su disco per creare CRC mismatch
+    raw_bytes = bytearray(zip_path.read_bytes())
+    idx = raw_bytes.find(b"ORIGINAL_CORRECT_DATA_STRING")
+    assert idx != -1
+    raw_bytes[idx] = ord("X")
+    zip_path.write_bytes(bytes(raw_bytes))
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError, match="Integrità compromessa"):
+        with service.materialize_member("tampered.jpg"):
+            pass
+
+
+# --- 11. Source unavailable after ingestion ---
+
+def test_source_unavailable_after_ingestion_raises_error(tmp_path: Path):
+    ghost_path = tmp_path / "deleted_source.zip"
+    # Il file non esiste
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=ghost_path)
+
+    with pytest.raises(MediaSourceUnavailableError, match="non disponibile"):
+        service.get_effective_archive_path()
+
+    with pytest.raises(MediaSourceUnavailableError, match="non disponibile"):
+        with service.materialize_member("audio.opus"):
+            pass
+
+
+# --- 12. STT backend unavailable ---
+
+def test_stt_backend_unavailable_marks_status_without_crash(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "audio_test.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("audio.opus", b"OggS_dummy_audio")
+
+    msg = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="12", media_ref="audio.opus", text="Nota vocale", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    bundle = MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc_audio", bundles=(bundle,))
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    # stt_backend = None (non disponibile)
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(doc, stt_backend=None)
+
+    assert len(enriched_doc.bundles) == 1
+    enb = enriched_doc.bundles[0]
+    assert enb.audio_transcription is not None
+    assert enb.audio_transcription.status == TranscriptionStatus.FAILED
+    assert "non configurato" in enb.audio_transcription.error_message
+
+

+# --- 15. Real processing with test fixtures ---
+
+class DummySttEngine:
+    def transcribe(self, asset: ResolvedMediaAsset) -> AudioTranscriptionResult:
+        return AudioTranscriptionResult(
+            message_id=asset.message_id,
+            source_name=asset.source_name,
+            source_record_id=asset.source_record_id,
+            status=TranscriptionStatus.SUCCESS,
+            full_transcript="Trascrizione vocale forense: incontro confermato alle 15:00.",
+            detected_language="it",
+            engine="faster-whisper",
+            model_name="tiny",
+            provenance_asset=asset,
+        )
+
+
+class DummyOcrEngine:
+    def extract_text(self, asset: ResolvedMediaAsset) -> ImageOcrResult:
+        return ImageOcrResult(
+            message_id=asset.message_id,
+            source_name=asset.source_name,
+            source_record_id=asset.source_record_id,
+            status=OcrStatus.SUCCESS,
+            full_text="DDT N. 1024 - DESTINAZIONE MAGAZZINO PORTUALE",
+            engine="tesseract",
+            provenance_asset=asset,
+        )
+
+
+def test_real_processing_with_test_fixtures(tmp_path: Path):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "evidence_fixture.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("audio_note.opus", b"OggS_real_test_audio")
+        zf.writestr("ddt_scan.png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n_real_test_image")
+
+    msg_audio = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="101", media_ref="audio_note.opus", text="Nota audio", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    msg_img = _make_dummy_message(msg_id="102", media_ref="ddt_scan.png", text="Scansione ddt", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+
+    doc_orig = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_forensic_evidence",
+        bundles=(
+            MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_audio),
+            MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_img),
+        ),
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc_orig,
+        stt_backend=DummySttEngine(),
+        ocr_backend=DummyOcrEngine(),
+    )
+
+    # 1. Verifica immutabilità documento originale
+    assert doc_orig.bundles[0].audio_transcription is None
+    assert doc_orig.bundles[1].image_ocr is None
+
+    # 2. Verifica arricchimento nuovo documento
+    enb_audio = enriched_doc.bundles[0]
+    assert enb_audio.audio_transcription is not None
+    assert enb_audio.audio_transcription.status == TranscriptionStatus.SUCCESS
+    assert "incontro confermato" in enb_audio.audio_transcription.full_transcript
+
+    enb_img = enriched_doc.bundles[1]
+    assert enb_img.image_ocr is not None
+    assert enb_img.image_ocr.status == OcrStatus.SUCCESS
+    assert "DDT N. 1024" in enb_img.image_ocr.full_text
+
+    # 3. Verifica text_evidence_sections generate
+    sec_types = {sec.source_type for sec in enriched_doc.all_evidence_sections}
+    assert EvidenceSourceType.STT_TRANSCRIPTION in sec_types
+    assert EvidenceSourceType.OCR_TEXT in sec_types
+    assert EvidenceSourceType.ORIGINAL_TEXT in sec_types
+
+
+# --- 13. Test Blocker 4: Traversal member in zip rejected even when searched via basename ---
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "malicious_member_name",
+    [
+        "folder/../evil.txt",
+        "folder\\..\\evil.txt",
+        "../evil.txt",
+        "..\\evil.txt",
+        "/etc/passwd",
+        "C:\\evil.txt",
+        "\\\\server\\share\\evil.txt",
+    ],
+)
+def test_zip_traversal_in_zip_archive_rejected_even_when_searched_via_basename(
+    tmp_path: Path, malicious_member_name: str
+):
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "malicious_member.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr(malicious_member_name, b"evil content")
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
+        # Ricerca sia con nome esatto sia con basename 'evil.txt' o 'passwd'
+        with pytest.raises(MediaArchiveSecurityError, match="non consentito"):
+            validate_zip_member_security(zf, "evil.txt")
+

+# --- 14. Test Blocker 5: MediaAssetRegistry Document and Session Isolation ---
+
+def test_media_asset_registry_document_isolation(tmp_path: Path):
+    from multimodal.asset_access import MediaAssetRegistry
+
+    reg_doc_a = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_a")
+    reg_doc_b = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_b")
+
+    file_a = tmp_path / "archive_a.zip"
+    file_a.write_bytes(b"DATA_A")
+    file_b = tmp_path / "archive_b.zip"
+    file_b.write_bytes(b"DATA_B")
+
+    reg_doc_a.register_source("source_a", file_a)
+    reg_doc_b.register_source("source_b", file_b)
+
+    # Verifica isolamento: reg_doc_a non vede source_b e viceversa
+    assert reg_doc_a.get_source("source_a") == file_a
+    assert reg_doc_a.get_source("source_b") is None
+
+    assert reg_doc_b.get_source("source_b") == file_b
+    assert reg_doc_b.get_source("source_a") is None
+
+
+def test_media_asset_registry_cleanup_and_session_isolation(tmp_path: Path):
+    from multimodal.asset_access import MediaAssetRegistry
+
+    reg_1 = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_1")
+    reg_2 = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_2")
+
+    path_1 = reg_1.register_bytes("src_1", b"TEST_DATA_1", filename="media_1.zip")
+    path_2 = reg_2.register_bytes("src_2", b"TEST_DATA_2", filename="media_2.zip")
+
+    assert path_1.exists()
+    assert path_2.exists()
+
+    # Cleanup di reg_1 non deve toccare i file gestiti di reg_2
+    reg_1.clear()
+    assert reg_1.get_source("src_1") is None
+
+    # reg_2 rimane intatto e leggibile
+    assert reg_2.get_source("src_2") == path_2
+    assert path_2.exists()
+
+    # Cleanup finale di reg_2
+    reg_2.clear()
+    assert reg_2.get_source("src_2") is None
+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_media_processing_lifecycle.py
+----------------------------------------------
+Test di regressione e audit forense per BLOCKER 5:
+- OCR riuscito -> COMPLETE.
+- OCR non disponibile -> FAILED (nessuna falsa conferma).
+- Un media riuscito e uno fallito -> PARTIAL.
+- Retry dopo disponibilità backend -> COMPLETE.
+- Retry non duplica evidenze.
+- Documento privo di media -> NOT_PROCESSED.
+- Errore ZIP -> nessun successo fittizio.
+- Isolamento sessione A/B.
+- Stato preservato dopo cambio conversazione (A -> B -> A).
+- Nessun Fake Vision su dati reali.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+import tempfile
+import zipfile
+import pytest
+
+from ai.models import ConversationEvidenceDocument
+from multimodal.asset_access import (
+    MediaAssetAccessService,
+    MediaAssetRegistry,
+    MediaProcessingStatus,
+)
+from multimodal.evidence import EvidenceSourceType, MessageEvidenceBundle
+from multimodal.models import (
+    AudioTranscriptionResult,
+    MediaKind,
+    OcrStatus,
+    TranscriptionStatus,
+    ImageOcrResult,
+    ResolvedMediaAsset,
+)
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    RawRecord,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+    ValidationResult,
+)
+from ui import state
+from unified.models import UnifiedMessage
+
+
+def _make_test_message(
+    msg_id: str,
+    media_ref: str | None = None,
+    text: str = "Test",
+    msg_type: CanonicalMessageType = CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+) -> UnifiedMessage:
+    raw = RawRecord(
+        source_name="whatsapp",
+        source_path="/fake/chat.txt",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        record_type="message",
+        raw_fields={"text": text},
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+        metadata={},
+    )
+    val = ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=())
+    ts = NormalizedTimestamp(status=TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT)
+    norm = NormalizedRecord(
+        raw_record=raw,
+        validation_result=val,
+        source_name="whatsapp",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        record_type="message",
+        timestamp=ts,
+        message_type=msg_type,
+        text_content=text,
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+    )
+    return UnifiedMessage(
+        message_id=f"msg::{msg_id}",
+        source_name="whatsapp",
+        source_record_id=msg_id,
+        source_path="/fake/chat.txt",
+        record_type="message",
+        timestamp=ts,
+        message_type=msg_type,
+        text_content=text,
+        media_reference=media_ref,
+        provenance_record=norm,
+    )
+
+
+class MockSuccessOcrEngine:
+    def extract_text(self, asset: ResolvedMediaAsset) -> ImageOcrResult:
+        return ImageOcrResult(
+            message_id=asset.message_id,
+            source_name=asset.source_name,
+            source_record_id=asset.source_record_id,
+            status=OcrStatus.SUCCESS,
+            full_text="Testo estratto con successo dall'immagine.",
+            engine="tesseract",
+            provenance_asset=asset,
+        )
+
+
+class MockFailingOcrEngine:
+    def extract_text(self, asset: ResolvedMediaAsset) -> ImageOcrResult:
+        return ImageOcrResult(
+            message_id=asset.message_id,
+            source_name=asset.source_name,
+            source_record_id=asset.source_record_id,
+            status=OcrStatus.FAILED,
+            full_text="",
+            engine="tesseract",
+            provenance_asset=asset,
+            error_message="Errore simulato OCR backend",
+        )
+
+
+class MockSuccessSttEngine:
+    def transcribe(self, asset: ResolvedMediaAsset) -> AudioTranscriptionResult:
+        return AudioTranscriptionResult(
+            message_id=asset.message_id,
+            source_name=asset.source_name,
+            source_record_id=asset.source_record_id,
+            status=TranscriptionStatus.SUCCESS,
+            full_transcript="Audio vocale trascritto correttamente.",
+            detected_language="it",
+            engine="faster-whisper",
+            model_name="tiny",
+            provenance_asset=asset,
+        )
+
+
+def test_ocr_success_leads_to_complete_status(tmp_path: Path):
+    """OCR riuscito -> COMPLETE e multimodal_processed=True."""
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_img.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("photo.png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n_valid_png_bytes")
+
+    msg = _make_test_message("1", media_ref="photo.png", text="Foto allegata", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_ocr_complete",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg),),
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc,
+        ocr_backend=MockSuccessOcrEngine(),
+    )
+
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_status") == MediaProcessingStatus.COMPLETE.value
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_processed") is True
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[0].image_ocr is not None
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[0].image_ocr.status == OcrStatus.SUCCESS
+    # Sezione evidenza aggiunta
+    assert any(sec.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.OCR_TEXT for sec in enriched_doc.all_evidence_sections)
+
+
+def test_ocr_unavailable_leads_to_failed_status(tmp_path: Path):
+    """OCR non disponibile -> FAILED, nessuna evidenza fittizia, multimodal_processed=False."""
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_img2.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("photo2.png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n_valid_png_bytes")
+
+    msg = _make_test_message("2", media_ref="photo2.png", text="Foto allegata", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_ocr_failed",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg),),
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc,
+        ocr_backend=None,  # Backend non disponibile
+    )
+
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_status") == MediaProcessingStatus.FAILED.value
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_processed") is False
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[0].image_ocr is not None
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[0].image_ocr.status == OcrStatus.FAILED
+    assert not any(sec.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.OCR_TEXT for sec in enriched_doc.all_evidence_sections)
+
+
+def test_mixed_media_one_success_one_fail_leads_to_partial_status(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Un media riuscito e uno fallito -> PARTIAL e multimodal_processed=False."""
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_mixed.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("photo_ok.png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n_ok")
+        zf.writestr("audio_fail.opus", b"OggS_bad_audio")
+
+    msg_img = _make_test_message("10", media_ref="photo_ok.png", text="Foto", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    msg_aud = _make_test_message("11", media_ref="audio_fail.opus", text="Audio", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_mixed",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_img), MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_aud)),
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    # OCR presente (successo), STT assente (fallimento)
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc,
+        ocr_backend=MockSuccessOcrEngine(),
+        stt_backend=None,
+    )
+
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_status") == MediaProcessingStatus.PARTIAL.value
+    assert enriched_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_processed") is False
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[0].image_ocr.status == OcrStatus.SUCCESS
+    assert enriched_doc.bundles[1].audio_transcription.status == TranscriptionStatus.FAILED
+
+
+def test_retry_after_backend_available_reaches_complete_without_duplication(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Retry dopo disponibilità backend raggiunge COMPLETE e non duplica evidenze."""
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "chat_retry.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("photo.png", b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n_ok")
+        zf.writestr("voice.opus", b"OggS_ok")
+
+    msg_img = _make_test_message("20", media_ref="photo.png", text="Foto", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    msg_aud = _make_test_message("21", media_ref="voice.opus", text="Voce", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.AUDIO)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_retry",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_img), MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_aud)),
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+
+    # 1. Primo tentativo: solo OCR disponibile -> PARTIAL
+    doc_v1 = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc,
+        ocr_backend=MockSuccessOcrEngine(),
+        stt_backend=None,
+    )
+    assert doc_v1.metadata.get("multimodal_status") == MediaProcessingStatus.PARTIAL.value
+    sections_v1_count = len(doc_v1.all_evidence_sections)
+    ocr_sections_v1 = [s for s in doc_v1.all_evidence_sections if s.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.OCR_TEXT]
+    assert len(ocr_sections_v1) == 1
+
+    # 2. Secondo tentativo (Retry): STT ora disponibile
+    doc_v2 = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc_v1,
+        ocr_backend=MockSuccessOcrEngine(),
+        stt_backend=MockSuccessSttEngine(),
+    )
+    assert doc_v2.metadata.get("multimodal_status") == MediaProcessingStatus.COMPLETE.value
+    assert doc_v2.metadata.get("multimodal_processed") is True
+
+    # Verifica non duplicazione delle sezioni OCR
+    ocr_sections_v2 = [s for s in doc_v2.all_evidence_sections if s.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.OCR_TEXT]
+    stt_sections_v2 = [s for s in doc_v2.all_evidence_sections if s.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.STT_TRANSCRIPTION]
+    assert len(ocr_sections_v2) == 1, "Le sezioni OCR non devono essere duplicate durante il retry"
+    assert len(stt_sections_v2) == 1
+
+
+def test_document_without_media_not_processed():
+    """Documento privo di media -> NOT_PROCESSED, nessun successo fittizio."""

+    assert res_doc.metadata.get("multimodal_processed") is False
+

+def test_session_isolation_and_conversation_switching():
+    """Isolamento sessioni A/B e stato preservato al cambio conversazione A -> B -> A."""
+    session_state: dict = {}
+    state.init_session_state(session_state)
+
+    msg_a = _make_test_message("a1", media_ref="doc_a_pic.png", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    msg_b = _make_test_message("b1", media_ref="doc_b_pic.png", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+
+    doc_a = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="chat_alpha",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_a),),
+        metadata={"multimodal_status": "COMPLETE", "multimodal_processed": True},
+    )
+    doc_b = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="chat_beta",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg_b),),
+        metadata={"multimodal_status": "NOT_PROCESSED", "multimodal_processed": False},
+    )
+
+    session_state["available_documents"] = {doc_a.document_id: doc_a, doc_b.document_id: doc_b}
+    session_state["selected_document_id"] = doc_a.document_id
+    session_state["conversation_document"] = doc_a
+
+    # Conversazione A ha stato COMPLETE
+    assert state.get_conversation_document(session_state).metadata["multimodal_status"] == "COMPLETE"
+
+    # Passaggio a Conversazione B
+    state.select_conversation(doc_b.document_id, state=session_state)
+    assert state.get_conversation_document(session_state).metadata["multimodal_status"] == "NOT_PROCESSED"
+
+    # Ritorno a Conversazione A: stato preservato intatto
+    state.select_conversation(doc_a.document_id, state=session_state)
+    assert state.get_conversation_document(session_state).metadata["multimodal_status"] == "COMPLETE"
+
+
+def test_no_fake_vision_on_real_data(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Nessun Fake Vision eseguito su dati reali se vision_backend non è configurato."""
+    zip_path = tmp_path / "real_chat.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("scan.jpg", b"\xff\xd8\xff\xe0JFIF_real_photo")
+
+    msg = _make_test_message("50", media_ref="scan.jpg", text="Foto reale", msg_type=CanonicalMessageType.IMAGE)
+    doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(
+        document_id="doc_real_no_vision",
+        bundles=(MessageEvidenceBundle(message=msg),),
+        metadata={"data_classification": "FORENSIC_REAL"},
+    )
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=zip_path)
+    res_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=doc,
+        ocr_backend=MockSuccessOcrEngine(),
+        vision_backend=None,  # Vision assente
+    )
+
+    # Verifica che image_vision rimanga rigorosamente None
+    assert res_doc.bundles[0].image_vision is None
+    assert not any(sec.source_type == EvidenceSourceType.VISION_DESCRIPTION for sec in res_doc.all_evidence_sections)

+"""
+tests/unit/test_media_registry_session_isolation.py
+----------------------------------------------------
+Test di regressione e audit forense per BLOCKER 1:
+- Session A registra un file, Session B non lo vede.
+- Session A e B importano documenti con document_id identico: nessuna contaminazione.
+- Reset A cancella soltanto risorse di A.
+- Reset B non modifica le risorse di A.
+- File temporanei realmente eliminati dopo cleanup.
+- Nessun accesso tramite registri globali o class methods (_fallback_default e _HybridRegistryMethod rimossi).
+- Multi-conversation A -> B -> A nella stessa sessione continua a funzionare.
+- L'ingestion ZIP conserva l'accesso autorizzato ai media dopo il cleanup dello staging.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import tempfile
+import zipfile
+import pytest
+
+from multimodal.asset_access import (
+    MediaAssetRegistry,
+    MediaAssetAccessService,
+    MediaSourceUnavailableError,
+)
+from ui import state
+from ui.ingestion import ingest_file_payload
+from ui.models import IngestionRequest, SourceFormat
+
+
+def test_session_a_registers_file_session_b_cannot_see_it(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Session A registra un file, Session B non lo vede."""
+    file_a = tmp_path / "chat_a.zip"
+    file_a.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+
+    reg_a = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_123")
+    reg_b = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_123")
+
+    reg_a.register_archive("doc_123", file_a)
+
+    assert reg_a.get_archive("doc_123") == file_a
+    assert reg_b.get_archive("doc_123") is None
+
+
+def test_identical_document_ids_in_separate_sessions_no_contamination(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Session A e B importano documenti con document_id identico: nessuna contaminazione."""
+    file_a = tmp_path / "archive_session_a.zip"
+    file_b = tmp_path / "archive_session_b.zip"
+    file_a.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+    file_b.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+
+    session_state_a: dict = {}
+    session_state_b: dict = {}
+
+    state.init_session_state(session_state_a)
+    state.init_session_state(session_state_b)
+
+    reg_a = state.get_media_asset_registry("shared_doc_id", state=session_state_a)
+    reg_b = state.get_media_asset_registry("shared_doc_id", state=session_state_b)
+
+    reg_a.register_archive("shared_doc_id", file_a)
+    reg_b.register_archive("shared_doc_id", file_b)
+
+    assert reg_a.get_archive("shared_doc_id") == file_a
+    assert reg_b.get_archive("shared_doc_id") == file_b
+    assert reg_a.get_archive("shared_doc_id") != reg_b.get_archive("shared_doc_id")
+
+
+def test_reset_a_only_clears_a_resources(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Reset A cancella soltanto le risorse di A; Reset B non modifica le risorse di A."""
+    file_a = tmp_path / "file_a.zip"
+    file_b = tmp_path / "file_b.zip"
+    file_a.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+    file_b.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+
+    session_a: dict = {}
+    session_b: dict = {}
+    state.init_session_state(session_a)
+    state.init_session_state(session_b)
+
+    reg_a = state.get_media_asset_registry("doc_a", state=session_a)
+    reg_b = state.get_media_asset_registry("doc_b", state=session_b)
+
+    reg_a.register_archive("doc_a", file_a)
+    reg_b.register_archive("doc_b", file_b)
+
+    # Reset A
+    state.reset_dataset(state=session_a)
+
+    assert reg_a.get_archive("doc_a") is None
+    # Risorse di B rimangono intatte
+    assert reg_b.get_archive("doc_b") == file_b
+
+    # Reset B
+    state.reset_dataset(state=session_b)
+    assert reg_b.get_archive("doc_b") is None
+
+
+def test_temp_files_actually_deleted_after_cleanup():
+    """File temporanei e cartelle realmente eliminati dopo cleanup del registry."""
+    reg = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_cleanup")
+    managed_dir = reg.managed_temp_dir
+    assert managed_dir.exists()
+
+    # Crea file gestito nel registry
+    temp_file = managed_dir / "test_artifact.bin"
+    temp_file.write_bytes(b"temporary forensic payload")
+    assert temp_file.exists()
+
+    # Esegui cleanup
+    reg.clear()
+
+    # Verifica rimozione fisica
+    assert not temp_file.exists()
+    assert not managed_dir.exists()
+
+
+def test_no_global_registry_or_class_methods():
+    """Nessun accesso consentito tramite registri globali o class methods su MediaAssetRegistry."""
+    # Verifica che _fallback_default non esista
+    assert not hasattr(MediaAssetRegistry, "_fallback_default")
+    assert not hasattr(MediaAssetRegistry, "_HybridRegistryMethod")
+
+    # Invocazione di metodi come class method deve fallire o richiedere istanza
+    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
+        MediaAssetRegistry.register_archive("doc_x", Path("some/path"))  # type: ignore
+
+    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
+        MediaAssetRegistry.get_archive("doc_x")  # type: ignore
+
+
+def test_multi_conversation_a_to_b_to_a_in_same_session(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Multi-conversation A -> B -> A nella stessa sessione continua a funzionare con isolamento."""
+    session: dict = {}
+    state.init_session_state(session)
+
+    file_a = tmp_path / "chat_alpha.zip"
+    file_b = tmp_path / "chat_beta.zip"
+    file_a.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+    file_b.write_bytes(b"PK\x05\x06" + b"\x00" * 18)
+
+    reg_a = state.get_media_asset_registry("chat_alpha", state=session)
+    reg_b = state.get_media_asset_registry("chat_beta", state=session)
+
+    reg_a.register_archive("chat_alpha", file_a)
+    reg_b.register_archive("chat_beta", file_b)
+
+    # Selezione Alpha -> Beta -> Alpha
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("chat_alpha", state=session).get_archive("chat_alpha") == file_a
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("chat_beta", state=session).get_archive("chat_beta") == file_b
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("chat_alpha", state=session).get_archive("chat_alpha") == file_a
+
+
+def test_zip_ingestion_preserves_authorized_media_access_after_staging_cleanup(tmp_path: Path):
+    """L'ingestion ZIP conserva l'accesso autorizzato ai media dopo il cleanup dello staging."""
+    zip_bytes_io = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".zip", delete=False)
+    zip_path = Path(zip_bytes_io.name)
+    zip_bytes_io.close()
+
+    try:
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
+            zf.writestr("_chat.txt", "10/05/2024, 10:00 - Alice: Guarda questa foto: photo.jpg\n".encode("utf-8"))
+            zf.writestr("photo.jpg", b"\xff\xd8\xff\xe0\x00\x10JFIF\x00\x01\x01_test_jpg")
+
+        payload = zip_path.read_bytes()
+    finally:
+        if zip_path.exists():
+            zip_path.unlink()
+
+    session_reg = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="session_reg_test")
+    res = ingest_file_payload(
+        IngestionRequest(
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+            filename="chat_export.zip",
+            file_bytes=payload,
+        ),
+        media_registry=session_reg,
+    )
+
+    doc = next(iter(res.documents.values()))
+    archive_path = session_reg.get_archive(doc.document_id)
+    assert archive_path is not None
+    assert archive_path.exists()
+
+    # Il service media con il registry esplicito accede al membro
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(archive_path=archive_path, registry=session_reg)
+    with service.materialize_member("photo.jpg") as photo_path:
+        assert photo_path.exists()
+        assert b"JFIF" in photo_path.read_bytes()
+
+    # Pulizia
+    session_reg.clear()
+    assert not archive_path.exists()
+
+
+# ==============================================================================
+# BLOCKER 2 — TEST OBBLIGATORI (DATASET REPLACEMENT TRANSACTIONAL LIFECYCLE)
+# ==============================================================================
+
+def test_dataset_replacement_transactional_lifecycle_and_cleanup(tmp_path: Path):
+    """
+    Verifica il ciclo di vita transazionale della sostituzione del dataset:
+    - Import dataset A con media temporanei.
+    - Fallimento import B: comportamento precedente conservato coerentemente (A resta intatto).
+    - Import dataset B nella stessa sessione: B diventa attivo, media di A eliminati, media di B conservati.
+    - Reset ripetuto senza eccezioni.
+    """
+    from ui.models import IngestionResult, IngestionSummary, IngestionStatus, DatasetMode
+    from ai.models import ConversationEvidenceDocument, MessageEvidenceBundle
+
+    session: dict = {}
+    state.init_session_state(session)
+
+    # 1. Dataset A con asset temporaneo gestito
+    doc_a = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc_a", bundles=(), source_name="src_a")
+    reg_a = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_a")
+    temp_file_a = reg_a.register_bytes("source_a", b"PAYLOAD_A", filename="archive_a.zip")
+    assert temp_file_a.exists()
+
+    summary_a = IngestionSummary(
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        original_filename="a.zip",
+        sha256="hash_a",
+        file_size_bytes=100,
+        raw_record_count=1,
+        validation_issue_count=0,
+        normalized_record_count=1,
+        unified_message_count=1,
+        conversation_count=1,
+        auxiliary_record_count=0,
+    )
+    res_a = IngestionResult(
+        summary=summary_a,
+        documents={"doc_a": doc_a},
+        media_registry=reg_a,
+    )
+
+    state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_a, state=session)
+
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) == "doc_a"
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("doc_a", state=session) is reg_a
+    assert temp_file_a.exists()
+
+    # 2. Fallimento import B: fallimento transazionale, dataset A rimane intatto
+    summary_b_fail = IngestionSummary(
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        original_filename="b_corrupt.zip",
+        sha256="hash_b_fail",
+        file_size_bytes=50,
+        raw_record_count=0,
+        validation_issue_count=1,
+        normalized_record_count=0,
+        unified_message_count=0,
+        conversation_count=0,
+        auxiliary_record_count=0,
+        status=IngestionStatus.FAILED,
+    )
+    res_b_fail = IngestionResult(
+        summary=summary_b_fail,
+        documents={},
+        error_stage="parsing",
+        error_message="Corrupted archive structure",
+    )
+
+    state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_b_fail, state=session)
+
+    # Dataset A e le sue risorse devono essere ANCORA attive e integre
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) == "doc_a"
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("doc_a", state=session) is reg_a
+    assert temp_file_a.exists()
+    assert state.get_error_message(state=session) == "Corrupted archive structure"
+
+    # 3. Import dataset B con successo: sostituzione transazionale
+    doc_b = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc_b", bundles=(), source_name="src_b")
+    reg_b = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="doc_b")
+    temp_file_b = reg_b.register_bytes("source_b", b"PAYLOAD_B", filename="archive_b.zip")
+    assert temp_file_b.exists()
+
+    summary_b = IngestionSummary(
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        original_filename="b.zip",
+        sha256="hash_b",
+        file_size_bytes=200,
+        raw_record_count=2,
+        validation_issue_count=0,
+        normalized_record_count=2,
+        unified_message_count=2,
+        conversation_count=1,
+        auxiliary_record_count=0,
+    )
+    res_b = IngestionResult(
+        summary=summary_b,
+        documents={"doc_b": doc_b},
+        media_registry=reg_b,
+    )
+
+    state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_b, state=session)
+
+    # Dataset B deve essere attivo
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) == "doc_b"
+    assert state.get_media_asset_registry("doc_b", state=session) is reg_b
+    assert temp_file_b.exists()
+
+    # Le risorse di A devono essere state eliminate fisicamente
+    assert not temp_file_a.exists()
+    # Il registry A non deve più essere raggiungibile dallo stato di sessione
+    session_regs = session.get(state.KEY_MEDIA_REGISTRIES_BY_DOCUMENT, {})
+    assert "doc_a" not in session_regs
+    assert "doc_b" in session_regs
+    assert reg_a.get_source("source_a") is None
+
+    # 4. Multi-conversation nello stesso dataset: A -> B -> A conserva le risorse
+    doc_multi_1 = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="conv_1", bundles=(), source_name="src_m")
+    doc_multi_2 = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="conv_2", bundles=(), source_name="src_m")
+    reg_m = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id="conv_shared")
+    temp_m = reg_m.register_bytes("source_m", b"PAYLOAD_MULTI", filename="archive_m.zip")
+
+    summary_m = IngestionSummary(
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        original_filename="multi.zip",
+        sha256="hash_m",
+        file_size_bytes=300,
+        raw_record_count=4,
+        validation_issue_count=0,
+        normalized_record_count=4,
+        unified_message_count=4,
+        conversation_count=2,
+        auxiliary_record_count=0,
+    )
+    res_m = IngestionResult(
+        summary=summary_m,
+        documents={"conv_1": doc_multi_1, "conv_2": doc_multi_2},
+        media_registry=reg_m,
+    )
+
+    state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_m, state=session)
+    assert not temp_file_b.exists()  # Il vecchio dataset B è stato pulito
+    assert temp_m.exists()
+
+    # Passa a conv_2 e poi torna a conv_1
+    state.select_conversation("conv_2", state=session)
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) == "conv_2"
+    assert temp_m.exists()
+
+    state.select_conversation("conv_1", state=session)
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) == "conv_1"
+    assert temp_m.exists()
+
+    # 5. Reset ripetuto senza eccezioni
+    state.reset_dataset(state=session)
+    assert not temp_m.exists()
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) is None
+
+    # Secondo reset idempotente
+    state.reset_dataset(state=session)
+    assert state.get_selected_document_id(state=session) is None
+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_model_resolution_audit.py
+-----------------------------------------
+Test esaustivi per il Model Resolution e Model Identity Lifecycle:
+- Criterio 16, 20: DeepSeek non viene instradato a Qwen resolver.
+- Criterio 17, 21: Qwen3 non viene accettato come Qwen2.5.
+- Criterio 18, 22: Model specs separate per Qwen, Llama, DeepSeek.
+- Criterio 19, 23, 42: Model equivalence (canonical ID, model key, instance ID, response model).
+- Criterio 20, 24, 43: JIT fallback non finge LOADED su 404 (status: jit_unverified).
+- Criterio 21, 25, 44: is_model_loaded propaga errori reali (protocollo, HTTP, timeout, network).
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request

+from unittest import mock

+from ai.backend import (
+    AiBackendProtocolError,
+    AiBackendRequestError,
+    AiBackendTimeoutError,
+    AiModelAmbiguousError,
+    AiModelMismatchError,
+    AiModelNotInstalledError,
+    LmStudioUnavailableError,
+)

+    LmStudioClient,

+    _normalize_model_item,

+)

+
+def _make_meta(
+    key: str,
+    display_name: str | None = None,
+    quant: str | None = "Q4_K_M",
+    loaded_instances: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
+) -> LocalModelMetadata:
+    return LocalModelMetadata(
+        key=key,
+        display_name=display_name or key,
+        quantization=quant,
+        loaded_instances=tuple(loaded_instances or []),
+    )
+
+
+def test_model_resolution_strict_family_isolation():
+    """Verifica che una lista con Qwen2.5, Qwen3, DeepSeek e Llama risolva esclusivamente la famiglia richiesta."""
+    installed = [
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF", "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q4_K_M", "Q4_K_M"),
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen3-7B-Instruct-GGUF", "Qwen3-7B-Instruct-Q4_K_M", "Q4_K_M"),
+        _make_meta("deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B-GGUF", "DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B-Q4_K_M", "Q4_K_M"),
+        _make_meta("meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct-GGUF", "Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct-Q4_K_M", "Q4_K_M"),
+    ]
+
+    # 1. Richiesta Qwen2.5 -> solo Qwen2.5
+    resolved_qwen = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(installed, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert "qwen2.5" in resolved_qwen.model_key.lower()
+    assert "deepseek" not in resolved_qwen.model_key.lower()
+    assert "qwen3" not in resolved_qwen.model_key.lower()
+    assert resolved_qwen.canonical_spec.family == "QWEN2.5"
+
+    # 2. Richiesta DeepSeek -> solo DeepSeek (non cross-routed a Qwen)
+    resolved_deepseek = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(installed, "deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b")
+    assert "deepseek" in resolved_deepseek.model_key.lower()
+    assert resolved_deepseek.canonical_spec.family == "DEEPSEEK"
+
+    # 3. Richiesta Llama -> solo Llama 3.1
+    resolved_llama = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(installed, "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct")
+    assert "llama-3.1" in resolved_llama.model_key.lower()
+    assert resolved_llama.canonical_spec.family == "LLAMA"
+
+
+def test_qwen3_never_resolved_as_qwen25():
+    """Se è installato solo Qwen3, richiedere Qwen2.5 DEVE sollevare AiModelNotInstalledError."""
+    installed = [
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen3-7B-Instruct-GGUF", "Qwen3-7B-Instruct", "Q4_K_M"),
+    ]
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(installed, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+
+def test_deepseek_never_resolved_as_qwen25():
+    """Se è installato solo DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, richiedere Qwen2.5 DEVE sollevare AiModelNotInstalledError."""
+    installed = [
+        _make_meta("deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B-GGUF", "DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B", "Q4_K_M"),

+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(installed, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")

+def test_preferred_quantization_q4_k_m():
+    """Se sono presenti quantizzazioni multiple, Q4_K_M viene preferita."""
+    installed = [
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q2_k", "Qwen2.5 7B Q2_K", "Q2_K"),
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q4_k_m", "Qwen2.5 7B Q4_K_M", "Q4_K_M"),
+        _make_meta("lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q8_0", "Qwen2.5 7B Q8_0", "Q8_0"),

+    assert "q4_k_m" in resolved.model_key.lower()

+def test_ambiguity_detection_without_preferred_quant():

+def test_model_equivalence_and_mismatch():
+    """Verifica l'equivalenza autorizzata tra model key, instance id e canonical id, e il rigetto di modelli estranei."""
+    # Equivalenza autorizzata per Qwen2.5
+    assert is_equivalent_operational_model(
+        target_model="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        response_model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct-abc123",
+    ) is True
+
+    # Rifiuto modello di altra famiglia (Llama spacciato per Qwen)
+    assert is_equivalent_operational_model(
+        target_model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        response_model="meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct",
+    ) is False
+
+    # Rifiuto DeepSeek spacciato per Qwen2.5
+    assert is_equivalent_operational_model(
+        target_model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        response_model="deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b",
+    ) is False
+
+    # ResolvedOperationalModel instance method
+    from ai.lmstudio import OPERATIONAL_SPECS
+    resolved = ResolvedOperationalModel(
+        canonical_spec=OPERATIONAL_SPECS["qwen2.5-7b-instruct"],
+        requested_canonical_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        model_key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        is_loaded=True,
+        instance_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct-abc123",
+        api_identifier="qwen2.5-7b-instruct-abc123",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+    )
+    assert resolved.is_equivalent_response_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct-abc123") is True
+    assert resolved.is_equivalent_response_model("meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct") is False
+

+def test_jit_fallback_404_returns_jit_unverified_and_not_loaded(monkeypatch):
+    """Su endpoint load 404, il client restituisce jit_unverified e NON dichiara il modello loaded."""
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/api/v1/models/load" in url:
+            raise urllib.error.HTTPError(url, 404, "Not Found", {}, io.BytesIO(b"Not Found"))
+        if "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            class NativeResp:
+                def __init__(self):
+                    self.status = 200
+                def read(self):
+                    return json.dumps({
+                        "data": [{"key": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", "loaded_instances": []}]
+                    }).encode("utf-8")
+                def __enter__(self):
+                    return self
+                def __exit__(self, *args):
+                    pass
+            return NativeResp()
+        raise ValueError(f"URL non atteso: {url}")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    load_res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    assert load_res["status"] == "jit_unverified"
+    assert load_res["load_mode"] == "jit_unverified"
+    assert client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct") is False
+
+
+def test_is_model_loaded_propagates_real_errors(monkeypatch):
+    """is_model_loaded non ingoia errori reali: HTTP 500, timeout, protocollo malformato, indisponibilità."""
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+
+    # 1. HTTP 500
+    def mock_http_500(req, timeout):
+        raise urllib.error.HTTPError(req.full_url, 500, "Internal Server Error", {}, io.BytesIO(b"error"))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_http_500)
+    with pytest.raises(AiBackendRequestError):
+        client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    # 2. Malformed JSON
+    def mock_malformed_json(req, timeout):
+        class MalformedResp:
+            def __init__(self):
+                self.status = 200
+            def read(self):
+                return b"THIS IS NOT JSON"
+            def __enter__(self):
+                return self
+            def __exit__(self, *args):
+                pass
+        return MalformedResp()
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_malformed_json)
+    with pytest.raises(AiBackendProtocolError):
+        client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    # 3. Server offline (Connection Refused)
+    import socket
+    def mock_conn_refused(req, timeout):
+        raise urllib.error.URLError(ConnectionRefusedError("Connection refused"))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_conn_refused)
+    with pytest.raises(LmStudioUnavailableError):
+        client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+    # 4. Timeout
+    def mock_timeout(req, timeout):
+        raise urllib.error.URLError(socket.timeout("Read timed out"))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_timeout)
+    with pytest.raises(AiBackendTimeoutError):
+        client.is_model_loaded("qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+
+
+# ==============================================================================
+# TEST OBBLIGATORI AUDIT: CONTEXT LENGTH PROVENANCE E VERIFICA OPERATIVA
+# ==============================================================================
+
+def test_context_resolution_loaded_instance_at_4096():
+    """1. Istanza caricata a 4096: context_length DEVE essere 4096 e NON 8192."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        loaded_instances=({"id": "inst-4096", "context_length": 4096},),
+        loaded_instance_context_length=4096,
+        max_context_length=32768,
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.configured_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.context_evidence_source == "LOADED_INSTANCE_METADATA"
+    assert resolved.server_confirmed_context_length == 4096
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_loaded_instance_at_8192():
+    """2. Istanza caricata a 8192: context_length confermato a 8192."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        loaded_instances=({"id": "inst-8192", "context_length": 8192},),
+        loaded_instance_context_length=8192,
+        max_context_length=32768,
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.server_confirmed_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.context_evidence_source == "LOADED_INSTANCE_METADATA"
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_max_32768_with_loaded_4096():
+    """3. Max context 32768 con istanza caricata a 4096: prevale l'istanza effettiva (4096), non max_context né 8192."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        max_context_length=32768,
+        loaded_instance_context_length=4096,
+        loaded_instances=({"id": "inst-qwen", "context_length": 4096},),
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.model_max_context_length == 32768
+    assert resolved.context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.context_length != 8192
+    assert resolved.context_length != 32768
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_instance_context_absent():
+    """4. Context dell'istanza assente: context_length è None (UNKNOWN) e NON 8192."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        loaded_instances=({"id": "inst-qwen"},),  # Nessun context_length nell'istanza
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.is_loaded is True
+    assert resolved.context_length is None
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length is None
+    assert resolved.context_evidence_source == "UNKNOWN"
+    assert resolved.server_confirmed_context_length is None
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_installed_but_unloaded():
+    """5. Modello installato ma non caricato: context_length è None, load_mode è 'unloaded'."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        loaded_instances=(),
+        max_context_length=32768,
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.is_loaded is False
+    assert resolved.context_length is None
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length is None
+    assert resolved.context_evidence_source == "NOT_LOADED"
+    assert resolved.model_max_context_length == 32768
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_configured_8192_backend_4096():
+    """6. Valore di configurazione 8192 ma backend a 4096: separazione esplicita tra configured e loaded."""
+    meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        display_name="Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        loaded_instances=({"id": "inst-qwen", "context_length": 4096},),
+    )
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+        [meta],
+        "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        configured_context_length=8192,
+    )
+    assert resolved.configured_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.requested_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.context_length == 4096
+    assert resolved.context_length != resolved.configured_context_length
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_loader_confirms_8192(monkeypatch):
+    """7. Loader che conferma correttamente 8192: ModelLoadResult registra la risposta positiva del server."""
+    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
+        class LoaderResp:
+            def __init__(self):
+                self.status = 200
+            def read(self):
+                return json.dumps({
+                    "status": "loaded",
+                    "model": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                    "instance_id": "inst-qwen2.5-8192",
+                    "context_length": 8192,
+                }).encode("utf-8")
+            def __enter__(self):
+                return self
+            def __exit__(self, *args):
+                pass
+        return LoaderResp()
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen)
+    client = LmStudioClient()
+    load_res = client.load_model("qwen2.5-7b-instruct", context_length=8192)
+
+    assert load_res.status in (ModelLifecycleStatus.LOADED, ModelLifecycleStatus.ALREADY_LOADED)
+    assert load_res.context_length == 8192
+    assert load_res.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert load_res.context_evidence_source == "LOADER_API_RESPONSE"
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_incoherent_metadata():
+    """8. Metadata incoerenti (valori non interi o malformati): non inventa arbitrariamente 8192."""
+    dict_item = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5 7B Instruct",
+        "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-qwen", "context_length": "invalid_string_not_int"}],
+        "max_context_length": "corrupt_data",
+        "loaded_instance_context_length": None,
+    }
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([dict_item], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.context_length is None
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length is None
+    assert resolved.model_max_context_length is None
+    assert resolved.context_length != 8192
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_absence_of_verifiable_info():
+    """9. Assenza totale di informazioni verificabili sul context: context_length rimane None."""

+        key="lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",

+        loaded_instance_context_length=None,

+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.context_length is None
+    assert resolved.server_confirmed_context_length is None
+    assert resolved.context_evidence_source == "UNKNOWN"
+
+
+def test_context_resolution_audit_reproduction_matrix():
+    """10. Replica esatta dello scenario di audit: variazione max_context non maschera il loaded_context."""
+    for max_ctx in (4096, 8192, 32768, None):
+        meta = LocalModelMetadata(
+            model_key="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            max_context_length=max_ctx,
+            loaded_instance_context_length=4096,
+        )
+        resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([meta], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+        # In NESSUN caso deve restituire 8192 quando l'istanza è caricata a 4096
+        assert resolved.context_length == 4096, f"Fallito per max_context={max_ctx}: atteso 4096, ottenuto {resolved.context_length}"
+        assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+        assert resolved.model_max_context_length == max_ctx
+

+def test_official_rest_payload_config_context_8192():

+def test_two_instances_same_model_different_contexts_and_selection():
+    """Blocker 1.5, 1.6: Due istanze dello stesso modello con context differenti e selezione tramite ID."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": "Q4_K_M",
+        "max_context_length": 32768,
+        "loaded_instances": [
+            {
+                "id": "inst-qwen-4k",
+                "config": {
+                    "context_length": 4096,
+                },
+            },
+            {
+                "id": "inst-qwen-8k",
+                "config": {
+                    "context_length": 8192,
+                },
+            },
+        ],
+    }
+    # 1. Senza specificare target_instance_id con configured_context_length=8192: seleziona in modo deterministico l'istanza a 8192
+    res_8k_auto = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", configured_context_length=8192)
+    assert res_8k_auto.instance_id == "inst-qwen-8k"
+    assert res_8k_auto.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert res_8k_auto.context_length == 8192
+
+    # Senza specificare target_instance_id con configured_context_length=4096: seleziona in modo deterministico l'istanza a 4096
+    res_4k_auto = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", configured_context_length=4096)
+    assert res_4k_auto.instance_id == "inst-qwen-4k"
+    assert res_4k_auto.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+    assert res_4k_auto.context_length == 4096
+
+    # 2. Con target_instance_id='inst-qwen-8k': seleziona rigorosamente la seconda istanza (8192)
+    res_8k = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", target_instance_id="inst-qwen-8k")
+    assert res_8k.instance_id == "inst-qwen-8k"
+    assert res_8k.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert res_8k.context_length == 8192
+
+    # 3. Con target_instance_id='inst-qwen-4k': seleziona rigorosamente la prima istanza (4096)
+    res_4k = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", target_instance_id="inst-qwen-4k")
+    assert res_4k.instance_id == "inst-qwen-4k"
+    assert res_4k.loaded_instance_context_length == 4096
+    assert res_4k.context_length == 4096
+
+

+def test_quantization_q4_k_m_verified_independently():

+    assert resolved.is_loaded is True
+    assert resolved.instance_id == "inst-only"
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192

+def test_blocker1_4_nonexistent_target_raises_not_installed():
+    """4. Target inesistente: ZERO fallback silenzioso, solleva AiModelNotInstalledError."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+        "loaded_instances": [
+            {"id": "instance-4096", "config": {"context_length": 4096}},
+            {"id": "instance-8192", "config": {"context_length": 8192}},
+        ],
+    }
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError) as exc_info:
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", target_instance_id="not-an-instance")
+    assert "not-an-instance" in str(exc_info.value)
+

+def test_blocker1_5_target_belonging_to_different_model_raises_mismatch():
+    """5. Target appartenente a un modello differente: solleva AiModelMismatchError."""
+    payloads = [
+        {
+            "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+            "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-qwen", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+        },
+        {
+            "key": "meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct-GGUF",
+            "display_name": "Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+            "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-llama", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+        },
+    ]
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelMismatchError) as exc_info:
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(payloads, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", target_instance_id="inst-llama")
+    assert "inst-llama" in str(exc_info.value)
+    assert "Meta-Llama-3.1" in str(exc_info.value)
+
+

+    assert resolved.configured_context_length == 8192

+def test_blocker1_7_target_with_context_8192_correct():
+    """7. Target con context 8192 corretto."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+        "loaded_instances": [
+            {"id": "instance-4096", "config": {"context_length": 4096}},
+            {"id": "instance-8192", "config": {"context_length": 8192}},
+        ],
+    }
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+        [payload],
+        "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        target_instance_id="instance-8192",
+        configured_context_length=8192,
+    )
+    assert resolved.instance_id == "instance-8192"
+    assert resolved.loaded_instance_context_length == 8192
+    assert resolved.configured_context_length == 8192
+
+
+def test_blocker1_8_no_target_single_instance():
+    """8. Nessun target e una sola istanza: selezione deterministica dell'unica istanza."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+        "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-sole", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],

+def test_blocker1_9_no_target_multiple_compatible_instances_raises_ambiguous():
+    """9. Nessun target e più istanze compatibili: solleva AiModelAmbiguousError."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+        "loaded_instances": [
+            {"id": "inst-qwen-1", "config": {"context_length": 8192}},
+            {"id": "inst-qwen-2", "config": {"context_length": 8192}},
+        ],
+    }
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelAmbiguousError) as exc_info:
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata([payload], "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", configured_context_length=8192)
+    assert "istanze multiple" in str(exc_info.value).lower() or "target_instance_id" in str(exc_info.value).lower()
+

+def test_blocker1_12_no_binding_created_for_nonexistent_target(monkeypatch):
+    """12. Nessun binding creato per un target inesistente."""
+    class MockHTTPResp:
+        def __init__(self, data):
+            self.data = data
+            self.status = 200
+        def read(self):
+            return self.data
+        def __enter__(self):
+            return self
+        def __exit__(self, *args):
+            pass
+
+    def mock_urlopen_resp(req, timeout=30.0):
+        url = req.full_url
+        if "/v1/models" in url:
+            payload = {"data": [{"id": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF"}]}
+            return MockHTTPResp(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        if "/api/v1/models" in url:
+            payload = {
+                "models": [
+                    {
+                        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+                        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+                        "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+                        "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-real-4096", "config": {"context_length": 4096}}],
+                    }
+                ]
+            }
+            return MockHTTPResp(json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"))
+        raise ValueError(f"URL non atteso: {url}")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", mock_urlopen_resp)
+    client = LmStudioClient(model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+        client.ensure_model_loaded(
+            model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            target_instance_id="ghost-instance-id",
+        )
+    # Verifica che NESSUN binding sia stato registrato
+    assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+

+def test_blocker2_7_q8_0_loaded_and_q4_k_m_installed():
+    """7. Q8_0 caricata e Q4_K_M installata: non deve selezionare Q8_0, ma Q4_K_M per caricarla!"""
+    payloads = [
+        {
+            "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q8_0",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q8_0",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q8_0", "bits_per_weight": 8},
+            "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-q8", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+        },
+        {
+            "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q4_k_m",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q4_K_M",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+            "loaded_instances": [],
+        },
+    ]
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+        payloads,
+        "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+    )
+    # DEVE aver selezionato il modello Q4_K_M (unloaded), non Q8_0!
+    assert resolved.model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q4_k_m"
+    assert resolved.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+    assert resolved.selected_quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+    assert resolved.is_loaded is False
+
+
+def test_blocker2_8_q8_0_only_available():
+    """8. Q8_0 unica quantizzazione disponibile:
+    - con required_quantization='Q4_K_M' e allow_fallback_quantization=False: solleva AiModelNotInstalledError (rifiutato).
+    - con required_quantization='Q4_K_M' e allow_fallback_quantization=True: fallback esplicito e tracciato (seleziona Q8_0).
+    """
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q8_0",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q8_0",
+        "quantization": {"name": "Q8_0", "bits_per_weight": 8},
+        "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-q8", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+    }
+    # 1. Strict mode (fallback disabilitato): REJECTED
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+            [payload],
+            "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+            allow_fallback_quantization=False,
+        )
+
+    # 2. UI / Explicit fallback autorizzato: ACCEPTED con tracciamento
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+        [payload],
+        "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+        allow_fallback_quantization=True,
+    )
+    assert resolved.selected_quantization == "Q8_0"
+    assert resolved.available_quantization == "Q8_0"
+    assert resolved.requested_quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+
+
+def test_blocker2_9_multiple_q4_k_m_unambiguous():
+    """9. Più quantizzazioni Q4_K_M, selezione non ambigua tramite canonical spec match."""
+    payloads = [
+        {
+            "key": "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+            "loaded_instances": [],
+        },
+        {
+            "key": "other-repo/qwen2.5-7b-instruct-extra",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5 7B Instruct Extra",
+            "quantization": {"name": "Q4_K_M", "bits_per_weight": 4},
+            "loaded_instances": [],
+        },
+    ]
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(payloads, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert resolved.model_key == "qwen2.5-7b-instruct"
+    assert resolved.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+
+

+def test_strict_quant_q4_k_m_unloaded_preferred_over_loaded_q8_0():
+    """8. Q4_K_M disponibile ma non caricata, Q8_0 già caricata: selezionare Q4_K_M per il caricamento."""
+    payloads = [
+        {
+            "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q8_0",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q8_0",
+            "quantization": "Q8_0",
+            "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-q8-loaded", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+        },
+        {
+            "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q4_k_m",
+            "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Q4_K_M",
+            "quantization": "Q4_K_M",
+            "loaded_instances": [],
+        },
+    ]
+    resolved = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+        payloads,
+        "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+        required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+        allow_fallback_quantization=False,
+    )
+    assert resolved.model_key == "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF/q4_k_m"
+    assert resolved.quantization == "Q4_K_M"
+    assert resolved.is_loaded is False
+    assert resolved.load_mode == "unloaded"
+
+
+def test_strict_quant_unknown_quantization_in_strict_mode():
+    """9. Quantizzazione sconosciuta in modalità rigorosa: AiModelNotInstalledError / NOT_VERIFIED."""
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": None,
+        "loaded_instances": [],
+    }
+    with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+        resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(
+            [payload],
+            "qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+            required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+            allow_fallback_quantization=False,
+        )

+def test_strict_quant_no_spurious_binding_after_quantization_mismatch():
+    """10. Nessun binding spurio dopo mismatch: client non registra binding se la quantizzazione è incompatibile."""
+    client = LmStudioClient(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1234", model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct")
+    assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+
+    # Simuliamo un payload con modello installato solo a Q8_0
+    payload = {
+        "key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF",
+        "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct",
+        "quantization": "Q8_0",
+        "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-q8", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}],
+    }
+    with mock.patch.object(client, "is_available", return_value=True), \
+         mock.patch.object(client, "get_native_models", return_value=[_normalize_model_item(payload)]):
+        with pytest.raises(AiModelNotInstalledError):
+            client.ensure_model_loaded(
+                model_id="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                required_quantization="Q4_K_M",
+                allow_fallback_quantization=False,
+            )
+        # Binding deve rimanere None: nessun binding spurio registrato!
+        assert client.get_active_binding() is None
+

+def test_strict_quant_no_regression_family_identities_qwen_llama_deepseek():
+    """11. Nessuna regressione nell'identità Qwen/Llama/DeepSeek con quantizzazione Q4_K_M."""
+    models = [
+        {"key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF", "display_name": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct", "quantization": "Q4_K_M"},
+        {"key": "deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B-GGUF", "display_name": "DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B", "quantization": "Q4_K_M"},
+        {"key": "meta-llama/Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct-GGUF", "display_name": "Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct", "quantization": "Q4_K_M"},
+    ]
+    res_qwen = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(models, "qwen2.5-7b-instruct", required_quantization="Q4_K_M")
+    assert res_qwen.canonical_spec.family == "QWEN2.5"
+
+    res_deepseek = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(models, "deepseek-r1-distill-qwen-7b", required_quantization="Q4_K_M")
+    assert res_deepseek.canonical_spec.family == "DEEPSEEK"
+
+    res_llama = resolve_operational_model_from_metadata(models, "meta-llama-3.1-8b-instruct", required_quantization="Q4_K_M")
+    assert res_llama.canonical_spec.family == "LLAMA"
+
