+"""
+tests/unit/test_packaging_release.py
+------------------------------------
+Test di packaging REALE per il release gate di ChatAnalysis:
+1. Costruisce una wheel reale dal repository (in una directory temporanea).
+2. Apre la wheel costruita (zipfile) ed esamina il manifest.
+3. Verifica la presenza tassativa del package analysis/ e dei suoi file:
+   - analysis/__init__.py
+   - analysis/models.py
+   - analysis/consistency.py
+   - analysis/service.py
+4. Verifica la presenza di tutti i package fondamentali:
+   - ai/, core/, entity_resolution/, importer/, multimodal/, normalization/,
+     search/, ui/, unified/, validation/
+5. Estrae la wheel in un ambiente isolato (senza repo root in sys.path)
+   e verifica che gli import fondamentali abbiano successo:
+   - import analysis
+   - import ui.application
+   - import ai.lmstudio
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import zipfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+
+
+def test_packaging_wheel_contains_analysis_and_core_packages():
+    """Costruisce una wheel reale e verifica i package inclusi e l'import isolato."""
+    # Assicura ambiente di build pulito privo di residui da compilazioni precedenti
+    shutil.rmtree(REPO_ROOT / "build", ignore_errors=True)
+
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
+        dist_dir = Path(tmp_dir) / "dist"
+        dist_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+        # 1. Costruzione wheel tramite pip wheel --no-deps
+        cmd = [
+            sys.executable,
+            "-m",
+            "pip",
+            "wheel",
+            "--no-deps",
+            "--wheel-dir",
+            str(dist_dir),
+            str(REPO_ROOT),
+        ]
+        res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
+        assert res.returncode == 0, f"pip wheel fallita:\nSTDOUT:\n{res.stdout}\nSTDERR:\n{res.stderr}"
+
+        wheels = list(dist_dir.glob("*.whl"))
+        assert len(wheels) == 1, f"Attesa 1 wheel, trovate: {wheels}"
+        whl_path = wheels[0]
+
+        # 2. Ispezione archivio wheel
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(whl_path, "r") as zf:
+            namelist = set(zf.namelist())
+
+            # 3. Verifica package analysis/
+            required_analysis_files = [
+                "analysis/__init__.py",
+                "analysis/models.py",
+                "analysis/consistency.py",
+                "analysis/service.py",
+            ]
+            for req_file in required_analysis_files:
+                assert req_file in namelist, f"File obbligatorio mancante nella wheel: {req_file}"
+
+            # 4. Verifica package fondamentali
+            fundamental_packages = [
+                "ai/__init__.py",
+                "core/__init__.py",
+                "entity_resolution/__init__.py",
+                "importer/__init__.py",
+                "multimodal/__init__.py",
+                "normalization/__init__.py",
+                "search/__init__.py",
+                "ui/__init__.py",
+                "unified/__init__.py",
+                "validation/__init__.py",
+            ]
+            for f_pkg in fundamental_packages:
+                assert f_pkg in namelist, f"Package fondamentale mancante nella wheel: {f_pkg}"
+
+            # Verifica assenza assoluta di __pycache__ e *.pyc
+            pycache_entries = [n for n in namelist if "__pycache__" in n or n.endswith(".pyc")]
+            assert not pycache_entries, f"Wheel contiene residui di compilazione o cache: {pycache_entries}"
+
+        # 5. Test di import isolato (senza repo root in PYTHONPATH)
+        extract_dir = Path(tmp_dir) / "isolated_site_packages"
+        extract_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(whl_path, "r") as zf:
+            zf.extractall(extract_dir)
+
+        # Esecuzione di uno script Python in sottoprocesso con PYTHONPATH puntato solo a extract_dir
+        isolated_script = (
+            "import sys\n"
+            "import analysis\n"
+            "from analysis.models import OperationalAnalysisConfig, DocumentAnalysisState\n"
+            "from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService\n"
+            "import ui.application\n"
+            "import ai.lmstudio\n"
+            "import importer.whatsapp_export\n"
+            "import multimodal.asset_access\n"
+            "print('ISOLATED_IMPORT_SUCCESS')\n"
+        )
+        env = os.environ.copy()
+        env["PYTHONPATH"] = str(extract_dir)
+        # Rimuovi virtualenv path pollution se presente
+        proc = subprocess.run(
+            [sys.executable, "-c", isolated_script],
+            capture_output=True,
+            text=True,
+            cwd=str(extract_dir),  # Esegui lontano dal repo root
+            env=env,
+            check=False,
+        )
+        assert proc.returncode == 0, f"Import isolato fallito:\nSTDOUT:\n{proc.stdout}\nSTDERR:\n{proc.stderr}"
+        assert "ISOLATED_IMPORT_SUCCESS" in proc.stdout

+"""
+tests/unit/test_smoke_operational_ai.py
+---------------------------------------
+Suite di test obbligatoria per il Release Gate e l'Hardening dello Smoke Test Operativo AI.
+
+Copre in modo esaustivo:
+1. Context confermato 8192 -> PASS consentito (Exit Code 0).
+2. Context confermato 4096 -> FAILED (Exit Code 1).
+3. Context UNKNOWN -> NOT_VERIFIED (Exit Code 2).
+4. Modello effettivo differente -> FAILED (Exit Code 1).
+5. Model key canonico associato a instance ID opaco verificato -> PASS consentito (Exit Code 0).
+6. Modello non verificabile -> NOT_VERIFIED (Exit Code 2).
+7. Fake client -> soltanto MOCK_VERIFIED (mai LIVE_VERIFIED).
+8. LM Studio assente -> Exit Code 2.
+9. Contaminazione simulata -> Exit Code 1.
+10. JSON invalido -> Exit Code 1.
+11. Bash con smoke fallito e output catturato tramite tee con pipefail -> exit code preservato.
+12. Smoke positivo mock -> Exit Code 0.
+13. Ingestion reale WhatsApp TXT end-to-end attraverso la vera pipeline applicativa.
+14. Ingestion reale archivio WhatsApp ZIP attraverso la vera pipeline applicativa.
+15. Isolamento cross-document rigoroso (DEMO vs FILE reale).
+16. Accesso corretto al campo .rationale di TopicDetectionResult e tracciamento metadata.
+17. Preservazione fedele del risultato scientifico per il caso di controllo (incluso ABSENT).
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from typing import Any
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from ai.backend import BaseLlmClient, LlmCompletionResponse
+from ai.models import (
+    ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    TopicDecision,
+    TopicDetectionResult,
+    TopicDiscoveryResult,
+    TopicQuery,
+)
+from analysis.service import LocalTopicAnalysisService
+from scripts.smoke_operational_ai import (
+    DEFAULT_SYNTHETIC_CHAT_CONTENT,
+    FakeSmokeClient,
+    ensure_synthetic_fixtures,
+    run_smoke_test,
+)
+from ui.demo import build_synthetic_demo_dataset
+from ui.ingestion import ingest_file_payload
+from ui.models import IngestionStatus, SourceFormat
+
+PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
+
+
+class TestSmokeOperationalAiHardening:
+    """Verifica e hardening dei release gate dello smoke test operativo."""
+
+    def test_smoke_test_fake_normal_passes_exit_0(self, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
+        """Smoke test simulato deterministico: supera tutti i controlli con Exit 0 e status SUCCESSO."""
+        exit_code = run_smoke_test(use_fake=True)
+        captured = capsys.readouterr().out
+
+        assert exit_code == 0
+        assert "ESITO SMOKE TEST OPERATIVO: SUCCESSO" in captured

+        assert "[PASSED      ] CHECK_MODEL_RESOLUTION" in captured

+    def test_bash_tee_pipeline_preserves_failing_exit_code(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
+        """11. Bash con smoke fallito e output catturato tramite tee -> exit code preservato via PIPESTATUS."""
+        bash_exe = None
+        if sys.platform == "win32":
+            candidates = [
+                r"C:\Program Files\Git\bin\bash.exe",
+                r"C:\Program Files\Git\usr\bin\bash.exe",
+            ]
+            for c in candidates:
+                if Path(c).is_file():
+                    bash_exe = c
+                    break
+        if not bash_exe:
+            which_bash = shutil.which("bash")
+            if which_bash and "system32" not in which_bash.lower():
+                bash_exe = which_bash
+
+        if not bash_exe:
+            pytest.skip("Bash nativo non disponibile in questo ambiente Windows")
+
+        log_path = tmp_path / "smoke_failing.log"
+        try:
+            py_posix = subprocess.check_output(
+                [bash_exe, "-c", f"cygpath -u '{sys.executable}'"],
+                text=True,
+            ).strip()
+            log_posix = subprocess.check_output(
+                [bash_exe, "-c", f"cygpath -u '{log_path}'"],
+                text=True,
+            ).strip()
+        except Exception:
+            py_posix = sys.executable.replace("\\", "/")
+            log_posix = str(log_path).replace("\\", "/")
+
+        bash_script = (
+            "set -o pipefail\n"
+            f'"{py_posix}" scripts/smoke_operational_ai.py --fake --simulate-contamination 2>&1 | tee "{log_posix}"\n'
+            "rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}\n"
+            'echo "CAPTURED_RC: $rc"\n'
+            "exit $rc\n"
+        )
+        proc = subprocess.run([bash_exe, "-c", bash_script], cwd=str(PROJECT_ROOT), capture_output=True, text=True)
+        assert proc.returncode == 1, f"Returncode was {proc.returncode}, stdout: {proc.stdout}, stderr: {proc.stderr}"
+        assert "CAPTURED_RC: 1" in proc.stdout
+        assert log_path.exists()
+        assert "FALLITO" in log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+

+    def test_real_whatsapp_txt_ingestion_e2e_pipeline(self) -> None:
+        """13. Ingestion reale WhatsApp TXT end-to-end attraverso la vera pipeline applicativa."""
+        txt_path, _ = ensure_synthetic_fixtures()
+        raw_bytes = txt_path.read_bytes()
+
+        res = ingest_file_payload(
+            file_bytes_or_io=raw_bytes,
+            original_filename="synthetic_whatsapp_chat.txt",
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        )
+
+        assert res.summary.status == IngestionStatus.SUCCESS
+        assert res.summary.raw_record_count == 4
+        assert res.summary.unified_message_count == 4
+        assert len(res.documents) == 1
+
+        doc = next(iter(res.documents.values()))
+        assert doc.source_name == "whatsapp_export"
+        assert doc.document_id.startswith("doc::whatsapp_export::")
+        assert len(doc.bundles) == 4
+        assert len(doc.all_evidence_sections) == 4
+
+        texts = [b.message.text_content for b in doc.bundles]
+        assert any("container" in t.lower() for t in texts)
+        assert any("sigilli" in t.lower() for t in texts)
+        assert any("inventariati" in t.lower() for t in texts)
+        assert any("sdoganamento" in t.lower() for t in texts)
+

+    def test_cross_document_isolation_demo_vs_file(self) -> None:
+        """15. Isolamento cross-document rigoroso (DEMO vs FILE reale)."""
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+        txt_path, _ = ensure_synthetic_fixtures()
+
+        ingest_res = ingest_file_payload(
+            file_bytes_or_io=txt_path.read_bytes(),
+            original_filename=txt_path.name,
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+        )
+        doc_file = next(iter(ingest_res.documents.values()))
+
+        client = FakeSmokeClient()
+        disc_demo = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc_demo, client=client, max_topics=2)
+        disc_file = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc_file, client=client, max_topics=2)
+
+        demo_run_id = disc_demo.metadata["run_id"]
+        file_run_id = disc_file.metadata["run_id"]
+
+        assert service.get_discovery_run(doc_demo.document_id, demo_run_id) is not None
+        assert service.get_discovery_run(doc_file.document_id, file_run_id) is not None
+        assert service.get_discovery_run(doc_demo.document_id, file_run_id) is None
+        assert service.get_discovery_run(doc_file.document_id, demo_run_id) is None
+
+    def test_topic_detection_rationale_field_and_metadata(self) -> None:
+        """16. Accesso corretto al campo .rationale di TopicDetectionResult e tracciamento metadata."""
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+        client = FakeSmokeClient()
+
+        query = TopicQuery(
+            topic_id="q1",
+            label="Ispezione Depositi",
+            description="Verifica magazzino e casse sigillate",
+        )
+        det_res = service.run_topic_detection(document=doc_demo, topic=query, client=client)
+
+        assert hasattr(det_res, "rationale")
+        assert isinstance(det_res.rationale, str)
+        assert len(det_res.rationale.strip()) > 0
+        assert det_res.decision == TopicDecision.PRESENT
+        assert len(det_res.evidence_ids) > 0

+    def test_control_case_absent_decision_preserves_faithful_scientific_record(self) -> None:
+        """17. Preservazione fedele del risultato scientifico per il caso di controllo (incluso ABSENT)."""
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+
+        class AbsentClient(BaseLlmClient):
+            def is_available(self) -> bool:
+                return True
+
+            def chat_completion(self, messages: list[dict[str, str]], **kwargs: Any) -> LlmCompletionResponse:
+                payload = {
+                    "decision": "ABSENT",
+                    "evidence_ids": [],
+                    "rationale": "Nessuna evidenza sufficiente riscontrata per comprovare il tema.",
+                }
+                return LlmCompletionResponse(
+                    content=json.dumps(payload),
+                    model="qwen2.5-7b-instruct",
+                    prompt_tokens=20,
+                    completion_tokens=20,
+                    total_tokens=40,
+                    latency_seconds=0.1,
+                )
+
+        absent_client = AbsentClient()
+        ctrl_query = TopicQuery(
+            topic_id="ctrl_logistica",
+            label="Logistica e Ispezione Depositi",
+            description="Ispezione visiva del magazzino merci con casse sigillate e sicurezza",
+        )
+        ctrl_det = service.run_topic_detection(document=doc_demo, topic=ctrl_query, client=absent_client)
+
+        assert ctrl_det.decision == TopicDecision.ABSENT
+        assert len(ctrl_det.evidence_ids) == 0
+        assert "Nessuna evidenza" in ctrl_det.rationale
+
+    def test_smoke_instance_replacement_mid_test_fails_exit_1(self, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
+        """Blocker 2: Sostituzione imprevista del modello durante lo smoke -> FAILED (Exit Code 1)."""

+        captured = capsys.readouterr().out
+        assert exit_code == 1

+    def test_verified_client_injected_and_shared_across_pipeline(self) -> None:
+        """Blocker 2: Il client verificato viene iniettato nel service e utilizzato sia da Discovery che da Detection."""
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+        client = FakeSmokeClient()
+        service._injected_client = client
+
+        # 1. Discovery usa il client iniettato
+        disc_res = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc_demo, client=client, max_topics=2)
+        assert client.call_count >= 1
+        call_count_after_disc = client.call_count
+
+        # 2. Detection indipendente usa lo stesso client
+        first_topic = disc_res.topics[0]
+        det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_demo,
+            discovered_topic=first_topic,
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=client,
+        )
+        assert client.call_count > call_count_after_disc
+        assert service._injected_client is client
+
+        # 3. Entrambe le inferenze preservano i rispettivi metadata
+        assert disc_res.metadata.get("run_id") != det_res.metadata.get("run_id")
+        assert "model" in disc_res.metadata
+        assert "model" in det_res.metadata
+
+    def test_independent_inferences_no_evidence_ids_hinted_to_detection(self) -> None:
+        """Blocker 2: Nessun evidence_id di Topic Discovery viene passato come suggerimento alla Detection."""
+        service = LocalTopicAnalysisService()
+        doc_demo, _, _ = build_synthetic_demo_dataset()
+        client = FakeSmokeClient()
+
+        disc_res = service.run_topic_discovery(document=doc_demo, client=client, max_topics=2)
+        first_topic = disc_res.topics[0]
+        assert len(first_topic.evidence_ids) > 0
+
+        # Durante la seconda inferenza, l'oggetto TopicQuery inviato alla detection
+        # contiene esclusivamente label e short_description (nessun evidence_id suggerito)
+        det_res, consistency = service.verify_discovered_topic(
+            document=doc_demo,
+            discovered_topic=first_topic,
+            discovery_result=disc_res,
+            client=client,
+        )
+        assert det_res is not None
+        assert disc_res.metadata["run_id"] != det_res.metadata["run_id"]
+
+    def test_programmatic_attribute_error_fails_exit_1(self, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
+        """Blocker 3: Errori programmatici (come AttributeError su metodo mancante) sono classificati FAILED (Exit 1)."""
+        from scripts import smoke_operational_ai
+        # Simuliamo un client live che solleva AttributeError per un metodo mancante
+        mock_client = MagicMock()
+        mock_client.is_available.return_value = True
+        mock_client.get_native_models.return_value = [
+            {"key": "lmstudio-community/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-GGUF", "quantization": "Q4_K_M", "loaded_instances": [{"id": "inst-1", "config": {"context_length": 8192}}]}
+        ]
+        # ensure_model_loaded funziona ma get_active_binding solleva AttributeError (metodo inesistente)
+        mock_client.ensure_model_loaded.return_value = "inst-1"
+        del mock_client.get_active_binding  # assenza metodo -> solleva AttributeError
+
+        monkeypatch.setattr(smoke_operational_ai, "LmStudioClient", lambda **kwargs: mock_client)
+        exit_code = run_smoke_test(use_fake=False)
+        captured = capsys.readouterr().out
+
+        assert exit_code == 1
+        assert "ESITO SMOKE TEST OPERATIVO: FALLITO" in captured
+        assert "CHECK_MODEL_RESOLUTION" in captured
+        assert "FAILED" in captured
+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_staging_and_large_file.py
+-----------------------------------------
+Test di conformità per FASE BLOCKER 3:
+LARGE-FILE INGESTION & LOCAL SQLITE STAGING.
+
+Verifiche:
+1. IngestionStagingStore: inserimento a batch e flush automatico.
+2. IngestionStagingStore: recupero ordinato deterministico per sequenza (seq_order).
+3. IngestionStagingStore: partizionamento per chat e isolamento.
+4. IngestionStagingStore: estrazione titoli chat da record ausiliari.
+5. IngestionStagingStore: context manager e chiusura sicura delle risorse SQLite.
+6. Ingestion completa di stream sintetico tramite ingest_file_payload con staging attivo.
+7. Preservazione dell'integrità del ConversationEvidenceDocument e dell'EvidenceIndex.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+import pytest
+
+from ui.models import SourceFormat
+from importer.models import RawRecord
+from multimodal.evidence import EvidenceSourceType, TextEvidenceSection
+from normalization.models import (
+    CanonicalMessageType,
+    NormalizedActor,
+    NormalizedRecord,
+    NormalizedTimestamp,
+    TimestampTzStatus,
+)
+from ui.ingestion import ingest_file_payload
+from ui.staging import IngestionStagingStore
+from validation.models import ValidationResult
+
+
+def _make_dummy_norm_record(
+    seq: int,
+    chat_id: str = "chat_1",
+    sender: str = "Alice",
+    text: str = "Test",
+    record_type: str = "message",
+) -> NormalizedRecord:
+    raw = RawRecord(
+        source_name="whatsapp_test",
+        source_path="/dummy/path.txt",
+        source_record_id=f"rec_{seq}",
+        record_type=record_type,
+        raw_fields={"subject": f"Title {chat_id}"} if record_type == "chat" else {},
+        media_reference=None,
+        metadata={},
+    )
+    val = ValidationResult(record=raw, issues=())
+    return NormalizedRecord(
+        raw_record=raw,
+        validation_result=val,
+        source_name="whatsapp_test",
+        source_record_id=f"rec_{seq}",
+        record_type=record_type,
+        chat_id=chat_id,
+        actor_from=NormalizedActor(raw_value=sender, actor_type="alias"),
+        actor_to=None,
+        timestamp=NormalizedTimestamp(
+            status=TimestampTzStatus.KNOWN_UTC,
+            raw_value="2024-01-01 10:00:00",
+            iso_string="2024-01-01T10:00:00Z",
+        ),
+        message_type=CanonicalMessageType.TEXT,
+        text_content=text,
+        media_reference=None,
+    )
+
+
+def test_staging_store_batch_insert_and_count(tmp_path: Path):
+    db_path = tmp_path / "staging_test.sqlite"
+    with IngestionStagingStore(db_path, batch_size=5) as store:
+        for i in range(12):
+            rec = _make_dummy_norm_record(seq=i, text=f"Message {i}")
+            store.stage_record(rec)
+
+        assert store.count() == 12
+        assert store.count("message") == 12
+        assert store.count("chat") == 0
+
+
+def test_staging_store_deterministic_order_preserved(tmp_path: Path):
+    db_path = tmp_path / "staging_test.sqlite"
+    with IngestionStagingStore(db_path, batch_size=10) as store:
+        for i in range(25):
+            store.stage_record(_make_dummy_norm_record(seq=i, text=f"Message {i}"))
+
+        retrieved = list(store.iter_all_records(chunk_size=7))
+        assert len(retrieved) == 25
+        for i, rec in enumerate(retrieved):
+            assert rec.source_record_id == f"rec_{i}"
+            assert rec.text_content == f"Message {i}"
+
+
+def test_staging_store_chat_partitioning_and_isolation(tmp_path: Path):
+    db_path = tmp_path / "staging_test.sqlite"
+    with IngestionStagingStore(db_path) as store:
+        # Chat 1: 5 messaggi
+        for i in range(5):
+            store.stage_record(_make_dummy_norm_record(seq=i, chat_id="chat_1"))
+        # Chat 2: 7 messaggi
+        for i in range(5, 12):
+            store.stage_record(_make_dummy_norm_record(seq=i, chat_id="chat_2"))
+
+        distinct_keys = store.get_distinct_chat_keys()
+        assert distinct_keys == ["chat_1", "chat_2"]
+
+        c1_records = list(store.iter_records_for_chat("chat_1"))
+        assert len(c1_records) == 5
+        assert all(r.chat_id == "chat_1" for r in c1_records)
+
+        c2_records = list(store.iter_records_for_chat("chat_2"))
+        assert len(c2_records) == 7
+        assert all(r.chat_id == "chat_2" for r in c2_records)
+
+
+def test_staging_store_extract_chat_titles(tmp_path: Path):
+    db_path = tmp_path / "staging_test.sqlite"
+    with IngestionStagingStore(db_path) as store:
+        chat_rec = _make_dummy_norm_record(seq=1, chat_id="chat_alpha", record_type="chat")
+        store.stage_record(chat_rec)
+
+        titles = store.extract_chat_titles()
+        assert titles.get("chat_alpha") == "Title chat_alpha"
+
+
+def test_synthetic_stream_ingestion_with_staging(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica l'intero ciclo di ingest_file_payload con staging SQLite su un export WhatsApp valido."""
+    content = (
+        "10/01/2024, 09:00 - I messaggi e le chiamate sono crittografati end-to-end.\n"
+        "10/01/2024, 09:01 - Alice: Primo messaggio di test\n"
+        "10/01/2024, 09:02 - Bob: Secondo messaggio di risposta\n"
+        "10/01/2024, 09:03 - Alice: Terzo messaggio con evidenza\n"
+    )
+    raw_bytes = content.encode("utf-8")
+    result = ingest_file_payload(
+        file_bytes_or_io=raw_bytes,
+        original_filename="chat.txt",
+        source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_EXPORT,
+    )
+
+    from ui.models import IngestionStatus
+    assert result.summary.status == IngestionStatus.SUCCESS
+    assert result.summary.raw_record_count == 4
+    assert result.summary.unified_message_count == 4
+    assert len(result.documents) == 1
+
+    doc = list(result.documents.values())[0]
+    assert len(doc.bundles) == 4
+    assert len(doc.all_evidence_sections) == 4

+    def test_multi_conversation_preserves_analysis_results(self):
+        """Verifica che cambiare conversazione (Chat A -> Chat B -> Chat A) preservi i risultati (FASE 25)."""
+        from ai.models import (
+            ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+            TopicDecision,
+            TopicDetectionResult,
+            TopicQuery,
+        )
+        from ui.models import (
+            ImportedConversationInfo,
+            IngestionResult,
+            IngestionStatus,
+            IngestionSummary,
+            SourceFormat,
+        )
+
+        s: dict = {}
+        state.init_session_state(s)
+
+        doc_a = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc::chatA", bundles=(), chat_id="chatA", source_name="src")
+        doc_b = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc::chatB", bundles=(), chat_id="chatB", source_name="src")
+
+        cinfo_a = ImportedConversationInfo(document_id="doc::chatA", chat_id="chatA", display_label="Chat A", bundle_count=0, section_count=0, languages=(), source_name="src")
+        cinfo_b = ImportedConversationInfo(document_id="doc::chatB", chat_id="chatB", display_label="Chat B", bundle_count=0, section_count=0, languages=(), source_name="src")
+
+        summary = IngestionSummary(
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_MSGSTORE,
+            original_filename="msgstore.db",
+            sha256="123",
+            file_size_bytes=100,
+            raw_record_count=0,
+            validation_issue_count=0,
+            normalized_record_count=0,
+            unified_message_count=0,
+            conversation_count=2,
+            auxiliary_record_count=0,
+            available_conversations=(cinfo_a, cinfo_b),
+            status=IngestionStatus.SUCCESS,
+        )
+        res = IngestionResult(summary=summary, documents={"doc::chatA": doc_a, "doc::chatB": doc_b})
+        state.set_real_ingestion_result(res, s)
+
+        # 1. Attivo Chat A ed eseguo una Topic Detection
+        state.select_conversation("doc::chatA", s)
+        det_a = TopicDetectionResult(
+            topic=TopicQuery(topic_id="T_A", label="Topic Chat A", description="Desc A"),
+            decision=TopicDecision.ABSENT,
+            evidence_ids=(),
+            rationale="Assente in A",
+            provenance_document_id="doc::chatA",
+        )
+        state.add_detection_result(det_a, s)
+        assert len(state.get_detection_results(s)) == 1
+        assert state.get_detection_results(s)[0].topic.topic_id == "T_A"
+
+        # 2. Passo a Chat B
+        state.select_conversation("doc::chatB", s)
+        # In Chat B i risultati di Chat A non devono apparire
+        assert state.get_detection_results(s) == ()
+
+        # Eseguo Topic Detection su Chat B
+        det_b = TopicDetectionResult(
+            topic=TopicQuery(topic_id="T_B", label="Topic Chat B", description="Desc B"),
+            decision=TopicDecision.ABSENT,
+            evidence_ids=(),
+            rationale="Assente in B",
+            provenance_document_id="doc::chatB",
+        )
+        state.add_detection_result(det_b, s)
+        assert len(state.get_detection_results(s)) == 1
+        assert state.get_detection_results(s)[0].topic.topic_id == "T_B"
+
+        # 3. Torno a Chat A
+        state.select_conversation("doc::chatA", s)
+        # I risultati di Chat A devono essere ancora intatti!
+        results_a = state.get_detection_results(s)
+        assert len(results_a) == 1
+        assert results_a[0].topic.topic_id == "T_A"
+
+        # 4. Torno a Chat B
+        state.select_conversation("doc::chatB", s)
+        results_b = state.get_detection_results(s)
+        assert len(results_b) == 1
+        assert results_b[0].topic.topic_id == "T_B"
+
+    def test_failed_ingestion_preserves_previous_dataset(self):
+        """Verifica che un'ingestion FAILED non cancelli il dataset precedentemente caricato."""
+        from ai.models import ConversationEvidenceDocument
+        from ui.models import (
+            ImportedConversationInfo,
+            IngestionResult,
+            IngestionStatus,
+            IngestionSummary,
+            SourceFormat,
+        )
+
+        s: dict = {}
+        state.init_session_state(s)
+
+        # 1. Carica un dataset iniziale con successo
+        doc = ConversationEvidenceDocument(document_id="doc::chat1", bundles=(), chat_id="chat1", source_name="src")
+        cinfo = ImportedConversationInfo(document_id="doc::chat1", chat_id="chat1", display_label="Chat 1", bundle_count=0, section_count=0, languages=(), source_name="src")
+        summary_ok = IngestionSummary(
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_MSGSTORE,
+            original_filename="msgstore.db",
+            sha256="111",
+            file_size_bytes=100,
+            raw_record_count=1,
+            validation_issue_count=0,
+            normalized_record_count=1,
+            unified_message_count=1,
+            conversation_count=1,
+            auxiliary_record_count=0,
+            available_conversations=(cinfo,),
+            status=IngestionStatus.SUCCESS,
+        )
+        res_ok = IngestionResult(summary=summary_ok, documents={"doc::chat1": doc})
+        state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_ok, s)
+
+        assert state.is_dataset_loaded(s) is True
+        assert state.get_selected_document_id(s) == "doc::chat1"
+
+        # 2. Simula una successiva ingestion FALLITA
+        summary_failed = IngestionSummary(
+            source_format=SourceFormat.WHATSAPP_MSGSTORE,
+            original_filename="corrupted.db",
+            sha256="000",
+            file_size_bytes=10,
+            raw_record_count=0,
+            validation_issue_count=1,
+            normalized_record_count=0,
+            unified_message_count=0,
+            conversation_count=0,
+            auxiliary_record_count=0,
+            status=IngestionStatus.FAILED,
+        )
+        res_failed = IngestionResult(
+            summary=summary_failed,
+            documents={},
+            error_message="Formato file non valido o database corrotto",
+        )
+        state.set_real_ingestion_result(res_failed, s)
+
+        # Il dataset precedente DEVE essere intatto e non cancellato!
+        assert state.is_dataset_loaded(s) is True
+        assert state.get_selected_document_id(s) == "doc::chat1"
+        assert state.get_conversation_document(s) is doc
+        assert state.get_error_message(s) == "Formato file non valido o database corrotto"
+

+"""
+tests/unit/test_whatsapp_strict_encoding.py
+-------------------------------------------
+Test di integrità forense per WhatsAppExportImporter:
+- Criterio 31, 33: Nessun errors="replace" sui dati forensi (nessun U+FFFD silenzioso).
+- Criterio 31: Sequenze di byte non decodificabili sollevano un ValueError controllato.
+- Criterio 32: Streaming riga per riga preservato, nessun buffer globale dell'intero file in memoria.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+import pytest
+
+from importer.whatsapp_export import WhatsAppExportImporter
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_invalid_utf8_bytes_raises_controlled_error(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica che un file TXT con byte non validi sollevi ValueError e non mascheri con replacement char."""
+    invalid_file = tmp_path / "chat_corrupt.txt"
+    # Byte caotici non decodificabili
+    invalid_file.write_bytes(b"\x80\x81\x82\x83\xff\xfe\xca\xfe\xba\xbe")
+
+    importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as exc_info:
+        list(importer.import_records(invalid_file))
+
+    assert "non presenta la struttura" in str(exc_info.value) or "decodificare" in str(exc_info.value)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_invalid_utf8_mid_stream_raises_controlled_error(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica che un errore di decodifica durante lo streaming del file sollevi ValueError forense controllato."""
+    chat_file = tmp_path / "chat_mid_corrupt.txt"
+    # Campione iniziale >64KB di puro testo valido UTF-8 per determinare l'encoding UTF-8
+    valid_lines = [
+        f"12/03/2024, 10:15 - Utente: Messaggio lungo di test forense con testo valido #{i}\n".encode("utf-8")
+        for i in range(1200)
+    ]
+    # Poi aggiungiamo una riga con byte esplicitamente invalidi in UTF-8
+    corrupt_line = b"12/03/2024, 10:16 - Attaccante: byte corrotto \x80\x81 fine.\n"
+    chat_file.write_bytes(b"".join(valid_lines) + corrupt_line)
+
+    importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as exc_info:
+        # Consuma il generatore streaming
+        list(importer.import_records(chat_file))
+
+    assert "Errore di decodifica forense" in str(exc_info.value)
+    assert "utf-8" in str(exc_info.value)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_valid_utf8_with_bom_supported(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica che un file UTF-8 con BOM sia decodificato correttamente senza esporre il carattere BOM."""
+    chat_file = tmp_path / "chat_bom.txt"
+    content = "\ufeff12/03/2024, 10:15 - Alice: Primo messaggio con BOM\n12/03/2024, 10:16 - Bob: Risposta\n"
+    chat_file.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8-sig")
+
+    importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+    records = list(importer.import_records(chat_file))
+    assert len(records) == 2
+    assert "\ufeff" not in records[0].raw_fields["sender"]
+    assert records[0].raw_fields["sender"] == "Alice"
+    assert records[0].raw_fields["text"] == "Primo messaggio con BOM"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.unit
+def test_line_by_line_streaming_generator(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica che import_records sia un generatore che produce record sequenzialmente senza caricare tutto in memoria."""
+    chat_file = tmp_path / "chat_stream.txt"
+    lines = [
+        f"12/03/2024, 10:{i:02d} - User{i}: Messaggio {i}\n"
+        for i in range(10)
+    ]
+    chat_file.write_text("".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
+
+    importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+    stream = importer.import_records(chat_file)
+    assert iter(stream) is stream  # Deve essere un iterator / generator
+
+    first_record = next(stream)
+    assert first_record.raw_fields["sender"] == "User0"
+    assert first_record.raw_fields["text"] == "Messaggio 0"
+
+    # Consuma il resto
+    remaining = list(stream)
+    assert len(remaining) == 9

+"""
+test_zip_security_hardening.py
+------------------------------
+Test dedicati di hardening di sicurezza per archivi ZIP WhatsApp (FASE 21, FASE 32).
+
+Verifica che tutti i seguenti pattern malevoli vengano rigorosamente intercettati e respinti:
+- ../evil.txt
+- ..\\evil.txt
+- folder/../evil.txt
+- folder\\..\\evil.txt
+- C:\\evil.txt
+- \\\\server\\share\\evil.txt
+- /etc/passwd
+- symlink POSIX
+- ZIP bomb (dimensione totale, per-file, compression ratio, troppi membri)
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import zipfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from importer.whatsapp_export import WhatsAppExportImporter, _validate_zip_archive
+
+
+def _create_malicious_zip(member_name: str, content: bytes = b"malicious content") -> io.BytesIO:
+    bio = io.BytesIO()
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr(member_name, content)
+    bio.seek(0)
+    return bio
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "traversal_path",
+    [
+        "../evil.txt",
+        "..\\evil.txt",
+        "folder/../evil.txt",
+        "folder\\..\\evil.txt",
+        "C:\\evil.txt",
+        "C:/evil.txt",
+        "\\\\server\\share\\evil.txt",
+        "//server/share/evil.txt",
+        "/etc/passwd",
+        "/var/log/syslog",
+        "sub/../../secret.txt",
+        "sub\\..\\..\\secret.txt",
+    ],
+)
+def test_zip_path_traversal_rejected(traversal_path: str):
+    """Verifica che qualsiasi tentativo di directory traversal (Windows o POSIX) sia respinto."""
+    bio = _create_malicious_zip(traversal_path)
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "r") as zf:
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="non consentito"):
+            _validate_zip_archive(zf)
+
+
+def test_zip_with_symlink_rejected():
+    """Verifica che membri con attributo symlink POSIX siano respinti."""
+    bio = io.BytesIO()
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "w") as zf:
+        zinfo = zipfile.ZipInfo("symlink_target.txt")
+        # Attributo UNIX symlink: S_IFLNK (0o120000)
+        zinfo.external_attr = 0o120777 << 16
+        zf.writestr(zinfo, b"/etc/shadow")
+    bio.seek(0)
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "r") as zf:
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="symlink non consentito"):
+            _validate_zip_archive(zf)
+
+
+def test_importer_validate_source_rejects_malicious_zip(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Verifica che validate_source dell'importer respinga l'archivio malevolo."""
+    bad_zip_path = tmp_path / "malicious.zip"
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bad_zip_path, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("folder\\..\\evil.txt", b"evil data")
+
+    importer = WhatsAppExportImporter()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="non consentito"):
+        importer.validate_source(bad_zip_path)
+
+
+def test_legitimate_nested_zip_accepted():
+    """Verifica che percorsi legittimi all'interno dello zip senza traversal siano accettati."""
+    bio = io.BytesIO()
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "w") as zf:
+        zf.writestr("_chat.txt", b"10/05/2024, 14:30 - Alice: Ciao\n10/05/2024, 14:31 - Bob: Salve\n")
+        zf.writestr("media/photo.jpg", b"fake photo binary data")
+        zf.writestr("media/subfolder/audio.opus", b"fake audio binary data")
+    bio.seek(0)
+
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(bio, "r") as zf:
+        # Non deve sollevare alcuna eccezione
+        _validate_zip_archive(zf)

+def get_operational_analysis_service(
+    base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:1234",
+    model_id: str | None = None,
+    timeout_seconds: float = DEFAULT_OPERATIONAL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
+    discovery_run_store: MutableMapping[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
+    authoritative_live_run_ids: MutableSet[str] | None = None,
+) -> Any:
+    """
+    Factory per ottenere l'istanza autoritativa di LocalTopicAnalysisService.
+    Garantisce configurazione coerente tra tutte le operazioni applicative della UI
+    e inietta il run store document-scoped persistente della sessione.
+    """

+        default_model=model_id or DEFAULT_OPERATIONAL_MODEL,
+        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
+        discovery_run_store=discovery_run_store,
+        authoritative_live_run_ids=authoritative_live_run_ids,
+    )

+def execute_topic_discovery(
+    document: ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    client: BaseLlmClient | None = None,
+    model_id: str | None = None,
+    base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:1234",
+    max_topics: int = 10,
+    temperature: float = DEFAULT_OPERATIONAL_TEMPERATURE,
+    seed: int | None = 42,
+    preserve_raw_response: bool = False,
+    session_state: MutableMapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> TopicDiscoveryResult:
+    """
+    Esegue Topic Discovery LIVE sulla conversazione corrente tramite LocalTopicAnalysisService.
+    Garantisce supporto trasparente al chunking multi-chunk per chat di grandi dimensioni.
+    """
+    from ui import state
+    disc_store = state.get_discovery_run_store(session_state)
+    auth_ids = state.get_authoritative_live_run_ids(session_state)
+    service = get_operational_analysis_service(
+        base_url=base_url,
+        model_id=model_id,
+        discovery_run_store=disc_store,
+        authoritative_live_run_ids=auth_ids,
+    )
+    res = service.run_topic_discovery(
+        document=document,
+        client=client,
+        model_name=model_id,
+        max_topics=max_topics,
+        temperature=temperature,
+        seed=seed,
+        preserve_raw_response=preserve_raw_response,
+    )
+    # Aggiorna lo stato scoped del documento per persistenza di sessione
+    try:
+        doc_analysis = state.get_document_analysis_state(document.document_id, state=session_state)
+        existing = list(doc_analysis.discovery_runs)
+        res_run_id = res.metadata.get("run_id") if isinstance(res.metadata, Mapping) else None
+        if not any(getattr(r, "metadata", {}).get("run_id") == res_run_id for r in existing):
+            existing.append(res)
+            doc_analysis.discovery_runs = tuple(existing)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return res
+
+
+def execute_discovery_verification(
+    document: ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    discovered_topic: DiscoveredTopic,
+    discovery_result: TopicDiscoveryResult | None = None,
+    discovery_run_id: str | None = None,
+    client: BaseLlmClient | None = None,
+    model_id: str | None = None,
+    base_url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:1234",
+    strategy: AnalysisLanguageStrategy = AnalysisLanguageStrategy.DIRECT_MULTILINGUAL,
+    temperature: float = DEFAULT_OPERATIONAL_TEMPERATURE,
+    seed: int | None = 42,
+    preserve_raw_response: bool = False,
+) -> tuple[TopicDetectionResult, Any]:
+    """
+    Esegue la "Prova del Nove":
+    Seconda inferenza indipendente di Topic Detection per verificare il topic emerso da Topic Discovery.
+    Restituisce (TopicDetectionResult, ConsistencyResult).
+    """
+    if discovery_result is None:
+        from ui import state
+        doc_analysis = state.get_document_analysis_state(document.document_id)
+        candidate_runs = list(doc_analysis.discovery_runs) + list(state.get_discovery_results())
+        for r in candidate_runs:
+            if getattr(r, "provenance_document_id", None) == document.document_id:
+                r_id = r.metadata.get("run_id") if isinstance(r.metadata, Mapping) else None
+                if discovery_run_id and str(r_id).strip() == str(discovery_run_id).strip():
+                    discovery_result = r
+                    break
+                elif not discovery_run_id:
+                    for t in r.topics:
+                        if (
+                            t.label.strip().casefold() == discovered_topic.label.strip().casefold()
+                            and t.short_description.strip() == discovered_topic.short_description.strip()
+                            and tuple(t.evidence_ids) == tuple(discovered_topic.evidence_ids)
+                        ):
+                            discovery_result = r
+                            discovery_run_id = str(r_id)
+                            break
+
+    service = get_operational_analysis_service(
+        base_url=base_url,
+        model_id=model_id,
+    )
+    return service.verify_discovered_topic(
+        document=document,
+        discovered_topic=discovered_topic,
+        discovery_result=discovery_result,
+        discovery_run_id=discovery_run_id,
+        client=client,
+        model_name=model_id,
+        strategy=strategy,
+        temperature=temperature,
+        seed=seed,
+        preserve_raw_response=preserve_raw_response,
+    )
+
+
+def execute_local_media_processing(
+    document: ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    registry: Any | None = None,
+    stt_backend: Any | None = None,
+    ocr_backend: Any | None = None,
+    vision_backend: Any | None = None,
+) -> tuple[ConversationEvidenceDocument, dict[str, Any]]:
+    """
+    Esegue l'elaborazione on-demand e rigorosamente locale dei media associati ai messaggi del documento:
+    - Audio: Faster-Whisper locale (se disponibile/installato)
+    - Immagini: Tesseract OCR locale (se disponibile/installato)
+    - Vision: esclusivamente modello reale configurato (NO Fake Vision su dati reali)
+    - Non modifica distruttivamente il documento originale: produce un nuovo ConversationEvidenceDocument arricchito
+    - Preserva evidence ID, message ID e provenance.
+    """
+    from multimodal.asset_access import MediaAssetAccessService
+    from multimodal.transcriber import FasterWhisperTranscriber
+    from multimodal.ocr import TesseractImageTextExtractor
+
+    status_report: dict[str, Any] = {
+        "stt_available": False,
+        "stt_engine": "none",
+        "ocr_available": False,
+        "ocr_engine": "none",
+        "vision_available": False,
+        "vision_engine": "none",
+        "initial_sections": len(document.all_evidence_sections),
+        "derived_sections": 0,
+        "processed_bundles": 0,
+    }
+
+    effective_stt = stt_backend
+    if effective_stt is None:
+        if FasterWhisperTranscriber.is_available():
+            try:
+                effective_stt = FasterWhisperTranscriber()
+                status_report["stt_available"] = True
+                status_report["stt_engine"] = "faster-whisper (locale)"
+            except Exception:
+                status_report["stt_available"] = False
+                status_report["stt_engine"] = "faster-whisper (inizializzazione fallita)"
+        else:
+            status_report["stt_available"] = False
+            status_report["stt_engine"] = "faster-whisper (non installato/non disponibile)"
+    else:
+        status_report["stt_available"] = True
+        status_report["stt_engine"] = getattr(effective_stt, "__class__", type(effective_stt)).__name__
+
+    effective_ocr = ocr_backend
+    if effective_ocr is None:
+        if TesseractImageTextExtractor.is_available():
+            try:
+                effective_ocr = TesseractImageTextExtractor()
+                status_report["ocr_available"] = True
+                status_report["ocr_engine"] = "tesseract (locale)"
+            except Exception:
+                status_report["ocr_available"] = False
+                status_report["ocr_engine"] = "tesseract (inizializzazione fallita)"
+        else:
+            status_report["ocr_available"] = False
+            status_report["ocr_engine"] = "tesseract (non installato/non disponibile nel PATH)"
+    else:
+        status_report["ocr_available"] = True
+        status_report["ocr_engine"] = getattr(effective_ocr, "__class__", type(effective_ocr)).__name__
+
+    effective_vision = vision_backend
+    if effective_vision is not None:
+        status_report["vision_available"] = True
+        status_report["vision_engine"] = getattr(effective_vision, "__class__", type(effective_vision)).__name__
+    else:
+        status_report["vision_available"] = False
+        status_report["vision_engine"] = "Nessun motore Vision locale configurato"
+
+    service = MediaAssetAccessService(
+        source_id=document.document_id,
+        registry=registry,
+    )
+
+    enriched_doc = service.process_document_media(
+        document=document,
+        stt_backend=effective_stt,
+        ocr_backend=effective_ocr,
+        vision_backend=effective_vision,
+    )
+
+    final_sections = len(enriched_doc.all_evidence_sections)
+    status_report["derived_sections"] = max(0, final_sections - status_report["initial_sections"])
+    status_report["processed_bundles"] = len(enriched_doc.bundles)
+
+    return enriched_doc, status_report
+
+

@@ -0,0 +1,321 @@
+"""
+ui/staging.py
+-------------
+Staging store locale e deterministico su SQLite per l'elaborazione bounded-memory
+dei file di grandi dimensioni durante la pipeline di ingestion forense.
+
+Principi architetturali:
+1. ZERO NETWORK & ZERO EXTERNAL DAEMONS:
+   Database SQLite temporaneo confinato nella sandbox locale della sessione.
+2. BOUNDED WORKING MEMORY:
+   I record normalizzati non vengono accumulati in liste complete in memoria,
+   ma scritti e letti a chunk controllati (batch commits / fetchmany).
+3. DETERMINISMO E INTEGRITÀ FORENSE:
+   Preservazione dell'ordine sequenziale originario (seq_order), delle chiavi
+   di sorgente e dell'isolamento per chat/conversazione.
+4. ISOLAMENTO MULTI-CHAT:
+   Consente l'iterazione e la costruzione dei ConversationEvidenceDocument chat-by-chat,
+   evitando dizionari monolitici contenenti tutti i messaggi dell'intero dataset.
+5. PULIZIA GARANTITA:
+   Gestito via context manager per chiudere e rilasciare i file descriptor.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import copyreg
+from pathlib import Path
+import pickle
+import sqlite3
+import types
+from typing import Any, Iterable, Iterator, Mapping, Sequence
+
+from normalization.models import NormalizedRecord, TimestampTzStatus
+
+# Registrazione serializzatore MappingProxyType per pickle
+def _reconstruct_mappingproxy(d: dict[str, Any]) -> types.MappingProxyType:
+    return types.MappingProxyType(d)
+
+copyreg.pickle(types.MappingProxyType, lambda mp: (_reconstruct_mappingproxy, (dict(mp),)))
+
+
+class IngestionStagingStore:
+    """
+    Store locale temporaneo SQLite per lo staging dei NormalizedRecord.
+    Garantisce bounded working memory durante l'ingestion e accesso indicizzato per chat.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        db_path: Path | str,
+        batch_size: int = 1000,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.db_path = Path(db_path)
+        self.batch_size = max(1, batch_size)
+        self._seq_order = 0
+        self._buffer: list[tuple[int, str, str, str, str, bytes]] = []
+
+        # Inizializza database SQLite locale
+        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(
+            str(self.db_path),
+            check_same_thread=False,
+            isolation_level=None,  # Gestione manuale delle transazioni
+        )
+        self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+        # Ottimizzazioni per prestazioni elevate su storage locale temporaneo
+        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = OFF;")
+        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = OFF;")
+        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA temp_store = MEMORY;")
+        self._conn.execute("PRAGMA cache_size = -16000;")  # ~16 MB RAM cache
+
+        self._create_tables()
+
+    def _create_tables(self) -> None:
+        with self._conn:
+            self._conn.execute("""
+                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS staged_records (
+                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+                    seq_order INTEGER NOT NULL,
+                    record_type TEXT NOT NULL,
+                    source_name TEXT NOT NULL,
+                    source_record_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+                    chat_key TEXT NOT NULL,
+                    text_content TEXT,
+                    timeline_kind TEXT,
+                    timeline_value TEXT,
+                    payload BLOB NOT NULL
+                );
+            """)
+            self._conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staged_seq ON staged_records(seq_order);")
+            self._conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staged_type ON staged_records(record_type);")
+            self._conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staged_chat_seq ON staged_records(chat_key, seq_order);")
+            self._conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staged_dup ON staged_records(record_type, timeline_kind, timeline_value, text_content);")
+
+    def stage_record(
+        self,
+        record: NormalizedRecord,
+        chat_key: str | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        """
+        Aggiunge un record al buffer di staging, svuotando su SQLite al raggiungimento del batch_size.
+        """
+        self._seq_order += 1
+        eff_chat_key = chat_key or self.extract_chat_key(record)
+        blob = pickle.dumps(record, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)
+
+        text_content = None
+        timeline_kind = None
+        timeline_value = None
+
+        if record.record_type == "message" and record.text_content:
+            clean_text = record.text_content.strip()
+            if clean_text and record.timestamp and record.timestamp.status != TimestampTzStatus.ABSENT:
+                text_content = clean_text
+                if record.timestamp.status == TimestampTzStatus.KNOWN_UTC and record.timestamp.utc_datetime:
+                    timeline_kind = "KNOWN_UTC"
+                    timeline_value = record.timestamp.utc_datetime.isoformat()
+                elif record.timestamp.status == TimestampTzStatus.NAIVE_UNKNOWN and record.timestamp.naive_datetime:
+                    timeline_kind = "NAIVE_UNKNOWN"
+                    timeline_value = record.timestamp.naive_datetime.isoformat()
+
+        self._buffer.append((
+            self._seq_order,
+            str(record.record_type or "unknown"),
+            str(record.source_name or "unknown"),
+            str(record.source_record_id or str(self._seq_order)),
+            eff_chat_key,
+            text_content,
+            timeline_kind,
+            timeline_value,
+            blob,
+        ))
+
+        if len(self._buffer) >= self.batch_size:
+            self.flush()
+
+    def stage_stream(
+        self,
+        records: Iterable[NormalizedRecord],
+    ) -> int:
+        """
+        Riceve uno stream di NormalizedRecord, lo inserisce a blocchi in SQLite e restituisce il conteggio totale.
+        """
+        count = 0
+        for rec in records:
+            self.stage_record(rec)
+            count += 1
+        self.flush()
+        return count
+
+    def flush(self) -> None:
+        """Esegue il commit immediato dei record nel buffer su SQLite."""
+        if not self._buffer:
+            return
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        cursor.execute("BEGIN TRANSACTION;")
+        cursor.executemany(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO staged_records (
+                seq_order, record_type, source_name, source_record_id, chat_key,
+                text_content, timeline_kind, timeline_value, payload
+            )
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);
+            """,
+            self._buffer,
+        )
+        self._conn.commit()
+        self._buffer.clear()
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def extract_chat_key(record: NormalizedRecord) -> str:
+        """
+        Estrae determinoristicamente la chiave di raggruppamento chat dal record normalizzato.
+        """
+        if record.chat_id is not None and str(record.chat_id).strip():
+            return str(record.chat_id).strip()
+        if record.actor_to is not None and record.actor_to.raw_value:
+            return f"peer:{record.actor_to.raw_value.strip()}"
+        return "UNRESOLVED"
+
+    def count(self, record_type: str | None = None) -> int:
+        """Restituisce il numero totale di record staged (o per record_type)."""
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        if record_type:
+            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM staged_records WHERE record_type = ?;", (record_type,))
+        else:
+            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM staged_records;")
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        return int(row[0]) if row else 0
+
+    def iter_all_records(self, chunk_size: int = 1000) -> Iterator[NormalizedRecord]:
+        """Itera su tutti i record staged in ordine sequenziale originario (seq_order)."""
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        cursor.execute("SELECT payload FROM staged_records ORDER BY seq_order ASC;")
+        while True:
+            rows = cursor.fetchmany(chunk_size)
+            if not rows:
+                break
+            for r in rows:
+                yield pickle.loads(r["payload"])
+
+    def iter_records_by_type(self, record_type: str, chunk_size: int = 1000) -> Iterator[NormalizedRecord]:
+        """Itera sui record dello specifico record_type in ordine seq_order."""
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT payload FROM staged_records WHERE record_type = ? ORDER BY seq_order ASC;",
+            (record_type,),
+        )
+        while True:
+            rows = cursor.fetchmany(chunk_size)
+            if not rows:
+                break
+            for r in rows:
+                yield pickle.loads(r["payload"])
+
+    def get_distinct_chat_keys(self) -> list[str]:
+        """
+        Restituisce l'elenco deterministico delle chiavi di chat per i record di tipo 'message'.
+        UNRESOLVED è sempre posizionato in coda se presente.
+        """
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        cursor.execute("SELECT DISTINCT chat_key FROM staged_records WHERE record_type = 'message';")
+        keys = [r[0] for r in cursor.fetchall() if r[0] is not None]
+
+        standard_keys = sorted([k for k in keys if k != "UNRESOLVED"])
+        ordered = list(standard_keys)
+        if "UNRESOLVED" in keys:
+            ordered.append("UNRESOLVED")
+        return ordered
+
+    def iter_records_for_chat(self, chat_key: str, chunk_size: int = 1000) -> Iterator[NormalizedRecord]:
+        """Itera sui soli record di tipo 'message' appartenenti alla specifica chat."""
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT payload FROM staged_records WHERE record_type = 'message' AND chat_key = ? ORDER BY seq_order ASC;",
+            (chat_key,),
+        )
+        while True:
+            rows = cursor.fetchmany(chunk_size)
+            if not rows:
+                break
+            for r in rows:
+                yield pickle.loads(r["payload"])
+
+    def extract_chat_titles(self) -> dict[str, str]:
+        """Estrae i titoli delle chat registrati nei record di tipo 'chat'."""
+        titles: dict[str, str] = {}
+        for rec in self.iter_records_by_type("chat"):
+            subj = None
+            if hasattr(rec, "raw_record") and hasattr(rec.raw_record, "raw_fields"):
+                raw_f = rec.raw_record.raw_fields
+                subj = raw_f.get("subject") or raw_f.get("title") or raw_f.get("chat_name")
+            cid = rec.chat_id or (rec.source_record_id if rec.source_record_id else None)
+            if cid and subj and str(subj).strip():
+                titles[str(cid).strip()] = str(subj).strip()
+        return titles
+
+    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[NormalizedRecord]:
+        """Consente l'iterazione trasparente a blocchi sui record staged come sequenza di NormalizedRecord."""
+        return self.iter_all_records()
+
+    def find_duplicate_candidate_records(self) -> dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, str]], list[NormalizedRecord]]:
+        """
+        Interroga lo staging SQLite per individuare esclusivamente i record duplicati candidati
+        aventi lo stesso testo e timestamp comparabile (count > 1).
+        Evita materializzazioni globali del dataset: carica solo i record che richiedono clustering.
+        """
+        self.flush()
+        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
+        # Query diretta e indicizzata sui soli record aventi timeline e testo identici (COUNT > 1)
+        cursor.execute("""
+            SELECT timeline_kind, timeline_value, text_content
+            FROM staged_records
+            WHERE record_type = 'message'
+              AND timeline_kind IS NOT NULL
+              AND timeline_value IS NOT NULL
+              AND text_content IS NOT NULL
+            GROUP BY timeline_kind, timeline_value, text_content
+            HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
+        """)
+        dup_groups = cursor.fetchall()
+        if not dup_groups:
+            return {}
+
+        result: dict[tuple[str, tuple[str, str]], list[NormalizedRecord]] = {}
+        for row in dup_groups:
+            tz_kind = row["timeline_kind"]
+            ts_val = row["timeline_value"]
+            text_val = row["text_content"]
+            rec_cur = self._conn.cursor()
+            rec_cur.execute("""
+                SELECT payload FROM staged_records
+                WHERE record_type = 'message'
+                  AND timeline_kind = ?
+                  AND timeline_value = ?
+                  AND text_content = ?
+                ORDER BY seq_order ASC;
+            """, (tz_kind, ts_val, text_val))
+            recs = [pickle.loads(r["payload"]) for r in rec_cur.fetchall()]
+            result[(text_val, (tz_kind, ts_val))] = recs
+        return result
+
+    def close(self) -> None:
+        """Chiude la connessione SQLite."""
+        try:
+            self.flush()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        try:
+            self._conn.close()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+    def __enter__(self) -> IngestionStagingStore:
+        return self
+
+    def __exit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc_val: Any, exc_tb: Any) -> None:
+        self.close()

+def get_media_asset_registry(
+    document_id: str | None = None,
+    state: MutableMapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> Any:
+    """Restituisce il MediaAssetRegistry isolato per il documento specificato o attivo."""

+    target_id = document_id or get_selected_document_id(state) or "default"
+    regs = target.setdefault(KEY_MEDIA_REGISTRIES_BY_DOCUMENT, {})
+    if target_id not in regs:
+        from multimodal.asset_access import MediaAssetRegistry
+        regs[target_id] = MediaAssetRegistry(document_id=target_id)
+    return regs[target_id]

+def update_conversation_document(
+    document: ConversationEvidenceDocument,
+    state: MutableMapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> None:
+    """
+    Aggiorna la versione corrente del documento nella sessione (es. dopo arricchimento con media derivati),
+    invalidando la cache dell'EvidenceIndex per forzare la reindicizzazione deterministica.
+    """
+    target = _get_target_state(state)
+    target[KEY_CONVERSATION_DOCUMENT] = document
+    available = target.setdefault(KEY_AVAILABLE_DOCUMENTS, {})
+    available[document.document_id] = document
+
+    active_analysis = get_active_analysis_state(state)
+    if active_analysis is not None:
+        active_analysis.cached_evidence_index = None

+def get_consistency_results(
+    document_id: str | None = None,
+    state: MutableMapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+) -> tuple[ConsistencyResult, ...]:
+    """Restituisce i risultati di consistency per il documento specificato o per quello attivo."""
+    target_id = document_id or get_selected_document_id(state)
+    if not target_id:
+        return ()
+    target = _get_target_state(state)
+    analysis_map = target.get(KEY_ANALYSIS_BY_DOCUMENT, {})
+    if target_id in analysis_map:
+        return analysis_map[target_id].consistency_results
+    return ()
